# CODEMAP

Quick index of the source. Paths are under `Assets/Scripts/` (`Core/` = plain C# logic, namespace `Arena`, no Unity;
`Game/` = Unity front end). `Game` (Core) is one big `partial class` split by feature; `ArenaApp` (Game/) is the
front-end `partial class`. Older, more detailed per-file notes (not maintained): `Docs/CodeNotes.md`. Player-facing reference: `README.md`.

## Engine and match flow (Core)
- `Game.cs`: match state, lifecycle, frame loop (`FixedStep`, `HostTick`), picks, local movement/dash
- `Entities.cs`: data types (`Player`, `Enemy`, `Bullet`, `Orb`, `Zone`, `Minion`, `Effect`, `PlayerInput`)
- `Waves.cs`: wave ticking, wave cleared, game over
- `TimedWaves.cs`: timed waves (`TimedWaves.Share` arrival curve with three surges and lulls (`Surges`, `Pulse`, `InSurge`), `BeginTimedWave`, `TickTimedSpawn`, alive cap; off in sim tests except suites `timed`, `portals`)
- `SpawnPortals.cs`, `Game/SpawnPortalLook.cs`: spawn portals (Docs/Spawns.md; the look: a "loading" gate, its ring fills while it opens): a timed wave's singles come in bursts through `EnemyType.SpawnPortal` (`TickPortalSpawn`, `OpenPortal`, `TickPortals`, `portalCargo`); shoot one closed to cancel the rest (`PortalSealed`, coins via `DropGold`); flanks behind the party (`FlankSpot`, red arrow `DrawFlankArrows`); `SpawnSpot` in `EnemyAI.cs`; preview `PORTALS=1 shot.sh x hud 8`; test suite `portals`
- `Recycling.cs`: enemy recycling: a regular enemy over `Recycling.Far` (1800 px) from every standing player for `Recycling.After` (4 s) moves to a fresh spawn spot (`RoomsRandomSpot`/`SurvivalSpot`) in the Expanding arena and Survival (`TickRecycling` after `TickEnemies`, `Enemy.FarT`, host only, no wire: clients snap the jump); test suite `recycle`
- `WaveReady.cs`: the between-waves Ready / Auto-ready system (`Msg.WaveReady`; its kind 2 carries what a client's screen shows, and the host works out each player's `BreakStatus` chip: `StatusOf`, `TickBreakStatus`) and the cleared wave's numbers and awards (`Msg.WaveSummary`); box drawn by `Game/WaveClearedUi.cs`
- `RemoteChests.cs`, `Game/ChestsTabUi.cs`: remote chests (Docs/RoundFlow.md step 5): `ChestSpot`, `UncollectedChests` (every unopened chest prop; vector chests are props now), `ChestCollectFee` (15 + 2 per wave), `CollectChest` / `Msg.CollectChest` / `HostCollectChest` (between waves only; `Props.cs OpenChest(remote)`: gold into wallets, cards at each player's feet); the wave box's Chests tab (rows, fee, your gold, the map); `DrawChestRewardTags` (the "+56 GOLD" / "+24 VECTORS" tag over Gold and Vector chests in the arena; preview `CHESTLOOKS=1 ZOOM=2 CAMAT=640,470`); preview `WCCHESTS=<n> WCSTEP=3`; test suite `remotechests` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/RemoteChests.cs`)
- `BossGate.cs`, `Game/BossGateLook.cs`: the boss gate: in the break before a boss a summoning circle opens mid-hub and the fight waits until every standing player is in it (`GateUp`/`GatePos`/`GateFill` in the snapshot, `TickBossGate` in place of the Ready countdown, `BotGate`, `GatesOn` off in sim tests but `bossgate`); the box's Gate tab and strip (`DrawGateBar`, `WaveClearedUi.cs`); tinted by the coming boss (`BossGateInk`), on the minimap (`DrawGateOnMap`) and the zoom arrows (`DrawGateMarker`); the open toast (`DrawGateToast`) and hum; preview `GATE=<fill> GATEIN=<n> [GATEBOSS=<EnemyType>] [ZOOM=2.6] shot.sh x hud 10 12.2 3`; test suite `bossgate`
- (`Rooms.cs` `ArenaKind` picks the arena: the Campaign is Expanding, `RunMod.SimpleArena` the open arena. The Rooms arena prototype (Wings) was removed Nov 18.)
- `Checkpoints.cs`: checkpoints and lives (Docs/Checkpoints.md): `LivesUsed`, `LivesMax`/`LivesLeft` (DiffKnob.Lives), `OfferRetry` (a wipe with a life left: respawn at the wave's start, `SaveStore.Read`, the checkpoint as fallback), `TakeRetry` (Session.RetryRun), `GiveUpRetry`/`SettleRetry` (Game over, or an untaken retry, ends the run), `Deathless`; the checkpoint file is SaveStore.WriteCheckpoint. Test suite `checkpoints`
- `Hunts.cs`: Boss Hunt mode (ten waves ending in a boss); the 100-wave Campaign's legs (`LongRun`, `Leg`, `ShownWave`, `ShownEnd`, `NextTrackWave`, `Pace`; test suite `longrun`); the boss between waves of a run (`BossSlot`, `BossNow`, `BossNext`, `IsBossWave`: the boss follows every fifth regular wave and keeps its number; HANDOFF §2)
- `HuntMap.cs`: the hunt map (Docs/HuntMap.md): `HuntMap.Generate(seed)` makes ten rows of 2-3 nodes on three lanes (non-crossing links, Skirmish/Elite/Boss so far), `Choices`/`Pick`/`Clear`/`Clues`/`Path`/`Restore` for progress, up to 5 boss clues each a health or damage clue (`ClueIsDamage`, from the seed), `Length` 10 or 20 and the Miniboss node at step 10 of 20 (Docs/LongHunt.md); played through `HuntMapGame.cs` (test suite `huntmap`); its step-10 checkpoint save and Continue are in `HuntMapGame.cs` + `RunSave.IsHunt` (suite `huntsave`)
- `Survival.cs`: Survival mode's spawner (Docs/Survival.md): `Pressure(t, players)` is the threat budget (6 + 0.08 t, 0.16 t after 10 min; 1.5x / 2x for 2 / 3 players), `Step(dt, liveThreat, liveCount)` returns the `SurvivalSpawn`s to add (singles topping the budget up, a squad every 45 s, a new faction every 180 s (all ten boss factions, each fading in over `IntroRamp`, spawn weight per faction: `FactionWeight`, `BasicWeight`, `IntroShare`), Elite share, cap of 60), `WaveAt` the scaling wave; test suite `survival`, balance report `run.sh 1 1 survbal [runs] [seed] [skill] [players] [cap min]`
- `SurvivalWorld.cs`: Survival's arena: `SurvivalRoom.World`, one 3200 x 1800 room (four times the classic arena) walled all round with a plus of walls in the middle (a gap at its centre) an L bracket in each corner and two pairs of wall portals (left/right, top/bottom), built as a `World` so the camera follow, walls and enemy steering of the walled worlds apply; `Game.SurvivalOn` makes `CurrentWorld` it and `ArenaKind` `Survival`
- `SurvivalGame.cs`: Survival played in a run (the room: `BeginSurvival` raises it, puts everyone near the middle, `SurvivalProps` fills it with crates and barrels and tops them up out of view, `SurvivalSpot` spawns enemies outside every player's view; its records: `SurvivalRecord` at the run's end, `Profile.SurvivalEnded`: plays, best time, best time per class, best phase, a Recent runs card, rank XP) (`Game.SurvivalOn`, `Surv`, `SurvTime`): the countdown ends in `BeginSurvival` instead of a wave, `TickSurvival` tops the field up from the spawner (squads at one spot), a pending level-up pick holds the run in a frozen Intermission (`SurvFrozen` stops `WorldRate`) until everyone has chosen, nothing clears or wins, everyone down ends it; start message flag + seed; not saved (suite `survival`)
- `HuntMapLayout.cs`: where the hunt map's right-angle lines run (`HuntMapLayout.Links`: ports along each node's edge, a height per link picked to cross the fewest others; tested in suite `huntmap`)
- `HuntMapGame.cs`: a Boss Hunt played as a map (the lobby's Hunt map switch, `Session.HuntMapMode`): the host rolls the map, everyone votes on the next node (`SendHuntVote`, `Msg.HuntMapVote`; a tie goes to the host, the 20 s timer picks), the node's wave is tougher for an Elite, a Rest heals and a Shop opens the Bazaar with no wave, an Ambush is a fast short wave paying XP and a chest, a Contraband is dark and pays a prize card, a Gamble is rolled into one of the fights (`HuntBosses.NodeAt` in `BossSchedule.cs`), clearing the wave clears the node; state goes out with `Msg.HuntMapState` (test suite `huntmap`)
- `BossSchedule.cs`: **which waves have a boss and which boss** (Docs/QuickPlay.md step 0): `BossSchedule` (`IsBossWave`, `BossAt`, `NextBossWaveFrom`, `LastBossWave`, `Slotted`, `ApproachStep`, `FinaleWave`), `CampaignBosses` (every fifth wave, `Game.BossCycle`), `HuntBosses`; `Game.Bosses` is the run's; `BossSchedule.Campaign` where the Campaign's order itself is meant (Boss Lab, the hunt list, the Campaign's faction mix); test suite `bossschedule`
- `GameMode.cs`: **the game modes in one place**: `GameMode` (Quick, Campaign, Hunt, Survival, then the hidden Hangout; the lobby carousel's order), `ModeDef` (one row per mode: profile key, name, `Lives`, `Saves`, `RunEvents`, `Challenges`, `Legs`, `Paths`, `BossBuildup`, `Extras`, `Records`, `Waves`), `Session.Mode`/`PlayMode` (the lobby's pick, one byte on the wire; a resumed run plays the Campaign), `Game.Mode`/`ModeRules` (the run's, worked out from Hunt/SurvivalOn/Quick). A rule that differs by mode reads `ModeRules` instead of chaining mode checks; a new mode adds an enum value and a row
- `QuickPlay.cs`: **Quick Play** (Docs/QuickPlay.md): `QuickPlay` (`Length` 10, `MakeOrder`: the boss bags, the Warden first on a first play; `HomeWave`), `QuickBosses` (its `BossSchedule`: a boss after every tenth wave), `Game.Quick`/`QuickOrder` (from `Msg.Start`), `QuickBossHp` (about a 2-minute fight at any wave); played on the Expanding arena (`Rooms.cs` `ArenaKind`); `Session.QuickMode`/`QuickDefault`, `Profile.QuickEnded`/`LastMode`, `RunSaves.QuickRecord`; the Game Mode screen's mode carousel (`LobbyUi.DrawModeSwitch`, `ModeScreenUi.cs`); test suite `quick` (`Tests/QuickPlay.cs`: Quick Play is recorded, a Boss Lab fight isn't); `BossLab.Quick` + report `quickfights` (bot fight lengths)
- `Tutorial.cs`, `Game/TutorialUi.cs`: **the first-run tutorial** (Docs/QuickPlay.md step 3): `Tutorial` (`Lessons`, `TutorialStep`, `Tick` from this machine's input and the game, `Note` for UI-only steps, `Skip`, `ShowAt` for previews), `Game.Tutorial`/`FinishTutorial` (`Profile.TutorialDone`); the prompt card at the top left (`DrawTutorial`, end of `Hud.DrawHud`); preview flag `TUTORIAL=`
- `Game/ModeTabKit.cs`: the Game Mode page's pieces (ModePageUi.cs): `ModeRing`, `ModeRow`/`ModeRowModel` (list rows), `ModeScope`/`ScopeGlow`/`ModeScopeModel`/`ModeScopeFinish` (the scope: the loadout preview's spotlight, in its colour), `ModeClock`
- `Game/CampaignUi.cs`: the lobby's **Campaign tab** (`CampaignTab` 10, `DrawCampaignTab`, Normal mode): its `CampaignPage` (ModePageUi.cs): the ten bosses as a list (named once reached or beaten), the next one in the scope, and your Campaign records; preview flag `CAMPAIGN=<bosses beaten>`
- `Game/ModePageUi.cs`: **the Game Mode page** of Quick Play, Campaign, Boss Hunt and Survival, drawn from one `ModePage` model (each mode's file builds it: `QuickPage`, `CampaignPage`, `HuntPage`, `SurvivalPage`): header band, the list (`ModeList`, `OnPick` for Boss Hunt's click), scope + dossier in the middle, a sidebar of YOUR RECORDS and RULES
- `Training.cs`, `Game/TrainingUi.cs`: **Training** (Docs/Training.md): `GameMode.Training` (a lobby mode; START never starts a run), `Training.Drills`/`Groups`/`Medal`/`BuiltCount`, `TrainingDrill` (`Key`, `Keys`, `Gold`/`Silver`, `Built`), `Profile.DrillDone`/`DrillBestTime` (`drill` lines); the Game Mode screen's Training page (`TrainingTab` 13: drill cards by group, a schematic scope, the brief; `DrawTrainingStartKey`: START DRILL in place of TO THE LOBBY, ModeScreenUi.cs); preview `TRAINING=1|2 shot.sh x lobby`; test suite `training` (`Tests/Training.cs`)
- `Game/RecruitLook.cs`: a Training recruit's model (no class or role look: grey fatigues, a helmet with a cyan visor, the Training Pistol in hand), drawn in place of the class model (CharacterModels.cs); the neutral footprint ring (`RecruitRing`, WorldPlayers.cs); preview `DRILL=1,6.2 ZOOM=7 shot.sh x hud 1 1 2`
- `Game/TankSoldierMock.cs`, `Tools/Preview/ShotTankLook.cs`: MOCKUP (preview only): three Tank Soldier model concepts (`tankLookMock`: 0 Bulwark, 1 Exo Frame, 2 Breacher; drawn in place of the Soldier's back, torso and head, CharacterModels.cs); preview `TANKSHEET=1 [TANKGUN=n] shot.sh x hud 2 1 1`
- `DrillGame.cs`, `Game/DrillUi.cs`: **a Training drill running** (Docs/Training.md): `Game.Drill`/`DrillOn`/`DrillStart` (the index on `Msg.Start`), `DrillRunner` per player (step, clock, done, target), `TickDrill` (no waves, a fight with no enemies, nobody hurt), `TickMoveDrill` (Move and Aim: `Training.MoveBeacon`/`MoveShootStep`), `TickDodgeDrill` (Dodge: each runner's wall of shots, `Training.WallSpeedAt`/`WallDir`; `DrillDodged` from Dodge.cs), `TickKickDrill`/`DrillKicked`/`DrillNoteKick` (Wall Kick: `Training.KickLayout`/`KickPanel`/`KickFlag`, the panels as the drill's floor plan via `ActiveLayout`; `Msg.DrillKick`), `TickShootDrill` (Shoot: one sliding target at a time, `Training.ShootSpot`/`ShootAt`/`ShootSpeedAt`), `TickParryDrill`/`MoveDrillShot` (Parry: a homing shot per runner, `Training.ParryStart`/`ParrySpeedAt`; blocked by a raised shield facing it), `TickTellsDrill` (Read the Tells: one wind-up at a time aimed where you stand, `Training.TellKind`/`TellStart`/`TellCatches`/`TellWindup`; `SyncTell`), `MakeRecruit` (everyone in a drill a plain recruit with the Training Pistol: `Player.Recruit`, `WeaponId.TrainingPistol`, `Training.Pistol`, kept out of the lobby registry, `Defs.WeaponOf`), `UpdateDrillView` (a client's clocks, walls, shots and own finish), `DrillTargetOf`/`DrillShieldsHit` (a shoot step's dummy: only its player hurts or sees it), `SyncDrill` (snapshot), `Session.StartDrill` (START DRILL, RETRY); the look: your beacons, the drill card, the result card (RETRY R, BACK B), `DrawDrillWall` (the wall, the red flash), `DrawDrillKick` (the lit panel, the flag), `DrawDrillTargets` (the target's rail and ring), `DrawDrillShot` (the shot, the warning arc round you), `DrawDrillTells`/`DrawTellShape` (the wind-up's line, lane or circle, its flash), the HUD corner's BEACONS / WALLS LEFT; preview `DRILL=<done>,<s>[,done[,hits]] [DRILLID=1 [DRILLHIT=1] | DRILLID=2 | DRILLID=3 | DRILLID=4 [PARRYOFF=1] | DRILLID=5 [TELLT=<s>] [TELLIN=1]] shot.sh x hud 1 1 2`
- `Game/QuickPlayUi.cs`: the lobby's **Quick Play tab** (`QuickTab` 8, `DrawQuickPlayTab`): its `QuickPage` (ModePageUi.cs): the boss pool as a list (??? until beaten), a BOSS DRAW scope, records and rules; preview flag `QUICKMET=`
- `Contraband.cs`: Contraband (the cycle chest on waves 6, 11, 16... when anyone has it; its holder's chest items are rare or better, in `Props.cs`)
- `RiotGearLook.cs` (Game): Riot Gear's Plate look: the plate that swings toward incoming shots, the recharge ghost, the block effect (`FxKind.RiotBlock`) and the HUD chip above the XP strip
- `RiotGear.cs`: Riot Gear's shield (`RiotBlocks`, called first in `Damage`; `TickRiot`; `PerksFor` picks who brings what at match start, `GivePerks` applies it on every screen); burns, gas, acid and lava ticks pass `overTime` and go through
- `Wiki.cs`: the in-game wiki's entries (`Wiki.All`, `WikiEntry`, `WikiKind`), built from the game's own tables (classes, roles, weapons, abilities, cards, perks, enemies, bosses): data links (`Links`), text mentions (`Mentions`/`MentionedBy`), `Search`, `References`; suite `wiki` (`Docs/Wiki.md`)
- `WikiTopics.cs`: the wiki's hand-written entries: mechanics (`All`: Alternative fire, Dodge, Parry, Wound, Elite, Mastery, Vectors...), status effects (`Statuses`: Burning, Frozen, Bleeding...; `PlayerStatuses`: Swallowed, Grabbed, Taunting) and props and chests (`Things`); add a line when one gets a name players see
- `WikiPhases.cs`: the wiki's boss phases (`WikiPhases.All`, by boss, with their health range, in the README's words; the shared `Last stand`); `Wiki.PhaseIn` finds the one a boss bar's text names
- `WikiAttacks.cs`: the wiki's boss attacks (`WikiAttacks.All`: boss, phase, name and text, in the README's words; about 70), linked to their phase and boss; `Wiki.PhaseIn` finds them on the boss bars too
- `Perks.cs`: perks (`PerkId`, `Perks.All`): unlock rules, slots, equip/unequip (one per boss), cleaning the profile's `perks` line; the effects come next (`Docs/RiotGear.md`)
- `GatekeeperRoom.cs`: the Expanding arena's mini-boss room (`RoomKind.Gatekeeper`; walk in to wake its faction's mini-boss, `ExpansionRoom.Mini`, the room locks, prize pick, becomes a combat room; an unbeaten duel ends with the wave)
- `FactionMinis.cs`: faction mini-bosses (Docs/FactionMinis.md): `FactionMinis.For(boss)`, `Game.MiniBossNow()` (the next boss's faction's), used by the mini-boss room; the Gatekeeper is the Data Gates' only
- `HoldingCell.cs` (Core), `Game/HoldingCellLook.cs`: the Warden's net and Holding Cells (Docs/Mockups/warden-net-cell.html): the searchlight's lock-on throws a net (`WardenState.NetId`/`NetT`/`NetFrom`, `WardenLight.Net`, Warden.cs), `JailPlayer` moves them into one of three cells far off the arena (`CellCentre`, `CellSlotAt`/`InCellZone`: fixed places; `ClampToArena`/`KeepInArena` hold anyone in one inside its bars), `TickHoldingCells` (30 s `CellTime`, Riot Guards through a gap in each wall, the door, downed inside or the Warden gone: `FreeFromCell`), `CellSkillUsed` (an ability: -1 s), `CellLockHit`/`CellLockStrike` (the padlock in the arena: -0.25 s a hit), `ArenaHeld` (nobody left fighting: enemies and shots outside the cells hold still), `Jailed` (targeting skips them), `CellBot`, `SyncHoldingCells` (snapshot); the look: the cell block, padlocks, the net, `CellViewFocus` (the camera, CameraZoom.cs), the HOLDING CELL panel and fades; preview `HOLDCELL=<s> [HOLDCELLS=2] | HOLDLOCK=1 | HOLDNET=<s> shot.sh x hud 10 1 2` (`Tools/Preview/ShotHoldingCell.cs`); test suite `holdingcell` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/HoldingCell.cs`)
- `RiotCaptain.cs` (Core), `Game/RiotCaptainLook.cs`: the Warden's faction mini-boss: tower shield (`CaptainShieldBlocks`, never breaks), shield charge (a wall dazes it, `CapDazed`), buckshot, tear gas, whistle (Taser Guards, `ClearCaptainAdds`); state in `Enemy.Phase`; boss bar note `CaptainNote`; test suite `captain`; preview `CAPTAIN=0|1 ZOOM=1.6 shot.sh x hud`
- `Challenges.cs`: challenge rooms (portal, crystal, gatekeeper, kill rush, altar)
- `Paths.cs`: choose-your-path offers, path enemy mixes, hazards, arena rules
- `Props.cs`: crates, pots, barrels, treasure chests (**every chest is made by `SpawnChest(pos, kind, onOpened, holdsWave, seed)`**, Docs/Chests.md: the hook runs once as it opens, `TrySpawnChest` for a free spot, `ChestAt`; `OpenChest` pays every `ChestKind` incl. Vector; a chest's item per player: `Loot`, `LootKind` Upgrade / Build / Card, `TakeLoot`, `LootName`, `LootInk`: Buildables and piece chests drop a build or a piece to take or discard); pushing crates and barrels (`ShoveProps`: Tank 70, Dps 50, Healer 34, enemy 3 px/s; `PropDir`: enemies path round them first)
- `PropGrid.cs`: the props in 128 px cells (`PropsNear`, `PropOrder`; `Prop.Pos` crossing a cell bumps `PropStamp` to rebuild), so `PushOutOfProps`, `ShoveProps`, `PropDir` and `PropStops` check only nearby props, in Props' order (same results as a full pass)
- `ExpansionGame.cs`, `Game/ExpansionUi.cs`: the Expanding arena in a run (per-player vectors: `Player.Vectors`, `AddVectors`, `GrantVectorsForWave`, `Expansion.Cost`): `SpendableVectors` (own vectors), the rooms bought (`Expanded`), build rounds (`OpenBuildRound`, `ResolveBuildRound`, `Links`/`ProposeExpandLink`/`Msg.ExpandLink` (portal pairs), everything built at once (`FreeBuild`, `BuildNow`/`ApplyBuilt`), `Msg.ExpandState`/`ExpandApply`, `RoomTokens`, `GrantRoomToken`, `ApplyRoomToken`), `ExpansionScale` (more enemies on a bigger map), `CurrentWorld` picks Single/Wings/the built map; the build chip; the build screen (`DrawExpansionDraw`): tool rail `DrawBuildRail` (`BuildTool`: pieces, teleporter, gate, trap; keys 1-4, map legend), guide panel `DrawBuildPanelTop` (wallet bar, live line; tool and legend help are hover tips via `BuildToolHelp` + `DrawTip(true)`)
- `SurpriseRooms.cs`: surprise rooms (Docs/SurpriseRooms.md step 1): `SurpriseShape` (Ring, Cross, LHall, THall, Chamfer) as tile masks (`Expansion.SurpriseBase`/`SurpriseMask`), placed on the lattice (`GridSurpriseOptions`, `SurpriseFor`: a seeded pick among the best 3, PieceShapes.cs); `GiveSurpriseRoom`/`SurpriseCount`/`NoteSurprise` live in `ExpansionGame.cs` (after a boss, `Waves.cs`); `ExpansionRoom.Surprise` is on the wire; tests `ExpandingSurpriseTest`, `ExpandingSurpriseGameTest` (suite `expand`)
- `GridRooms.cs`, `Game/GridRoomsUi.cs`: grid rooms (Docs/GridRooms.md; the only way the Expanding arena builds since Nov 18): `Expansion.GridCore`/`GridCoreCuts`/`TickGridCore` (the free 4 x 3 cell core a Grid run starts with); the core's look: no tint or door dashes of its own (`CoreArea`/`CoreSeam`, `RoomsLook.cs`); preview `EXPANDMAP=0 GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDAT=1500,450 ZOOM=0.7 shot.sh x hud`, `GridNodeFor` (nodes at a one-cell door spot's middle, far enough out for their tier: `RingTier`/`CycleTier`), `GridDoorAlong`/`DropSecondGridDoors` (a door on a lattice wall sits in one cell's middle, one door a cell; used by `Expansion.Build`); UI (`GridRoomsUi.cs`, Docs/GridPieces.md step 3): the build screen's Pieces tool: the hand, the picked piece's ghost on the door spot under the mouse, R through the fits there, door-spot marks; preview `EXPANDVOTE=0 EXPANDDRAW=0,0,0,0 GRID=1 [GRIDCARDS=mono,tett:burninggrounds,shop] [GRIDSEL=n] [MOUSE=x,y] shot.sh x hud`; suite `gridrooms` (`Tests/GridRooms.cs`); the map's lattice fading out from the map (`DrawGridLattice`) and the placing effect (`DrawPlaceFx`: slide in, slam, flash, gold wall, sparks), the core drawn as one room (`CoreArea`/`CoreSeam` in `RoomsLook.cs`, the build map's door bars); the tool rail hides Teleporter and Trap until you own one (`DrawBuildRail`); mystery rooms on the lattice (`Mystery.PiecesOf`, `Expansion.GridMysteryOptions`)
- `PieceShapes.cs`: grid pieces (Docs/GridPieces.md step 1): `PieceShape` (the 1 to 4 cell polyominoes), `Expansion.PieceShapeOrients` (28 distinct turns and mirrors), `PieceShapeRooms` (the open room a piece builds on the lattice, free), `GridSpots`/`PlacementsFor` (a cell of the piece on a door spot), `Msg.PieceShape`; surprise and breach rooms on the lattice in a Grid run (`PieceShapeOfSurprise`, `GridSurpriseOptions`, `SurpriseOptionsFor`/`SurpriseFor`, used by the boss gift, breach Stashes, the breach event and the Engineer's Breach); suite `gridrooms` (`Tests/PieceShapes.cs`)
- `Hand.cs`, `Game/CardsUi.cs`: the hand of cards (the starting pick as the run starts, before the opening GET READY: `DealStartPicks` (from the host's start, Game.cs; `DealRoundCards` covers a seat it missed), the countdown held then `Game.OpeningCountdown` (`openingBreak`/`openingHeld`, Waves.cs), the launch paused at `LaunchPickAt` with the picker held back until then (`LaunchHoldsPicker`, LaunchIntro.cs), test `StartPickOpeningTest`; `StartCardOffer`/`Expansion.GridStartOffer`, `Player.StartPick`/`OfferStart` (also `OfferWave`), the picker's roll-and-lock look `DrawStartRolling` (BossCardsUi.cs) timed by `IntroRevealed`/`StartRollTick` (PickIntro.cs), preview `STARTCARDS=<seed> INTRO=<s> shot.sh x bosscards`; the wave card pick each build round: `WaveCardOffer`, `Player.WavePicks`/`OfferWave`, picked like the boss's in `HostPick`, no reroll, preview `bosscards` + `WAVECARDS=1`; `PieceCard` codes: grid pieces from 32 up, the service cards Shop/Market/Arms/Medic and the Builder Shop; Box to Chamfer and MiniBoss retired with Room cards, Nov 18), `Player.Hand` (6, in the player snapshot), the sources (`DealRoundCards`, `DropWaveCards`, `ChestCards`; the Black Market sells none since Nov 18), `CardName`/`CardBlurb`/`CardPrice`/`CardRoomKind`, `BotCardGoal`; UI: `CardColor`, `DrawCardGlyph` (a piece's cells, a service card's cell and mark)
- `BuilderShop.cs`, `Game/BuilderShopUi.cs`: the Builder Shop (a one-cell service card in a Grid run, Docs/GridPieces.md): `PieceCard.BuilderShop` builds a 5 x 4 `RoomKind.Builder` room; standing in it in a build round shows its shelf (`BuilderOffers`: 5 cards a wave, `BuilderPrice` a quarter off, `BuilderRefusal`), bought with `BuyBuilderCard`/`Msg.BuilderBuy`/`HostBuilderBuy`; UI: the bottom shelf panel `DrawBuilderShop` (`MouseOnBuilderShop` blocks firing), the workbench `DrawBuilderCounter`; preview `EXPANDMAP=0 CARDROOM=buildershop BUILDEROPEN=1 [BUILDERVEC=n]`; suites `gridrooms` (`GridBuilderOffers`) and `armsdealer` (the boss pick)
- `GridPieces.cs`: grid pieces (Docs/GridPieces.md step 2): hand codes from `PieceCodeBase` 32 up (`Expansion.MakePieceCard`/`MakePieceCardMods`/`ShapeOfPieceCard`/`PieceCardMods`/`PieceCardFloor`/`PieceCardCombat`: code = base + shape + 16 * mods, the modifiers themselves in `PieceMods.cs`; `IsGridPieceCard`, `PieceCardPrice`), `GridStartHand`/`GridRoundCard`/`GridDropCard`/`GridBuilderOffers` (every offered shape card rolls `PieceMods.Roll`; the sources in a Grid run), `Game.HandOn` (cards in play), `PlacementsForCard`/`IsStallCard`/`CoversPrizeNode` (service cards and the Builder Shop stay off chest, vector chest and relic nodes), `PlayPiece`/`HostPlayPiece` (`Msg.PieceShape`: slot, orientation, x, y; free, the card leaves the hand), `BotPlayPiece`; mini-boss rooms waiting on the lattice (`GridMiniFor`, `GridMinisWaiting`, `PlaceGridMini` from `PlaceFloatingRooms`; preview `GRIDMINI=1`); tests `PieceShapesTest`, `PieceCardsTest`, `GridMiniTest`, `PieceShapesGameTest` (suite `gridrooms`, `Tests/PieceShapes.cs`)
- `BreachEvent.cs`: the breach event (Docs/SurpriseRooms.md step 3): `BreachEvent.IsEventWave` (half the breach waves from wave 7, seeded), `Game.EventPlan` (host: a breach with a surprise room that fits behind its wall), `EventMark` (the gold breach, in `Msg.ExpandState`), `TickBreachEvent`/`RevealBreachEvent` (20 s in or at the wave's end), `PinBreaches` in `Breaches.cs` (the other breaches stay); `RevealBreachRooms` (a cleared breach wave: a free Stash behind each breach left, its door lined up with the breach by `CentreOnBreach`, Docs/BreachRooms.md); look: `DrawBreach(..., gold)` in `Game/BreachLook.cs`; test `ExpandingBreachEventTest`
- `Game/SurpriseLook.cs`: the surprise rooms' look: the gold build-in and rim (`DrawSurpriseRoom`, from `RoomsLook.cs`) and the SURPRISE ROOM chip (`DrawSurpriseBanner`); preview `EXPANDMAP=3 EXPANDSURPRISE=ring`
- `Expansion.cs`: the Expanding arena's rooms: `RoomKind`, `RoomToken` + `FloorToken`/`CanRetype`/`Retype` (room kits, Docs/RoomModifiers.md), `ExpansionRoom` (a built box + its parent; a piece's other boxes `Leg >= PieceBase`, `GroupOf`/`HeadOf`/`Joined`; `World.Rim`/`World.Seam` draw a group as one room), `FromTiles` (tiles cut into boxes joined open: what pieces and surprise rooms build), `BoughtCount`/`ConnectedAreas`/`Grown`, `AddCover`/`AddFins` (a built room's obstacles and side walls, `FinStyle`; preview `FINSTYLE=<n>`), `Build` (hub + rooms -> a `World`); `DetRandom` (platform-independent RNG); portal links (`PortalLink`, `LinkPrice` by `WalkDistance` saved, `BestLink`, built as `World.Portals`; wall portals: `OuterWall`/`WallSpot`/`LinkHalf`, `LinkWalk`; the teleporter tool snaps to walls only: `NearestWallSpot`). Preview `EXPANDMAP=<n>` (the core and n pieces) `EXPANDROOMS=x,y,w,h;...` (plain boxes) `EXPANDAT=x,y`. Test suite `expand`; map nodes (Docs/Nodes.md): `MapNode`/`NodeKind`, `NodeClaimed` (placed by `GridNodeFor`, GridRooms.cs); chests open in `TickNodes` (`ExpansionGame.cs`); `Build(..., nodes)` gives the room round a shop or Black Market node its job; node spots: a claimed chest, vector chest or relic stands in the middle of its grid cell (else of its room) (`CentresInRoom`, `NodeSpotIn`, `World.NodeSpots`, `Game.NodeSpot(i)`), test `ExpandingNodeCentreTest`; node tiers: `NodeTier`, `Tiered`, `NodeChestKind` by tier; look `DrawNodeTier` (`NodeLook.cs`); bots pick prize nodes by need (`BotNodeWant`/`BotNodeScore`); suite `chests`. (Drawn rooms, outlines, offers, costs and floating-room connectors were removed Nov 18.)
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/PickHeld.cs`: level-up cards held between waves while an awake enemy is near (`Game.PickHeld`, `PickHoldReach`; the plate `HudPicker.DrawPickHeld`; preview `PICKHELD=1 shot.sh x hud`) (suite `pickheld`)
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/Pickups.cs`: per-player pickups (Docs/RoundFlow.md step 3): only a pickup's owner takes it (walking, Magnet), owners reach the client, a carried Magnet is anyone's; Hemomancer blood orbs (only their kills drop them, shared, drift to a hurt player) (suite `pickups`)
- `Breaches.cs`, `Game/BreachLook.cs`: the Expanding arena's breaches (Docs/RoomBuilding2.md step 1): `Breaches.Candidates`/`For` (the wave's outer-wall spots, one at the middle of each lattice cell's side, seeded from the map and wave, no wire), `Game.ActiveBreaches`, `BreachSpot` (where `SpawnEnemy`/`SpawnSquadAt` bring enemies in), `BreachAge` (the 1.5 s telegraph); the look: a red holographic barrier in the cracked wall, floor chevrons, a minimap dash (`RoomsLook.cs`); preview `EXPANDMAP=6 BREACHAGE=5 BREACHAT=1`; test `ExpandingBreachesTest` (suite `expand`)
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/ChestNodes.cs`: chest nodes (a chest node spawns a real chest through `SpawnChest`, a Vector one bursts into vector pickups; a claimed node's fixture stands in the middle of its cell); runs in suite `expand`
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/ExpandingStayIn.cs`: enemies stay inside the rooms (`KeepEnemiesInRooms` in `EnemyAI.cs`: one that ends a tick outside every room, from crowding, a shove or a blink, goes back to `Enemy.RoomAt`); suite `stayin`, also in `expand`
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/NetAuditFixes.cs`: the late Nov 2026 audit's network fixes (more than 255 zones in a snapshot, `BulletSync` keeping a shot's `Look` and `Radius`, a leaver removed on other clients by `RemoveUnseen(Players)`); suite `netaudit`
- `Game/BreachTour.cs`: the breach warning at a breach wave's start (`Breaches.WarnTime`/`Lead`/`Pan`/`Hold`/`Back`, `Game.BreachWarnOn`: no spawns, inputs held still): `BreachTourFocus` (the camera flies to each breach and back, from `CameraZoom.cs`), `DrawTourFlash` (the strobe and red rings), `DrawBreachWarning` (letterbox and the red BREACH n/N scan band); the flood: `FloodBreachWave` (`Breaches.Flood` = 3x enemies, `AliveScale`); preview `EXPANDMAP=6 BREACHAGE=1.0 BREACHTOUR=1 shot.sh x hud 7`; checks in `ExpandingBreachesTest`
- `MapReset.cs`: map reset (Docs/MapReset.md step 1): the Campaign's Expanding map goes back to the bare hub after each boss (`Game.ResetMap` from `WaveCleared`, `MapResetOn` = `ModeDef.MapResets`), kits kept, `RefundFor(Player.MapSpent)` (counted in `PayVectors`) back on top of the boss refill, stray coins to the nearest player; `MapNumber` (in `Msg.ExpandState`, saved with `MapSpent`), `MapResetAge` for the look; the keepsake vote (step 2: `OpenKeepsake`, `CastKeepsakeVote`/`Msg.KeepsakeVote`, `KeepsakeWinner`, `TickKeepsake` holds the break, `KeptHead`, saved as `KeepsakePending`) and `Expansion.RoomGroup`/`KeepsakeChoices`/`RoomTiles`/`BiggestRoom`/`PlaceKeepsake` (the fresh map's Grid core first, then the kept room moved whole by cells onto a door spot of it); test suite `mapreset`
- `Game/MapResetLook.cs`: the map reset's look (Docs/MapReset.md step 3): `DrawMapFold` (the old map's boxes, `Game.FoldRooms`, fold into the hub), `DrawKeptRoom` (gold build-in and rim, from `RoomsLook.cs`), `DrawMapResetBanner` (MAP n/10, your vectors back from `Game.MapRefunds`); preview `MAPRESET=<s> ZOOM=0.42 EXPANDMAP=0 EXPANDROOMS=... shot.sh x hud`
- `Game/KeepsakeUi.cs`: the keepsake vote window (KEEP ONE ROOM: a card per room with its outline at one shared scale, size, kind, voters' pips, LEADING tag, the clock); preview `KEEPSAKE=1 EXPANDMAP=0 EXPANDROOMS=... shot.sh x hud`
- `MysteryDoors.cs`, `Game/MysteryDoorLook.cs`: mystery doors (Docs/MysteryDoors.md): `MysteryDoor` (spot, price, `Saver`), `Mystery` rules (`SmallPack` 25 of `MysteryPackType`, the faction's basic enemy; `PriceForCells`: 4 + 5 a cell, `Cap` 3, `NewDoors` 1-2 seeded), `Game.MysteryDoors` (synced in `Msg.ExpandState`), host-only plans (`mysteryPlans`: a surprise shape behind each door, `MysteryDoorOf` finds its door spot), `RollMysteryDoors` (a cleared wave, `Waves.cs`: unsaved doors expire, new ones roll), `TickMysteryDoors` (re-plan or drop a door when the map changes), `UseMysteryDoor`/`Msg.MysteryDoor`/`HostMysteryDoor` (G opens, B saves, one save per player, `ArenaApp.cs`), `BuyMysteryDoor`/`Msg.MysteryBuy`/`HostMysteryBuy` (a click on the build map opens, a right click saves, from anywhere, build round only; both share `SaveOrOpenMysteryDoor`; hover and tip in `ExpansionUi.cs`), `OpenMysteryDoor` (pays, builds, mid-wave once rooms are up); what's behind (owner's rooms rework): `MysteryRoom` (small, medium, hallway) and `MysteryKind` (enemies, empty, shop, Black Market, chest; host only), `Mystery.RoomFor`/`KindFor`/`ShapesOf` (`SurpriseShape.Tiny`/`SmallRect`/`Medium`/`Box`/`LongHall`), `HallFits` (door on a short end), `FarEnd` (the hallway's chest node), enemies (`SpringAmbush`, `SpringPendingAmbushes`); preview `MYSTERYHALL=1`; bots (step 4): `BotMysteryDoor` (from `Game.cs`), `Mystery.BotReserve`, counters `MysteryDoorsOpened`/`MysteryVectorsSpent`; the look (step 3): the Hologram door `DrawMysteryDoor`, the reveal `DrawMysteryReveal` (`Game.MysteryOpened`, `NoteOpenedDoors`), `DrawMysteryHint` (price tags, keys), `DrawMysteryOnMap` (minimap, `RoomsLook.cs`), `DrawHoloMystery` (build map, `ExpansionUi.cs`); preview `EXPANDMAP=6 MYSTERY=1 [MYSTERYAT=1] [MYSTERYSAVED=1] [MYSTERYOPEN=<s>] ZOOM=2.2 shot.sh x hud 8 3`, build map `EXPANDVOTE=4 MYSTERY=1 EXPANDDRAW=0,0,0,0`; suite `mysterydoor` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/MysteryDoors.cs`)
- `RoomFog.cs`, `Game/RoomFogLook.cs`: room fog (Docs/FogOfWar.md): `ExpansionRoom.Unseen` (rooms after the core start unseen, `FogNewRooms` before every room list goes out), the line-of-sight cone (`RoomFog.Sees`/`Blocked`: 90 degrees along the aim + a close circle, walls stop it), the explored cell grid (`ScanFog` from `TickRoomFog`, `FogExplored`/`FogRoomShare`, synced by `Msg.FogCells` and the end of `ExpandState`, saved as `fog` lines; a room 90% explored is seen), `FogHides`/`FogSees` (enemies, coins and orbs in an unseen room show only under a cone), `NearestUnseen` (bots walk to the nearest unexplored cell, measured through its room's door, BotBrain.cs); the look: per-cell veil (`DrawFogCells`: dark hatched, dim explored, clear in the cone), the gold lift, dark rooms with a "?" on the minimap and build map; suite `roomfog` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/RoomFog.cs`); preview `FOG=1 EXPANDMAP=4 FOGROOM=4 FOGAIM=0,trail`
- `PieceMods.cs`: piece card modifiers (Docs/PieceMods.md): `PieceMod` (a modifier: its bits in the card code, `Chance`, `RollValue`, `Name`/`Blurb`/`Price`, `ApplyRoom`, `OnPlaced`), `FloorMod`, `CombatMod`, `LootMod` (a guaranteed chest kind, `Game.PlaceRoomChest` in `RoomChests.cs`), `OneWayMod` (`OneWayDoors.cs`), the list `PieceMods.All` (add or remove one there), `Roll`, `Clean`, `ApplyRoom`, `OnPlaced`; test `PieceCardsTest`
- `OneWayDoors.cs`, `Game/OneWayDoorLook.cs`, `Game/OneWayDoorSoundBank.cs`: One-way pieces (`PieceMods.OneWay`, Docs/PieceMods.md): `Expansion.MarkOneWayDoors` (from `Build`: a One-way piece's first door both ways, the rest `RoomDoor.Pass` in or out by `OneWayInward`), `World.OneWays`/`OneWayHold` (from `PushOutOfSolids`)/`OneWayStop` (`Game.OneWayStep`: player and enemy steps)/`OneWayBlocks` (steering's routes and corner graph, `LayoutRules.cs`); the look (amber jambs, flowing chevrons, `DrawOneWayDoor` from `DrawRoomsFloor`; on the build map an arrow each, `DrawHoloOneWays`, and a ONE-WAY DOOR key; a One-way card's ghost shows the arrows its doors would get, `DrawGhostOneWays` from `DrawGridGhost`); the bump (`Game.OneWayPushes` from `LocalControl`, `TickOneWayBump`: `Sfx.OneWayBump` and an amber ring, local only; `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh OneWay`); preview `GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDMAP=0 ONEWAY=tetl ZOOM=1.5 shot.sh x hud`, build map `EXPANDVOTE=0 GRID=1 EXPANDDRAW=0,0,0,0 ONEWAY=tetl shot.sh x hud`, ghost `... GRIDCARDS=tett:oneway GRIDSEL=0 MOUSE=700,420`; suite `oneway`
- `DormantGroups.cs`: dormant groups (Docs/FogOfWar.md): a built Combat piece card's cells each roll `DormantGroups.CellChance` for a sleeping group of the wave's faction enemies (`DormantPool`, `GroupSize`, `PlantDormantGroups` from `CombatMod.OnPlaced`, at least one group); `Enemy.Dormant` (no AI, not in `Foes`, ignored by bots and recycling) until `WakeDormant` (the room seen in `TickDormant`, or a hit in `HurtEnemy`); `ClearDormant` at a map reset; the z's: `DrawDormantMarks` (RoomFogLook.cs)
- `CellLayouts.cs`: the 18 hand-made layouts for a grid cell (`CellLayout`: walls, sleeper, trap and loot slots; 12 open, 6 room-like), `Plans` (each cell's `CellPlan`: layout + quarter turns, from where the rest of its piece lies: dead end, corridor, corner, junction, lone), `TurnedOf`, `AddWalls`, `SleeperSlots`, `LootSlots`, `Blocked`, `Clear`, used by `Expansion.AddCover` and `PlantDormantGroups` in a Grid run (rooms off the lattice keep the random cover); mockup `Docs/Mockups/cell-layouts.html`; test suite `celllayouts`
- `CellVariants.cs`: cell variants (Docs/CellVariants.md): other versions of a cell layout, by the plain one's name (`VariantsOf`, `LayoutOf`, `NameOf`; `CellPlan.Variant`), rolled per plain Combat lattice cell (`RollVariant`, `VariantChance` 40%, seeded, no wire); the 8 pit variants (`CellLayout.Pits`: boxes or discs, `InPit`, `AddPits` into `World.Pits` from `Expansion.Build`); `Forced` (dev: previews and tests); mockup `Docs/Mockups/cell-pits.html`; preview `PITCELL=<variant name> [PITFALL=<s>] EXPANDMAP=0 GRIDCORE=1 ZOOM=1 shot.sh x hud`; suite `pits`
- `Pits.cs`, `Game/PitLook.cs`: pits (Docs/CellVariants.md): `ActivePits`, `PitAt`, `PitRim` (the rim a body climbs out on); enemies: `PitEnemy` (host, after `TickKnock`: a shoved enemy dies, the kill to `LastHitBy`; `HoversOverPits`), `KeepEnemyOffPits` (walking never goes in), `PitSteerLayout` (steering with pits as walls, from `SteerLayout`); players: `PitLocal` (your own game, end of `LocalControl`: drop in for `PitDrop` s (`PitFallT`, controls held), climb out; bots keep off), `Msg.PitFall` → `HostPitFall` (`PitHurt` 15% max HP, never below 1, `PitShield` 1 s), `FxKind.PitFall`; the look: the Hazard rim (`DrawPits`, under the fog veil and walls), the fall (`DrawPitFall`; `PitHides` hides the body down there), minimap marks (`RoomsLook.cs`); suite `pits` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/Pits.cs`)
- `CoreLayouts.cs`: the core's ten floor plans (Docs/Mockups/core-layouts.html; on the 50 px floor grid, test `corelayouts`): `CoreLayoutId`, `CoreLayout` (pieces for a quarter mirrored four ways, or a half turned 180°), `Expanded`, `AddSolids` (into `Expansion.Build`'s cover in place of the core rooms' random cover, `World.Core`), `Crates` (`World.With` uses them in place of the boss cycle's plan), `Pick` (never the last, all ten before a repeat); `Game.CoreLayout`/`CoreLayoutsUsed`, `PickCoreLayout` (run start, each Campaign map reset in `ResetMap`), synced in `Msg.ExpandState`, saved on the `expand` line; preview `CORELAYOUT=<0-9> EXPANDMAP=0 GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDAT=800,500 ZOOM=0.36 shot.sh x hud`; suite `corelayouts` (`Tests/CoreLayouts.cs`)
- `RoomTraps.cs`, `Game/RoomTrapLook.cs`: room traps from the cell layouts' trap slots (`RoomTraps.For` rolls each slot of a Combat grid room: turret (wall slots only) / spike plate / slow field / empty, seeded from the cell so every machine agrees, no wire data; a map turret is set into the wall beside its slot (`SnapToWall`, `RoomTrap.Face`, in `RoomTrapList`; none with no wall) and aims only within 75° of straight out (`InArc`); look `DrawEmbrasureTurret` (Dev Kit turrets stand free: `DrawTrapTurret`), preview `TURRETSTAND=dx,dy`; `Game.RoomTrapList`, `TickRoomTraps` host: spike ticks (square plates), turret shots at the nearest player; `RoomTrapStops` (from `WorldStops`): your shots wear a turret down and wreck it, hits synced as `Msg.TrapHit` (health bar: `TurretHpFrac`), wrecks synced at the end of `Msg.ExpandState` (`WriteTrapWrecks`), cleared on a map reset; `TrapSlowAt` feeds `HoneySlowAt`); the look draws them under the bodies (`DrawRoomTraps`: the spike grate jabs on the tick, the slow field's grid sags under players, `SagPoint`); preview `EXPANDMAP=5 FOGROOM=4 ZOOM=0.8 shot.sh x hud 1 0 1` (`TRAPSTAND=slow|spike[,dx,dy]` stands you on one); test suite `celllayouts`
- `BonusLoot.cs`: bonus loot stashes at a cell layout's loot slots (`CellLayout.Loot`, `CellLayouts.LootSlots`): `BonusLoot` numbers (`Chance` 60% a slot, `Gold(wave)`, a vector or health orb now and then), `Game.PlantBonusLoot` (host, from `HostPlayPieceCard` and `OpenMysteryDoor` beside `RollRoomChest`; plain Combat/floor lattice rooms; a full copy per player via `LootOwners`); test in suites `celllayouts` and `mysterydoor`
- `Gates.cs`, `Game/GateLook.cs`: the Expanding arena's shutter gates (Docs/RoomBuilding2.md step 3): `GateFit` (a door), `Gates` rules (price, `CanFit`, `NearestDoor`), `Game.GateFits` (synced in `Msg.ExpandState`), the clocks (`GateShut`/`GateCool`, `Msg.GateUse`, `Msg.GateState`, `TickGates`), `PushOutOfGates` (from `PushOutOfSolids`), `SteerLayout` (steering routes round shut gates); the Laser grid look, the G hint (`DrawGateHint`), the draw screen's Gate mode (`expGateMode`, `ExpansionUi.cs`); previews `EXPANDMAP=6 EXPANDGATE=6`, `EXPANDGATEMODE=1`; test `ExpandingGatesTest`
- `World.cs`: an arena's geometry as data (`World`: areas, doors, portals (floor, or a wall stretch: `WorldPortal.Half`, `PortalExitFor(at, r)`/`WallHop`, mouths for steering `LayoutDef.Mouths`; look `DrawWallPortals` in `Game/RoomsLook.cs`), wall spans, cover, steering floor plan `With`), `ArenaLayout` (Single, Wings, Expanding later), `World.Single` (the plain box); the Wings map is `Rooms.Wings`, `Game.CurrentWorld`/`ArenaKind` pick the one in play (Docs/ExpandingArena.md)
- `Rooms.cs`, `Game/RoomsLook.cs`: the walled worlds' shared numbers (`Rooms.DoorW`, `WallT`, `SpawnClear`, `EnemyDamageMult`...) and their run code: `PickRooms`/`RaiseRoomsNow`/`RoomsOn` (the Expanding arena's walls up on regular waves), `CurrentWorld`, `ArenaKind`, random spawns (`RoomsRandomSpot`, `AreaCrowd`), a duel room's lock (`AreaLocked`, `RoomsLeash`, `RoomsKeepHome`), portals (`RoomsPortal`), `PullIntoHub` (boss waves), `GrantVaultPrize` (a won mini-boss duel); the floor, door and minimap look. Camera follow in `CameraZoom.cs`
- `Layouts.cs`, `LayoutRules.cs`, `Game/LayoutLook.cs`: the six arena layouts (walls, pillars, player and enemy edge gates, per boss cycle): the data, how they play (push-out, shots, spawns, steering), the look (phosphor vector, neon-tinted per coming boss: `BossWallInk`, `WallInk`, Docs/Mockups/arena-layouts-digital.html)
- `SolidGrid.cs`: a floor plan's walls filed by 128 px cell (`LayoutDef.Grid`, built once per `Solids` array), so `SegBlocked` tests only the walls along a line and `ClearOfWalls` only those in its cell; exact for bodies up to `Margin`; a grown Expanding map has ~300 walls and ~1000 corners, and `SteerDir` tries corners cheapest-first; test `SolidGridTest` (suite `layouts`)
- `Game/WallTouchLook.cs`, `Game/WallHumSoundBank.cs`: a player touching a wall: arcs and sparks along its face (`DrawWallTouch`, after `DrawLayout`), each player's touch level easing in and out (`TickWallTouch`), and the looping neon hum (`Sfx.WallHum`, `SetWallHum`; local, never sent); preview `WALLTOUCH=1 [WALLT=<s>] LAYOUT=3 shot.sh x hud 3 1 1`
- `Game/SolidUnion.cs`: a floor plan's box walls merged into one shape for drawing (non-overlapping fills, outside edges only, cached), so corners, T-joins and crossings draw seamless; drawn by `DrawSolidUnion` in `LayoutLook.cs` (world-fixed hatching `HatchWorld`)
- `Shop.cs`: the Bazaar (gold, shelves that grow with Add stock, shop actions, selling upgrades, gold instead of a card)
- `BossCards.cs`, `Game/BossCardsUi.cs`: the boss card pick (Docs/ArmsDealer.md step 3; `Game.BossCardPicks`: Expanding + room cards): `BossCards.Offer` (5 codes: a Piece, or `KitBase` + a PathId for a Floor kit), `Player.PendingCards`/`OfferCards` (the offer rides in `Player.Offer`), take/reroll/skip in `Game.HostPick`/`NextOffer`, `BotBossCard`; the picker's BOSS SPOILS! cards (`DrawBossCardTrophy`); preview mode `bosscards`
- `ArmsDealer.cs`, `Game/ArmsDealerLook.cs`: the Arms Dealer (Docs/ArmsDealer.md; `RoomKind.Arms`, from its room card or a mystery door): every weapon mod for gold (`ModIds`, `ModPrices` 150/250/400 by stacks held, `ShopAction.BuyMod`, only in its room), its wares on pedestals (`ShopPedestalLook.cs`; the old stall look `DrawArmsStall` is no longer drawn); preview `shop` mode `MARKET=arms`, `GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDMAP=0 CARDROOM=arms`; suite `armsdealer`
- `ShopPedestals.cs`, `Game/ShopPedestalLook.cs`: pedestal rooms (every vendor room: shop, Black Market, Arms Dealer, Medic; owner Nov 26): `ShopPedestals.Spots`/`AllSpots`/`Walls`/`Near`/`Reach`, `RoomArea.PedestalWalls` (West/East/North/South bits), `Expansion.PedestalShop`/`ShopPedestalWalls`/`OnShopPedestalWall`/`ShopWallBetween` (a lattice vendor cell's doors, from the wall facing its Parent: shop 2, Black Market and Arms Dealer 1, Medic 3; no door, door spot or parent link through a pedestal wall, used by `Build`, `GridSpots`, `FromTiles`, `ConnectedAreas`; no cover in a vendor room); UI: `DrawShopPedestals` (runner, wall strips, lanterns, `DrawWarePedestal`, `BuildGlyphGl`), `ShopWares`/`Place` (each kind's wares spread over its walls), `DrawPedestalTags` (prices, the ware you stand at), `TickPedestalKey` (F buys; Sell opens `DrawSellUpgrade`), `PedestalSellUp`; vendor rooms have no screen (`ShopScreenUp`); preview `GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDMAP=0 CARDROOM=shop|market|arms|medic CARDABOVE=1 SHOPGOLD=90 [CARDDX=x CARDDY=y] ZOOM=0.9 shot.sh x hud 1 0 1`; suite `shoppedestals`
- `Game/ShopStallLook.cs`: `StallDeep`, the shop's deep gold left from the old stall (used by the pedestals: ShopPedestalLook.cs)
- `Medic.cs`, `Game/MedicLook.cs`: the Medic (`RoomKind.Medic`, from its service card or a mystery door): the only room shop with a full heal and a revive token (`ExpansionGame.cs` `ServiceAllowed`; its wares on pedestals, `ShopPedestalLook.cs`), `HasMedicRoom`/`InMedicRoom`, the old bay look (`DrawMedicBay`, no longer drawn); preview `shop` mode `MARKET=medic`, `GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDMAP=0 CARDROOM=medic`; suite `armsdealer`
- `Market.cs`: the Black Market: `BuildKind` (teleporter, traps), prices, each player's stash, `PlacedBuild` traps on the map, `PlaceBuild` / `Msg.PlaceBuild`
- `Turret.cs`, `Game/TurretLook.cs`: the Black Market's Turret (`BuildKind.Turret` in `Placed`, with `Owner` and `Waves`): `TickTurrets` (host: shoots the nearest enemy in `TurretRange` in a wave, `TurretDamage` by wave, the owner's `TurretShot`s), `AgeTurrets` (a wave cleared: one fewer, gone after `Market.TurretWaves`), `TurretsOf` (`Market.TurretsEach` per player); the look (plate, feet, owner ring, tracking twin barrels, wave pips); preview `GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDMAP=0 TURRETS=1`; suite `turret`
- `Coins.cs`, `Game/CoinLook.cs`: gold coins dropped by kills in the Expanding arena (`Coin`, `DropCoins`, `TickCoins`, `SyncCoin`; `SyncCoins`: the snapshot sends only coins dropped, moved or taken since the last one, a full list every `CoinFullEvery`); they never despawn, magnet to the nearest living player and give that player their gold; the look spins and glints, and they show as gold dots on the minimap (`RoomsLook.cs`); vector pickups are coins with `Coin.Vec` (`DropVector`, rare drops that `AddVectors`; `DropVectorAt`, also the 1-vector pickup every `VectorKillEvery`th kill drops, `GrantVectorKill`); per-player copies (Docs/RoundFlow.md step 3): `Coin.Owner`, `LootOwners`, `AddCoin`, `CanTake`, `MinePickup` (the client hides teammates' copies; also `Orb.Owner`, `Combat.cs SpawnOrbEach`, and `Gadget.Owner`); `SweepFloorLoot` (step 4: your own coins, vector pickups and chest cards go to you as the next wave starts)
- `Game/VectorPickupLook.cs`: the vector pickup's look ("Drawn room": a tile whose outline draws itself, amber ring when big; mockup `Docs/Mockups/vector-pickup.html`; preview `VECPICK=1 Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud`)
- `Game/VectorGainLook.cs`: vectors coming in, seen on every screen with no wire: a Stash cache taken (`Claimed` flipping) or a vector pickup collected bursts and streams shards into the taker (`DrawVectorBursts`); your own gain pops "+n VECTORS" over you and flies shards into the VECTORS chip, which glows and counts up, all scaled by the amount (`VecWeight`: +1 a blip, +12 the full show) (`DrawVectorGainHud`, `VectorChipFlash`/`VectorChipCount` in `DrawVectorChip`); a full stock: FULL over waiting pickups and Stashes, the chip pulsing amber (`DrawVectorsFull`, `FullTag`, `vecFullNear`; preview `VECPICK=1 VECFULL=1`); preview `VECGAIN=<s> [VECGAINN=n]` (with `BREACHROOMS=1 STASHAT=1` the Stash burst)
- `Game/LootSweepLook.cs`: the floor-loot sweep as the next wave starts (Docs/RoundFlow.md step 4; host: `Core/Coins.cs SweepFloorLoot`): your coins streaming in and "+n GOLD COLLECTED" over you, from the coins of yours that went as the wave began; preview `SWEEP=<s>`
- `Game/GadgetWheel.cs`: the gadget bag's front end: hold the Gadget key with gadgets in the bag (or the Gadget wheel key, C, `KeyAction.GadgetWheel`, `TickGadgetWheelKey`) and the wheel opens at the cursor (`OpenGadgetWheel`, `DrawGadgetWheel`; a slice swaps via `Game.EquipGadget`, the middle throws), the chip over the keys (`DrawGadgetChip`: the hand, then a tile per bag slot, `DrawBagTile`) and the glyphs; the keys are read in `TickGadgetKey` (`GadgetLook.cs`); preview `GADGETS=wheel|bag|floor|recall` (`Tools/Preview/ShotGadgets.cs`)
- `Gadgets.cs`, `Game/GadgetLook.cs`: gadgets carried in a free hand (plus a 4-slot bag, `Player.BagKind`/`BagLeft`/`BagUses`, `GiveGadget`/`GiveGadgetUses`, `EquipGadgetSlot`, `Msg.Gadget` action 2) and passed between players (`GadgetId`; timed: Magnet, Energy Drink (`EnergyDrinkOn` in `Player.Speed`), Shield Generator (`TickShieldGens` wipes enemy bullets), Cloak (`CloakOn` keeps `Player.VanishT` up), Overclock Chip (`OverclockOn` in `FireRate` and `TickSlots`); EMP (`EmpPulse`); Lodestone: a 3 s cast to the hub, 3 uses (`LodestoneToHub`, `CancelLodestone` from `Damage`); Medkit (`UseMedkit`: clears the wound, heals 30%, 3 uses); throwables (`Throwable`, `ThrowCharge`: Grenade and Incendiary as Grenade launcher HE bullets, `Bullet.Incendiary` → `IncendiaryBurst` (a Flame Wall zone); Smoke Bomb a `ZoneKind.SmokeShell`; Gas Grenade a `ZoneKind.ToxicGas`, `TickToxicGas`; Land Mine a `ZoneKind.FlakMine`); uses `Player.GadgetUses`/`Gadget.Uses`; Gadget chests, `ChestKind.Gadget`, `GiveChestGadget`/`ChestGadgets`; `Gadget`, `Player.Gadget`/`GadgetLeft`/`GadgetOn`, `DropGadget` (kill drops, `KillGadgets`: every kind but the Lodestone), `TickGadgets`, `HostGadget`, `Msg.Gadget`, `SyncGadgets`); the Magnet pulls every coin and vector pickup to its holder (`MagnetOn` in `TickCoins`); the look draws them on the floor, thrown and held, the throw arc and the Gadget key (`TickGadgetKey`: tap to use, hold to throw, right click while holding cancels, `CancelGadgetThrow`); previews `GADGETS=floor|clouds|energy|shield|cloak|overclock|wheel2` (`Tools/Preview/ShotGadgets.cs`); test suite `gadgets`; each player rolls their own kill drop (`Gadget.Owner`, `HasGadget`), anyone's once carried
- `Game/GadgetShapes.cs`: the newer gadgets' looks: `DrawGadgetShape` (any kind's world shape), the Shield Generator, Medkit, Grenade, can (Smoke Bomb, Gas Grenade), Incendiary bottle, Cloak, Overclock Chip, Land Mine and EMP shapes, the Cloak's and Overclock Chip's auras (`DrawCloakAura`, `DrawOverclockAura`), `GadgetTone` (each kind's colour), the Shield Generator's dome (`DrawShieldDome`), their HUD glyphs (`DrawNewGadgetGlyph`); the Gas Grenade's cloud is Tear Gas's look in purple (`DrawTearGas(z, t, true)`, TankAltLook.cs)
- `Difficulty.cs`, `Modifiers.cs`, `Balance.cs`: difficulty presets (and the mechanics tier: `DiffKnob.Mechanics`, `MechTier`, `Game.MechTier`/`MechAtLeast`/`MechOk`; Docs/DifficultyTiers.md), run modifiers, general balance numbers
- `ShowcaseAlt.cs`: how the lobby loadout preview presses a weapon's Alt: one `Table` line per weapon with an Alt (every one, 40+; test `showcasealts`; `Tap`/`Hold` helpers, phases loaded grenades etc.), and `ChalkAim` (Spirit Chalk draws a loop, ignites it, sweeps a line; test `nimbuschalk`)
- `Pause.cs`, `DevTools.cs`, `Showcase.cs`: pause sync, F1 dev tools, lobby loadout preview game (with stand-in teammates for ally abilities; `AltFire`: the MAIN FIRE | ALT FIRE keys, `LobbyUi.cs` `PickShowcaseFire`; the floor's light and the colour wheel key's hue strip: `DrawShowcaseFloor`/`ShowcaseGlow`/`DrawHuePanel`; preview `SHOWCASEKEYS=1 [SHOWCASEALT=1] [SHOWCASEHUE=<0-1>] [HUEOPEN=1] shot.sh x lobby`)
- `DevSpawners.cs`: dev spawners (F1 dock, Spawn an enemy with Spawner on): `DevSpawner` pads that bring in one enemy type every few seconds while a wave is on, up to a cap alive (`DevPlaceSpawner`, `DevRemoveSpawner`, `DevClearSpawners`, `TickDevSpawners`, `Enemy.DevSpawner`; `SyncDevSpawners` in the snapshot); look `Game/DevSpawnerLook.cs`; test suite `spawners`
- `Flood.cs`, `Game/FloodLook.cs`, `Game/FloodSoundBank.cs` (`Sfx.FloodCast`/`FloodLap`/`FloodDrain`, played from the pool's clock by `UpdateFloodSound`, never sent; `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh Flood`): Flood, the Tank Elementalist's 4th choice (`AbilityId.Flood`, `AbilityDef.Fourth`: the lobby's 4th choice, `Upgrades.AbilityLimit`/`FourthAbilities`): the stamp (`CastFlood`: 10 damage, 60 px shove), a `ZoneKind.Flood` pool where cast (`FloodReach` spreads it 0.5 s, 6 s, drains 0.8 s) that slows 40% (heavies 20% via `WadeMult`) and keeps enemies wet 3 s after (`TickFlood`); look B Breaker (crest, swells, foam rim, wader wakes; `Docs/Mockups/flood.html`); Deluge mastery, High Tide card; preview `FLOOD=<s> [FLOODMASTERED=1] shot.sh x hud`; test `run.sh 1 30 flood`
- `GaleWall.cs`, `Game/GaleWallLook.cs`: the Elementalist DPS's Gale Wall (`AbilityId.GaleWall`, the lobby's 4th choice: `AbilityDef.Fourth`, `Upgrades.AbilityLimit`): the lens (`Player.GaleT`/`GalePos`/`GaleAim`/`GaleWide`/`GaleDots`, replicated), `GaleStops` from the enemy-bullet loop in `Combat.cs` (each shot a charge), `UnleashGaleWall` on a second press (`GalePress`) or the timer (`TickGaleWalls`): the cone; look: the Plasma membrane of `Docs/Mockups/kinetic-wall-fx.html` (boot, wobbling skins, ripples from `FxKind.GaleCatch`, crackling held shots, strain; the `FxKind.GaleBlast` collapse and cone); sounds `Game/GaleWallSoundBank.cs` (wind: gust cast, howl loop, puff catch, whistle full, gale unleash; icon `galewall`; played by `TickGaleWallSounds`, never sent); Momentum Bank card; preview `GALEWALL=<s> [GALEBLAST=<s>] [GALECAP=1] shot.sh x hud` (`Tools/Preview/ShotGaleWall.cs`); test `run.sh 1 30 galewall`
- `SanctumGlass.cs`, `Game/SanctumGlassLook.cs`: the Healer Knight's Sanctum Glass (`AbilityId.SanctumGlass`, the lobby's 4th choice: `AbilityDef.Fourth`): the flat stained-glass pane (`Player.GlassT`/`GlassPos`/`GlassAim`/`GlassWide`/`GlassHealed`/`GlassCap`, replicated), `GlassStops` from the enemy-bullet loop in `Combat.cs` (every enemy shot, red aimed too, becomes a healing mote to the most hurt player; `FxKind.GlassMote`), the cap and timer (`TickSanctumGlass`, `ShatterGlass`: `FxKind.GlassShatter`); look: panes, floor light, flashes, cracks, time and healing lines, curving motes, spinning shards; icon `sanctumglass`; Rose Window card (`Stats.GlassCapMult`); preview `SANCTUM=<s> [SANCTUMCAP=1] shot.sh x hud 1 6 2` (`Tools/Preview/ShotSanctumGlass.cs`); test `run.sh 1 30 sanctumglass` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/SanctumGlass.cs`)
- `DeployableCover.cs`, `Game/DeployableCoverLook.cs`: the Tank Soldier's Deployable Cover (`AbilityId.DeployableCover`, the lobby's 4th choice, two charges): `CastCover` plants a `ZoneKind.Cover` 64 px ahead (Aim its facing, BlockHp/SoakMax its health, GuardFrac replicated), solid after `CoverSolid`; `CoverStops` (enemy-bullet loop in Combat.cs) and `KeepOffCovers` use the bracket's three boxes (`CoverPiece`/`CoverTouches`); three per player (`CoverCap`, +1 per Reinforced Plating card, which also adds `Stats.CoverHpMult`; `FoldCover` the oldest), `FoldAllCovers` at wave end; look A Stake Drop (throw, landing, wall and arms unfolding, bolts, wear, `FxKind.CoverHit`/`CoverBreak`/`CoverFold`; `Docs/Mockups/deployable-cover.html`); sounds `Game/CoverSoundBank.cs` (`Sfx.CoverDeploy`/`CoverBreak`/`CoverFold`/`CoverTing`, played locally by `UpdateCoverSound`; render with `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh Cover`); preview `COVER=<s> [COVERWEAR=1] [COVERBREAK=<s>] ZOOM=1.6 shot.sh x hud` (`Tools/Preview/ShotCover.cs`); test `run.sh 1 30 cover`
- `DecoyDummy.cs`, `Game/DecoyDummyLook.cs`: the Healer Soldier's Decoy Dummy (`AbilityId.DecoyDummy`, the lobby's 4th choice, three charges of 4 s): `CastDecoy` throws a `ZoneKind.DecoyDummy` to the cursor (Aim/Marched the throw, BlockHp/SoakMax its health, GuardFrac replicated, `Zone.Blast` the pop's reach), up after `DecoyFly` + `DecoyInflate` (`DecoyUp`); `DecoyTarget` (TickEnemies) draws regular enemies within `DecoyPull` (bosses for `DecoyBossGlance`), `DecoyStops` (enemy-bullet loop in Combat.cs) and `DecoyContact` (melee bites, kept outside) wear it (`WearDecoy`), `PopDecoy` the confetti blast (stun + shove), `FoldDecoy`/`FoldAllDecoys` the deflate (3 per player, 12 s, wave end); Double-Ply card (`Stats.DecoyHpMult`); look: throw, puff-up, vinyl soldier, pull ring, wobble, `FxKind.DecoyHit`/`DecoyPop`/`DecoyFold`; sounds `Game/DecoyDummySoundBank.cs` (`Sfx.DecoyThrow`/`DecoySqueak`/`DecoyPop`/`DecoyFold`, played locally by `UpdateDecoySound`); preview `DECOY=<s> [DECOYWEAR=1] [DECOYPOP=<s>] ZOOM=1.6 shot.sh x hud` (`Tools/Preview/ShotDecoy.cs`); test `run.sh 1 30 decoy`
- `Engineer.cs`: the Engineer class (`Kit.Engineer`; Tank Vector Wall / Bastion, Healer Med Pylon / Safe Room (`MinionKind.VectorBlock`, `ZoneKind.MedPylon` / `SafeRoom`, `SmashWall`, `CastPylon`); one kit per role so far: `WeaponDef.FillsGaps` / `AbilityDef.FillsGaps` stand in for its empty lobby places, `Defs.WeaponGap` / `KitLocked`, `Upgrades.AbilityGap` / `UltGap`): `TickEngineer` (robots, pylons), `RobotTarget` (robots draw enemies), Drag Pylon (`ZoneKind.DragPylon`) and Breach (`CastBreach`: blast, Expanding-arena room via `SurpriseOptions`, `BreachLoot`); test `run.sh 1 1 engineer` (`Tests/Engineer.cs`)
- `EngineerBots.cs`: the Engineer's robot weapons (each role's one weapon, filling its locked places): Iron Fist / Stasis Gun / Vector Blade (`WeaponId`s; defs in `Engineer.cs`), a click summons `MinionKind.TankBot` / `MedicBot` / `AssaultBot` at the cursor (`SummonBot`, `SendBot` on a click on an enemy), `TickBot` (walks into range, shoots, the Medic Bot heals), the basic attacks (`TickBotWeapon`: fist shove, blade swipe, the held `ZoneKind.Stasis` field `HoldStasis`), `KeepBots` / `BotFallen` (8 s rebuild); test `run.sh 1 1 engbots` (`Tests/EngineerBots.cs`)
- `Game/EngineerBotLook.cs`: the robots (`DrawEngineerBot`: the Engineer model in look 3, robot steel), the held tools (`DrawEngineerTool`: gauntlet, stasis emitter, blade), the stasis field (`DrawStasisZone`) and `FxKind.FistShove`; preview `ENGINEER=bots ENGROLE=dps|tank|healer [ENGBUILD=<0-1>] ZOOM=2.2 shot.sh x hud`
- `BumbleRemote.cs`, `Game/BumbleRemoteLook.cs`: the DPS Engineer's Bumble Remote (`WeaponId.BumbleRemote`, the DPS 2nd key): you never attack; a weapon companion `MinionKind.BumbleBot` (flown by `MoveBumble`, `Minion.Wind` 0 cruise, 1 Fire held, 2 dive) springs after your cursor within `BumbleLeash`, shoots `Defs.DpsShot` stingers at the enemy nearest it (twice as fast with Fire held), draws nobody in (enemies keep going for you); Alt Swarm Burst (`LocalBumbleBurst`/`HostBumbleBurst` over `Msg.WeaponDash` kind 67, `TickBumbleDive` from `TickEngineer`, `BumbleBurst`: blast, shove, free `MinionKind.Bee`s, `FxKind.BumbleBurst`, `Player.BumbleCd`); look: the robot bee, tether, lock ring, stingers (`DrawStinger` via `DrawEngineerShot`), the burst, the remote (Engineer tool 4); test `bumbleremote`; preview `BUMBLE=<s> [BUMALT=<s>] ZOOM=1.6 shot.sh x hud 3 1 2`
- `DeployableTurret.cs`, `Game/DeployTurretLook.cs`, `Game/DeployTurretSoundBank.cs`: the DPS Soldier's default ultimate (took Artillery Assault's place; no 4th choice now), the Deployable Turret (`AbilityId.DeployableTurret`, `ZoneKind.DeployTurret`; `Docs/Mockups/deployable-turret.html`): a zone you sit in for 20 s, 26 mastered (`Seated`: rooted, no dodge or moving abilities, the turret fires instead of your weapon in `TickPlayers`); `TickDeployTurret` (host) turns it onto your aim, spools the rate 7 to 30 a second and fires hitscan rounds (`DtShot`, pierce 2); `TurretBlow` the overheat blast; the look draws the drop, feet, twin barrels, heat, flash, tracers, brass and blast from the zone and its owner's aim and trigger (`DrawDeployTurrets`); `TurretSoak` (it takes the hits meant for you: 350 health, `GuardFrac`, a bar under it; wrecked = thrown clear and it blows), `TurretPress`/`DismountTurret` (key again: out early, it overheats with you gone, Radius 0); sounds `Sfx.DtDrop`/`DtShot`/`DtSpool`/`DtHeat`/`DtBlast`/`DtLatch` played locally (render `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh Dt`); card Overclocked Barrels; preview `DEPLOYTURRET=<s since the cast> [DEPLOYCAP=1] shot.sh x hud 1 6 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotDeployTurret.cs`); suite `deployturret`
- `Game/EngineerLook.cs`: the Engineer's in-match looks: the Drag Pylon zone (`DrawDragPylonZone`), Vector Wall tiles (`DrawVectorBlock`), Breach's hammer and hit (`FxKind.BreachSmash`), plus the Tools/Preview mockup (`ArenaApp.engineerMock`; `ENGINEER=real|pylon|breach|bots [ENGT=<s>] shot.sh x hud`, `Tools/Preview/ShotEngineer.cs`)
- `Arcanist.cs`: the Arcanist class (`Kit.Arcanist`, Docs/Arcanist.md; one kit per role, `FillsGaps`): Kinetic Field (`ArcShotSpeed` in `MoveBullets`), Orbit Shards / Dancing Blades (`MinionKind.ArcShard` / `ArcBlade` companions, `MoveArcane`; Alt `WeaponAlt.ShardOn/Off`, `WeaponAlt.BladeRecall`), Glyph Stylus (`Defs.GlyphShot`), Kinetic Grasp (`GraspRun`, `GraspRelease`), Grand Sigil (`ZoneKind.GrandSigil`, rim freeze, `ReleaseSigil`), Arcane Tether, Sanctum Sigil (`ZoneKind.SanctumSigil`, runes in `Zone.Aim`), Telekinetic Crush (`TelekineticCrush.cs`), Runic Array (`Player.ArrayT`, `Defs.SpectralBlade`); `SyncArcanist`; test `run.sh 1 1 arcanist` (`Tests/Arcanist.cs`)
- `TelekineticCrush.cs`: the DPS Arcanist's Telekinetic Crush: a toggle on a force meter (`Player.CrushOn`, `CrushMeter`, replicated; the slot mirrors the meter); a grip (`ZoneKind.ArcCrush`, `Zone.Aim` = caught shots) springs after the cursor carrying up to 6 enemies (`HoldInGrip`) and catching shots (`CatchInGrip`); `CloseCrush` slams (still) or flings (`TickCrushFlings`) and sends the shots back as spectral blades; burnout lock; look `DrawArcCrush` (Game/ArcanistLook.cs); test `run.sh 1 1 arcanist`
- `Game/ArcanistLook.cs`: the Arcanist's looks: the Kinetic Field ring, Shard Guard, Kinetic Grasp's hold and Runic Array's sigils (`DrawArcanistBody`), rune bolts and spectral blades (`DrawArcanistShot`), the shards and blades (`DrawArcShard`, `DrawArcBlade`; the dance, Crossing Cuts from Core `BladeDanceAt`, over the enemies in `DrawArcBladesOver`, timed from when this client first sees it; preview `DANCE=<s>`; mockup `Docs/Mockups/dancing-blades-rework.html` B), the Grand Sigil, Sanctum Sigil and Telekinetic Crush zones, the held stylus and casting glyph (the model's palette, mantle and hood are in `CharacterModels.cs`); preview `ARCANIST=tank|healer|dps shot.sh x hud` (`Tools/Preview/ShotArcanist.cs`), `KIT=7 shot.sh x kitgrid`
- `Game/EngineerModel.cs`: the Engineer's player model (`DrawPlayerModel` hands a `Kit.Engineer` player to `DrawEngineerPlayer`: look A Hi-vis; B Drafter and C Welder kept for previews), the robots' rivet gun (`DrawRivetGun`) and rivets (`KitLook.Shot`); sheets `ENGSHEET=0|1 shot.sh x englook`
- `WaterWard.cs`, `Game/WaterWardLook.cs`: Water Ward, the Healer Elementalist's 4th choice (`AbilityId.WaterWard`, `AbilityDef.Fourth`): `CastWaterWard` calls 2 drops (falling `WwFall` s, then circling 54 px out, max 10; `WwFull` refuses the press), `TickWaterWards` (turn, land, even spacing `WwSpace`, splash on touch: `WwSplash` 12 dmg + wet 52 px), `WardStops` from the enemy-bullet loop in `Combat.cs` (burst: wet 72 px); replicated `Player.WwAng`/`WwOff`/`WwFall`; look C Bubbles of `Docs/Mockups/water-ward.html` (`FxKind.WwSplash`/`WwBurst`, each screen's `wwVis` matched by ring place); HUD count on the key (`Hud.cs`); sounds in `Game/WaterWardSoundBank.cs` (`Sfx.WwCast`/`WwSplash`/`WwBurst`, sent with the effects); Deep Current card; preview `WWARD=<s> [WWENEMY=<s>] [WWSHOOT=1] shot.sh x hud` (`Tools/Preview/ShotWaterWard.cs`); test `run.sh 1 30 waterward`
- `GustGate.cs`, `Game/GustGateLook.cs`: Gust Gate, the Healer Elementalist's 2nd choice (`AbilityId.GustGate`, took Energy Ward's place): `CastGustGate` makes a `ZoneKind.GustGate` ring at the cursor (70 px, 8 s); `TickGustGate` (from `TickZones`) boosts players crossing its edge (`Player.GustT`, replicated; `GustMult` in `Player.Speed`; `GustCd`/`GustLast` host only, set by `RememberGustSteps`) and pushes enemies out with `KnockEnemy`; Jet Stream card; look: streaks, motes, time arc, speed lines on boosted players; preview `GUSTGATE=<s> ZOOM=2.2 shot.sh x hud 3 1 2` (`Tools/Preview/ShotGustGate.cs`); test `run.sh 1 30 gustgate` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/GustGate.cs`)
- `Tsunami.cs`: the Elementalist DPS's Tsunami: the wave, wet (slow) and lightning that arcs to every wet enemy (`WetArcs`, called from each lightning source)
- `Chill.cs`: Slowed vs Chilled: `Chill` (every ice attack: slow + chill stacks, the third freezes and lifts the slows; steady ice gated a stack a second), `ShowsSlowed` (the Slowed flag: any slow that moves it)
- `Charged.cs`: the Charged status (electricity, enemies only): lightning hits charge (`Charge`); charged alone doesn't conduct, but wet and charged it shorts out: constant damage plus an arc to the nearest few every 2.5 s (`TickCharged`, `ShortArcs`); drawn in `Game/WorldEnemies.cs`
- `Brittle.cs`: the Brittle status (earth, enemies only): earth hits crack (`Embrittle`: Stonespear, Earthball + rubble, Meteor, Stone Colossus), +20% damage taken (`BrittleDmgMult` in `HurtEnemy`), brittle and frozen it shatters (`TickBrittle`, `Shatter`); replicated `Enemy.Brittle` (the enemy sync's element byte); test `run.sh 1 30 elementstatus`
- `Dazzled.cs`: the Dazzled status (light, enemies only): Radiant Orb, Chain Light and Lumen Ward dazzle (`Dazzle`); a dazzled enemy's shots stray (`DazzleStrayOf` in `SpawnBullet`); replicated `Enemy.Dazzled`
- `Game/ElementStatusLook.cs`: Brittle's cracks and Dazzled's glints on the body, and their status-row icons (cracked stone, small sun); preview `ELEMSTATUS=1 ZOOM=2.4 CAMAT=660,460 shot.sh x hud 1 6 1`
- `Steam.cs`, `Game/SteamLook.cs`: the fire + water combo: a wet and burning enemy turns to steam (`TickSteam`: both used up), leaving a `ZoneKind.Steam` cloud that scalds and jams enemies in it (`TickSteamCloud`); a freeze ending leaves 6 s of wet (meltwater)
- `Wind.cs`: wind reactions (`WindHit`, called by every wind source): Wind Chill (wind on a wet enemy chills it, 3 freeze; `WindChillImmuneT` after) and Fan the Flames (wind on a burning enemy spreads its burn downwind)
- `Nimbus.cs`, `Chalk.cs`: the Nimbus (cloud minion, rain that heals and soaks enemies wet, strikes `FxKind.NimbusStrike`, the Alt Eye of the Storm over `Msg.WeaponDash` `WeaponAlt.Storm`: `HostNimbusStorm`, `TickNimbusStorm`, `Player.NimbusStormT/Cd`, `FxKind.NimbusStorm`; mockup `Docs/Mockups/nimbus-rework.html`; preview `NIMBUS=-1 NIMBUSSTORM=1.5`; test `nimbuschalk`) and Spirit Chalk (ink dots, burn/heal/bullets, loops that pen enemies in, the Alt Ignite over `Msg.WeaponDash` `WeaponAlt.Ignite`: `HostChalkIgnite`, `InkPen.Lit`, `InkDot.Lit`; preview `CHALKFIRE=1.2 ZOOM=2.4`)
- `MathTypes.cs`: `V2`, `Rgba`, `MathX`

## Combat (Core)
- `Combat.cs`: bullets, collisions, damage to players/enemies, on-hit/on-kill, burn, orbs
- `Weapons.cs`: fire rate, firing (`FireVolley` calls `WeaponDef.Fire`, else the class's `KitDef.Fire`), volleys, range (`WeaponRange`: `WeaponDef.Range`, else a swing's or shot's reach), the Revolver's Gunslinger stance (`RevolverTrigger`: a click fires, a click pulls the hammer), Mender's Bow
- `BowFocus.cs`, `Game/BowFocusLook.cs`: the Mender's Bow's Focus bar and Focus shot (zone, marker, `FocusArrow` and its wind via `KnockEnemy`; the release's Focus flag rides `Msg.Input`) and Scatter, its Alt (`WeaponAlt.Scatter` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, five `ScatterArrow`s); the look: the bar, the bigger bow, quiver and the arrow swung from it, Focus and Scatter arrows and effects (Docs/Mockups/menders-bow-rework.html); preview `BOWFOCUS=rest|draw|bar|focus|miss|swing|scatter,<s> ZOOM=2.5 shot.sh x hud 1 1 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotBowFocus.cs`); test `run.sh 1 1 bow`
- `PistolWhip.cs`, `Game/PistolWhipLook.cs`: the Revolver's Alt, the Fan stance (`WeaponAlt.RevStance` over `Msg.WeaponDash` switches; `RevFanStance`: a click is the Pistol Whip, a Knight swing, 110° and 120 px, that stuns 1 s and cuts small shots; a click within `RevLink` fans the hammer, `RevFanRounds` in a 30° cone, `FanStunBonus` on stunned enemies, then `RevFanReload`; parry after a whip resets the combo, parry + click fans, `FiresBehindShield`); replicated `Player.RevStance`/`RevStep`/`RevCocked`/`RevCockT`/`RevFanReload`; the look: a brass crescent in the glow pass, sparks and a landing flash, the hand swinging the gun (`WhipHandPose`); the pips under you are `DrawRevolverPips` (WorldShots.cs); mockup `Docs/Mockups/revolver-rework.html`; preview `WHIP=<s>` (`WHIPFAN=1`); test suites `pistolwhip`, `revolver`
- `RailBeam.cs`, `Game/RailBeamLook.cs`: the Soldier DPS's Railgun (from Docs/Mockups/railgun-rework.html): hold to charge (a `ChargeWeapon`), let go for an instant beam that ricochets off edges, walls and pillars (`FireRailgun`, `RailCast`, `RailLine` for the aim preview; `RailDamage`/`RailWidth`/`RailPierce`/`RailBounces` by charge), its charged path (`RailPath`, an ion trail in KitWeapons.cs), the heat meter (`Player.RailHeat`, replicated) and Alt's Heat Dump cone of fire (`WeaponAlt.HeatDump`, `HostHeatDump`); looks `FxKind.RailBeam`, `FxKind.HeatDump`, the heat gauge and your aim preview; preview `RAILGUN=<s>` (`RAILHOLD`, `RAILAIM`, `RAILHEAT`, `RAILDUMP=1`)
