- `SleepyCat.cs`, `Game/SleepyCatLook.cs`, `Game/SleepyCatSoundBank.cs`, `Tools/Preview/ShotSleepyCat.cs`: Sleepy Cat, the Healer Summoner's fourth ultimate (`AbilityId.SleepyCat`, `UltKey = 3`, `CatUltimates`; mockup claude.ai artifact "Sleepy Cat"): one `ZoneKind.SleepyCat` zone (Pos the cat, Aim its heading, Radius the meow's reach, replicated `Marched` speed, `Quiet` napping, `BoltsLeft` meows left, `GunCd` since the last meow); `CastSleepyCat`, `TickSleepyCat` (the run after `AimAt`, a click's `Meow` marking `Enemy.CatMark`, bots auto-meow via `CatCanMark`), `StartCatNap` (a `Stun` of `ScNap`, bosses `ScBossNap`, `Enemy.CatNapT`; the purr heal), `TickCatNap` (EnemyAI), `CatOn` (Fire put away, Game.cs); the replicated `Enemy.CatNap` byte (`CatNapByte`, `CatMarked`/`CatAsleep`/`CatSleepLeft`; sleepers show no daze stars). Look: the reach ring, meow pulse and paw pips, the galloping cat and its meow arcs, the napping loaf with moon and Zs, the purr ring and nap arc (`DrawSleepyCatFloor`, `DrawSleepyCats`), paws and sleep rings on enemies (`DrawCatMarks`). Sounds `Sfx.Meow`, `Sfx.CatNap` (`render.sh Meow`). Preview `CAT=<s> [CATCAP=1] shot.sh x hud 1 6 1`; test suite `sleepycat`
- `BloodRavager.cs`, `Game/BloodRavagerLook.cs`: Blood Ravager, the DPS Hemomancer's second ultimate (`AbilityId.BloodRavager`, `UltKey = 1`, `RavagerUltimates`; mockup claude.ai artifact "Hemomancer Blood Legs", variant Ravager): `RavagerGait` (the eight legs' walk, shared by the host's stomps in `Player.RvGait` and each screen's look), `CastRavager`, `TickRavager` (clock, `RavagerStomp` where feet land, the ram, the lashes' hits `RavagerLashHit`), `HostRavagerLash` (picks the enemy in `RvLashR` and the free leg nearest it, up to `RvLashMax`; `Msg.WeaponDash` kind `WeaponAlt.Ravager` from `LocalRavager`), replicated `Player.RvT`/`RvLen`, `FxKind.RavagerLash` (Pos the foot, Pos2 (leg, enemy id), Radius the owner); `RavagerOn` puts the weapon away, `RavagerSpeed` in `Player.Speed`. Look: `DrawRavagerUnder` (auras: glow, reach ring, back legs), `DrawRavagerOver` (front legs, lash pips), `DrawRavagerStains`, `DrawRavagerDrops`. Preview `RAVAGER=<s> [RAVAGERCLICK=0] [RAVAGERSTILL=1] ZOOM=2.2 shot.sh x hud 1 6 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotBloodRavager.cs`); test suite `ravager` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/BloodRavager.cs`)
- `BloodBoil.cs`, `Game/BloodBoilLook.cs`: Blood Boil, the DPS Hemomancer's third ultimate (`AbilityId.BloodBoil`, `UltKey = 2`, `BoilUltimates`; mockup claude.ai artifact "Blood Boil"): `CastBoil`, `TickBoil` (the clock and the `BoilDrain` drain down to `BoilFloor`), `EndBoil` (time up, pressed again via `BoilPress`, down, wave over), replicated `Player.BoilT`/`BoilLen`; `BoilLevel` (0 to 1 from health) drives `BoilSpeedMult` in `Player.Speed`, `BoilFireMult` in `FireRate` and `BoilDmgMult` in `BloodDamageMult`; `FxKind.BoilEnd` the steam at the end. Look: `DrawBoilFloor` (boiling puddles), `DrawBoilUnder` (glow, heat rings on the heartbeat, your time arc), `DrawBoilOver` (bubbles, steam), `DrawBoilGlow`, `DrawBoilEndFx`. Preview `BOIL=<s> [BOILHP=<0-1>] [BOILEND=1] ZOOM=2.2 shot.sh x hud 1 6 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotBloodBoil.cs`); test suite `boil` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/BloodBoil.cs`)
- `MammothFall.cs`, `Game/MammothFallLook.cs`: Mammoth Fall, the DPS Summoner's third ultimate (`AbilityId.MammothFall`, `UltKey = 2`, `MammothUltimates`): one `ZoneKind.Mammoth` zone (Pos the mammoth, replicated `Origin` where it lands and `Side` its prey, Aim its heading, Radius the shockwave); `CastMammothFall`, `TickMammoth` (from `TickZones`: the summon's ring following `Player.AimAt` within `MfRange`, the dive, `MammothSlam`, `MammothRampage` charging the nearest enemy near the crater and trampling, `Zone.HitAt`), the run-off; Rare card Ice Age (`Stats.MammothWaveMult`/`MammothStun`, Summoner.cs). Look: `DrawMammothFloor` (rune, aim ring, shadows, crater, shockwave, prey brackets; WorldEnemies) and `DrawMammoths` (rift, forming, dive, rampage, fade; `DrawArenaOver`), the landing's shake. Preview `MAMMOTH=<s> ZOOM=1.3 CAMAT=640,450 shot.sh x hud 1 6 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotMammothFall.cs`); test suite `mammoth` (`Tests/MammothFall.cs`); mockup claude.ai artifact "Mammoth Fall" (approximate)
- `BroodQueen.cs`, `Game/BroodQueenLook.cs`: Brood Queen, the DPS Summoner's fourth ultimate (`AbilityId.BroodQueen`, `UltKey = 3`, `BroodQueenUltimates`): the queen is a `MinionKind.BroodQueen` minion (`CastBroodQueen`, `TickBroodQueen`: the drop and landing, hunting and venom bites, the taunt through `ImpTaunt`, the brood, the web spit, the climb; Stacks her state, LinkTo her prey, BlockCapacity her health) and her `MinionKind.Broodling`s (`TickBroodling`, `BroodPrey`; hatched into `broodPending` and joined after the minion loop, `FlushBroodlings`); webs are `ZoneKind.BroodWeb` (`TickBroodWeb`). Rare card Hatchery (`Stats.BroodHatch`, Summoner.cs). Look: `DrawBroodQueen`, `DrawBroodling` (DrawMinion), `DrawBroodWebFloor`. Preview `BROOD=<s> ZOOM=1.3 CAMAT=600,450 shot.sh x hud 1 6 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotBroodQueen.cs`); test suite `broodqueen` (`Tests/BroodQueen.cs`); mockup claude.ai artifact "Spider Queen" (approximate)
- `ForcePact.cs`, `Game/ForcePactLook.cs`: Force Pact (Tank Summoner ability, `AbilityId.ForcePact`): binds the enemy nearest the cursor, the thrall (`MinionKind.Thrall`: type, health, fights near you, respawns via `Player.PactCd`), the pact slots; the Soul Chains look and the `FxKind.Pact*` effects
- `Daze.cs`: close-in hits daze enemies (`Daze`, `TickDaze`, `Enemy.DazeT`/`DazeImmuneT`, replicated `Enemy.Dazed`): no shooting, specials or contact for a split second; `Stun` (`Enemy.StunT`) holds an enemy like a freeze but shows the stars, not ice (`IceFrozen`/`IceSlowed`; the Arc Knuckles); the stars are `DrawDazeStars` (Game/WorldEnemies.cs)
- `MeleeHit.cs`: one feel for every melee hit: the standard shove `MeleeKnock` (a `Swing.Knock` of 0 uses it, `MeleeKnockOf`) and `MeleeImpact` (daze + shove) for hits outside the swing system; finishers, Alts and the Lance keep their own push
- `Parry.cs`, `Stun.cs`, `Knock.cs`, `Repulsion.cs` (the Repulsion Cannon's blast and its Alt Undertow: `RepelGain`, `HostRepelPull` over `Msg.WeaponDash` `WeaponAlt.PullOn`/`WeaponAlt.PullOff`, `TickRepulsion`, replicated `Player.RepelMeter`/`RepelPull`): parry shield (a Knight's wider and longer: `KnightParryArc`, `ParryReachOf`; a perfect parry's needle, flash and the world slowing: `WorldSlowT`, `Game/ParryLook.cs`, `Game/ParrySoundBank.cs`), stun, knockback slides (`KnockEnemy`; `ShoveFrom`/`AwayFrom` push straight away from a point), repulsion cannon
- `WeaponMods.cs`: the weapon mod cards and what each does per weapon kind (`WeaponKind` shot/area/cone/melee from `WeaponDef.Kind`; `UpgradeDef.ByKind`, `KindEffect`), per-weapon overrides (`WeaponDef.Mods`: `Say`, `As`, `Without`), `Off` (never offered: does nothing for that weapon), `ApplyFor` (`Upgrades.Compute` with your weapon), `DescFor`; test `run.sh 1 1 weaponmods`
- `Upgrades.cs`, `StatSheet.cs`, `BuildPower.cs`: upgrade cards (every offer takes an `OfferCtx`; `Game.OfferFor(p)` in a run) (`All` = `Gather` of the general cards, class ability cards, milestones, vector cards and each class's `Game.<Class>Cards`, kept beside its abilities; filters `Kit`, `Weapons`, `RequiresAbility`) and ability registry, stat panel numbers, boss health scaling
- `KeyText.cs`: the bound keys (`Alt`, and the ability keys for labels that show a key; set by `Keybinds.SyncKeyText`); `Fill` swaps "Alt" in weapon, ability and upgrade descriptions for the bound key

## Classes (Core)
- `Classes.cs`, `Kits.cs`, `Defs.cs`: class registry and hooks, minion/zone defs, weapon/enemy/bullet defs
- `IdTable.cs`, `Tools/Ids/ids.py`: ids in name order: the growing id lists (AbilityId, EnemyType, FxKind, MinionKind, Sfx, WeaponId, ZoneKind) are kept sorted, one per line, so parallel sessions don't clash (`ids.py` checks, run by CI; `--fix` sorts); `IdTable.Of` turns `[Id] = def` tables (Defs.Enemies, Kits.Zones, Kits.Minions) into arrays indexed by the id's number
- `Knight.cs`: melee swings, sword waves, Knight abilities
- `Halberd.cs`, `HalberdMotion.cs`, `Game/HalberdLook.cs`: the Tank Knight's Halberd: Reaper's Wheel (Sweep, Return Sweep, Overhead Chop, Reaping Wheel; `FireHalberd`, step in `Player.Combo`) and the Alt Set the Pike (held over `Msg.WeaponDash`: slowed, the pike turns on its own to the nearest enemy, `HbPikeA`, and pokes it away); each step's pose timed from the click (the host times its hits from it) and the Cleaver look: held in two hands, shadow, trail, ghosts, dust, sparks, cut marks, the chop's crack, the wheel's ring, the set pike and its pokes (`Docs/Mockups/halberd-swing.html`, `halberd-rework.html`)
- `Rapier.cs`: the DPS Knight's Rapier: Tempo (`Player.RapierTempo`, one per enemy a thrust hits in `RapierGainTempo`; `RapierCooldown` sets the wait between thrusts; `TickRapier` holds it for `RapierGrace`, then drains it, and replicates `RapierGraceLeft`) and the Alt Riposte stance (`WeaponAlt.RapierOn`/`WeaponAlt.RapierOff` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, `HostRapierStance`: slow guard, a press toggles it and a click lunges (`RapierClickLunge`), `RapierStanceReflect` sends bullets back and fills `Player.RapierCharge`, letting go lunges `RapierLungeLength` far, cut by `TickRapierLunge`; the old Flourish, `HostRapierFlourish`, stays unused); the thrust is a `Knight.cs` swing; the look (Needle thrust, Tempo diamonds and grace arc, `DrawRapierPips`, the stance arcs `DrawRapierStance`) is in `Game/MeleeLooks.cs`; mockup `Docs/Mockups/rapier-tempo.html`
- `Censer.cs`, `CenserMotion.cs`, `Game/CenserLook.cs`: the Healer Knight's Censer: the Thurible Rite (Sweep, Return Sweep, Benediction; `FireCenser`, step in `Player.Combo`), Embers (`Player.Embers`, lit by hits in `CenserLanded`), the Alt Censer Cast (`WeaponAlt.Cast` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, `HostCenserCast`, a burst per Ember and a healing incense cloud that also revives downed allies faster), its sounds (`Game/CenserSoundBank.cs`), and the look: both hands on the haft like the Halberd, a simulated chain, smoke, trail, heal motes, pips and Ember flames (`Docs/Mockups/censer-rework.html`)
- `TwinBlades.cs`, `TwinBladesMotion.cs`, `Game/TwinBladesLook.cs`, `Game/TwinSoundBank.cs`: the Twin Blades' Crescent Waltz: the five-step chain (host swings timed from the motion, steps forward on your own game), Twin Fang's thrown pair (a spinning pinwheel that follows `Player.AimAt` until a wall or `FangFollow` runs out, then flies back: `FlyFang`, `FangHitsWall`; replicated as `Player.FangOut`/`FangAt`, drawn by `DrawFangPair`; preview `SWINGW=TwinBlades TWINSTEP=fang SWING=<s>`), and the look: both arms, two sabres held and swung, trails, the chain pips; its sounds and the hit cue (`FxKind.TwinHit`) that drives your hit-pause and shake (`Juice.cs` `TwinHitFeel`) (`Docs/Mockups/twin-blades-rework.html`); each hit of steps 1-4 shoves slightly (`TwinCutKnock`/`TwinJabKnock`/`TwinSpinKnock`), test suite `twinblades` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/TwinBlades.cs`)
- `ArcKnuckles.cs`, `Game/ArcKnucklesLook.cs`, `Game/ArcKnucklesSoundBank.cs`: the Knight DPS's fourth weapon (`Docs/ArcKnuckles.md`, Choice 3: `Defs.WeaponCount`, the lobby's 4th key; the Wildcard's pick 18: `Defs.WildWeaponPick`/`WildWeaponKit`): two stances (`Player.KnuckleStance`, swapped on the weapon-action key: `LocalKnuckleDash` → `Msg.WeaponDash` `WeaponAlt.KnuckleStance` → `SwapKnuckleStance`); `FireArcKnuckles` (the Counter Hammer if armed: `FireCounterHammer`, `ArmKnuckleCounter` from `ParryHit`, `Player.KnuckleArmed`; else the stance's move); the Rocket stance's dash `HostRocketDash` (locked onto `KnuckleLockTarget`: `Player.KnuckleDashTo`/`KnuckleTarget`, `Swing.OnlyEnemy`); every dash and lunge moves you through `KnuckleMoveTo` (your own game in `LocalKnuckleDash` when `Player.KnuckleDashes` changes); `KnuckleHitCue` → `FxKind.KnuckleHit` → `Juice.KnuckleHitFeel`; `ResetKnuckleChain` (the parry, the swap); each punch one `FxKind.KnuckleStep` (looks 1-5) drawn from its age off the mockup's keyframes with its blast (`DrawKnuckleBlast`), the pips and charge ring (`DrawKnucklePips`), the target reticle (`DrawKnuckleLock`); `ArcKnucklesChain.cs`: the Rocket chain (Flurry, Grab & Throw, Rocket Punch; `Player.KnuckleChain`, `FireKnuckleChain`, `TickKnuckleChain` with `KnuckleRocketIdle`, `TickGrabRuns` carrying the enemy along `KnuckleGrabFist`; `FxKind.KnuckleChain`), the `Sfx.Knuckle*` cues. Preview: `SWINGW=ArcKnuckles SWING=<s> KNUCKLESTEP=0-5|dash|flurry|grab|punch|prompt [MOVE=<0-7>] [ROCKET=1] [CHARGE=<0-1>] [FULL=1] [ARMED=1] [KNWHEEL=r|l|u|d|c]` (mode hud); test suite `knuckles` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/ArcKnuckles.cs`)
- `ArcKnucklesBrawl.cs`: the Arc Knuckles' Brawl stance (Docs/Mockups/arc-knuckles-stances.html): `TickKnuckleFire` (a click a move, held repeats, the Quake Punch's charge; from the fire loop), `FireBrawl` (Pull Punch `KnucklePullTarget`, Drive Punch `KnuckleNearTarget` + `Swing.KnuckleKnock`, Slip Dash, Pin Punch, Barrage, Quake Punch; `Player.KnuckleMove`/`KnuckleRooted`/`KnuckleCharge`), the click wheel's steer (`PlayerInput.Steer` → `Player.KnuckleSteer`, `KnuckleSteerCode`/`KnuckleSteerDir`), `KnuckleHitExtra` (from `LandSwing`), the Wall Slam (`Enemy.KnuckleSlamBy`, `KnuckleWallSlam` from `TickKnock`), `SwapKnuckleStance`
- `Game/ArcKnucklesWheel.cs`: your own Arc Knuckles' click wheel: opens where a landed Pull Punch was clicked, fixed on the screen (`KnuckleWheelSteer` from the input, `ScreenToArena`, which `MouseArena` uses), drawn with the players (`DrawKnuckleWheel`) and its move name on the HUD (`DrawKnuckleWheelLabel`)
- `Lightbringer.cs`, `LightbringerMotion.cs`, `Game/LightbringerLook.cs`: the Healer Knight's Lightbringer: Dawn, Mercy, Noon chain (`FireLightbringer`; the step shares `Player.Combo` with the Twin Blades; `LightLanded` from `LandSwing`: the gauge, the healing and warding crescents, Noon's ring heal), Mercy's ward (`Ward`, `Player.MercyT`), the Sunlance (`WeaponAlt.Lance` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, `HostSunlance`, `SunlanceTarget`, `LandSunlance`); the motion reuses `TwinBladesMotion`'s pose and `At`; the look draws the arms and longsword held and swung (the model leaves its own out), `FxKind.LightStep` (with Noon's ring) and `FxKind.Sunlance` from their age, the chain pips and the ward hexagon (`Docs/Mockups/lightbringer-rework.html`, `Docs/LightbringerRework.md`)
- `Game/LightbringerFxLook.cs`: the Lightbringer's light, ported from the chosen looks of `Docs/Mockups/lightbringer-looks.html`: Noon's Sundial (`DrawSundial`), the Sunrise crescents (`DrawSunriseCrescent`; a player's `KnightWave` whose replicated `Look` is Dart for Dawn, Crescent for Mercy), the Sunlance's Spear (`DrawSpearLight`), Mercy's Hex plates (`DrawMercyPlates`) and `FxKind.LightMark` (heal burst, ward snap, a struck ward; `DrawLightMark`); the additive layers go in the glow pass (`DrawLightGlow`, from `Juice.cs` `DrawGlowPass`), or the ordinary pass with Visual effects off
- `Lance.cs`, `LanceMotion.cs`, `Game/LanceLook.cs`, `Game/LanceSoundBank.cs`: the Tank Knight's Lance, the Breach: Jab, Second Jab, Wide Sweep (eight cones timed from the motion, `LanceSweep`), Breaching Thrust (`FireLance`; the step shares `Player.Combo` with the Twin Blades; steps forward on your own game from `LanceMotion.MoveFrom`); Impact (`LanceBump`, `LanceWallImpact`, called from `Knock.cs` `TickKnock` for enemies a Lance swing shoved, `Enemy.ImpactBy`); Hook & Haul (`WeaponAlt.Hook` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, `HostLanceHook`, `HookTarget`, `LandHook`, primes `Player.LancePrimed`); the look draws the arms, heater shield and Winged War Lance held and swung (the model leaves its own out), `FxKind.LanceStep`/`LanceHook` from their age, `LanceSpark`, `LanceImpact` (its orange number is `Juice.cs` `ImpactNumber`), four pips and the hook's cooldown ring; its sounds and the hit cue (`FxKind.LanceHit`, `Juice.cs` `LanceHitFeel`) (`Docs/Mockups/lance-rework.html`)
- `BulwarkBarrier.cs`, `Game/BulwarkBarrierLook.cs`: the Tank Knight's fourth weapon, a kite shield (`Docs/Mockups/bastion-shield.html`): the always-on half guard (`BulwarkBlocks`/`BulwarkHit`, called from the bullet loop in `Combat.cs`), the Shield Chain (Rim Cut, Backhand, Boss Slam, Shield Throw; `FireBulwark`, step in `Player.Combo` like the Lance; the throw flies on the host in `TickShieldThrows` along `BbThrowAt`, drawn from `FxKind.BulwarkThrow`), braced melee blocking (`BulwarkMelee`, from `ParryContact`/`ParryStrike`) filling Ram, the Brace held on the weapon-action key (`WeaponAlt.BraceOn`/`Off` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, `HostBrace`, `Player.BbBrace`) soaking shots into `Player.BbRam`, the Shield Bash (`HostBulwarkBash`: a box `Swing` along the lane, your own game dashes when `Player.BbBashes` changes in `LocalBulwark`; full Ram throws `Defs.SteelShard` shards from `TickBulwark`); the look draws the plate on springs (`BbAnim`) from the aim, `FxKind.BulwarkStep`/`BulwarkBash`/`BulwarkBlock`/`BulwarkThrow` from their age, and the Ram pips. Preview: `BULWARK=<s> BBMODE=chain|guard|brace|bash|melee` (mode hud); test suite `bulwark`
- `LastStand.cs`: the Tank Knight's Last Stand ultimate: plant, a crescent of nine plates that stops enemy bullets from the front while you take 10% of the damage (`StandBlocks`/`StandHit`, called from the bullet loop in `Combat.cs`), root, turns slowly, ends on cancel, going down or the wave ending (`LastStandLook.cs` draws the plates, stakes and hit sparks; `Docs/Mockups/last-stand-aegis.html`)
- `GuardiansOath.cs`, `Game/GuardiansOathLook.cs`: the Healer Knight's Guardian's Oath: press, then click the ally to grab (`OathPickSlot`, `LocalOathPick`, `OathPickTarget`; the pick's reach ring, ally rings and tether in `DrawOathPick`, preview `OATHPICK=1|0`), charge to them, haul both back to the cast spot (`StepFrom`), plant a damage-cutting Ward of Light (`ZoneKind.OathWard`, `Zone.DamageCut`); the ward is drawn in `Game/WorldEnemies.cs`; mockup in `Docs/Mockups/guardians-oath.html`
- `BoStaff.cs`, `Game/BoStaffLook.cs`: the Bo Staff's shove (`HostBoShove` from `WeaponAlt.Shove` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, landed by `TickBoShoves`, `FxKind.StaffShove`) and its chain pips (`DrawBoStaffPips`); the slash, jab, spin chain is in `FireNinjaWeapon` (`Ninja.cs`, `Player.BoStep`, no timer) and uses the ordinary melee swing look
- `Quarterstaff.cs`, `Game/QuarterstaffLook.cs`: the Healer Knight's Quarterstaff stacks (`Player.StaffStacks`, one per enemy a swipe hits, limit `StaffCap`, no decay) and the Alt jab (`WeaponAlt.Jab` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, `HostStaffJab`: a cone swing); the Beacon look: held model with a lantern head (`DrawQuarterstaffModel`), stack pips (`DrawStaffPips`), the jab's cone (`StaffJabCone`); the swipes are `Knight.cs` swings drawn by `MeleeLooks.cs`, alternating left and right
- `GreatswordChain.cs`, `Greatsword.cs`, `Game/GreatswordLook.cs`, `Game/GreatswordSoundBank.cs`: the Greatsword. `GreatswordChain.cs` holds the ten-step chain (`GreatChain`, `GreatMoves`, `FireGreatsword`; `Player.GreatStep` 1-10, the swing count in `Player.Combo`, `LocalGreatStep` moves you for Backstep Reap and Comet Leap) and the Moonwheel (`HostMoonwheel`, `FlyWheel`: a `FangRun` with `Great`, flown by `TwinBlades.cs`); `Greatsword.cs` keeps the chain clock (`TickGreat`), and its reset; the look draws each step as one motion from the click (`DrawGreatMove`, `DrawGreatSweeps`: the blade and the mockup's tapered crescent trail, `DrawGreatCrescent`, also used for the hanging crescents; MeleeLooks.cs leaves its Slash undrawn), ten pips (`DrawGreatPips`), the thrown sword (`DrawGreatWheel`) and Eclipse's lance (`DrawGreatRendFx`); mockup `Docs/Mockups/greatsword-chain-forge.html`
- `Kusarigama.cs`, `Game/KusarigamaLook.cs`: the Kusarigama's sickle cut then weight throw (`Player.KusariStep`; the throw is state: `HookId`, `HookT`, `KusariAim`) and Alt whirl (`HostKusariWhirl`, `TickKusari`: pull, chain sweep, snap); the look (Ink Brush: sickle, chain links and iron weight always; brush stroke, throw line, ensō and splashes in the glow pass) draws from that state
- `Katana.cs`, `Game/KatanaLook.cs`: the Katana's two stance combos (`Docs/KatanaCombo.md`): each step (`FireKatana`), the switch (`HostKatanaSwitch`; the stance you leave keeps its place), the Momentum Burst, caltrops (`Defs.Caltrop`, steered by the host), timed cuts (`TickKatanaLater`), your own dashes (`LocalKatana`); the look: the katana model in both hands, dashes, crescents, slashes, cuts and enemy halves, the Burst, caltrops, combo pips (`FxKind.KatanaDash/Sweep/Slash/Burst/Cut`)
- `CeremonialArmor.cs`, `Game/CeremonialArmorLook.cs`, `Game/CeremonialArmorSoundBank.cs` (`Sfx.CereSummon` with its click at the landing, `CereBreak`; sent; `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh Cere`): the tank Ninja's Ceremonial Armor (`Docs/Mockups/ceremonial-armor.html`, Mandala): `SummonCerePlate` (one plate a cast, up to `CerePlateMax`), `CereSlot`/`CerePlate` (two counter-turning rings from `PlateClock`), `TickCereArmor` (blocks enemy bullets, pushes enemies, `BreakCerePlate`), `CereCut` (full regalia, in Damage); replicated `PlateN`, `PlateClock`, `PlateNewT`, `PlateMax`, `PlateHp`; `FxKind.CereBlock`/`CereBreak`; the look eases its own clock and spacing (`CereViewOf`), draws the aura, rings and sigil under the model (`DrawCeremonialFloor`) and the plates over it (`DrawCeremonialArmor`); preview `CERE=<plates>,<s>[,1]` (`Tools/Preview/ShotCeremonial.cs`); tests in suite `ninja`
- `BambooGrove.cs`, `Game/BambooGroveLook.cs`: the Tank Ninja's 4th choice, Bamboo Grove (`AbilityId.BambooGrove`, `AbilityDef.Fourth`; mockup `Docs/Mockups/bamboo-grove.html`, take B Ripple): a 0.35 s wind-up, then six rings of bamboo burst out to 140 px (`CastBambooGrove`, `groves`, `TickBambooGroves` from TickNinjas; `BgFrontAt`): 40 and a stun (1.2 s, heavy 0.6, boss 0.3) as the wave reaches each enemy, then a bleed (8/s, 4 s) once the stun ends (`groveBleeds`); the outer ring stands as a fence 3.2 s (mastered 6 s) that holds enemies out (`KeepOffGroveFences`) and stops enemy shots (`GroveStops`); card Rhizome (`Stats.BgRadiusMult`/`BgBleedMult`); the look draws everything from `FxKind.BambooGrove` (stalks hashed from its middle): the wash, cracks and front under everything (`DrawBambooGroveFloor`), the far stalks before the enemies and the near ones after (`DrawBambooGroveBack`/`Front`); preview `BAMBOO=<s> [BAMBOOCAP=1] ZOOM=2 shot.sh x hud 1 6 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotBambooGrove.cs`); test suite `bamboo` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/BambooGrove.cs`)

- `SiegeBell.cs`, `Game/SiegeBellLook.cs`, `Game/SiegeBellSoundBank.cs`: Siege Bell, the Tank Knight's 4th choice (`AbilityId.SiegeBell`, `AbilityDef.Fourth`; mockup `Docs/Mockups/siege-bell.html`, look C Cathedral): `CastSiegeBell` lowers a bell 50 px ahead (`bells`, `TickBells` from TickKnights); tolls at `BellTolls` (0.3, 0.8 s: 120 px, 8 and a flinch), the third (1.3 s) a shockwave to 260 px over 0.45 s (`BellFrontAt`/`BellReachT`: 20, a 24 px shove, a 2.5 s stun, heavy 1.5, boss 0.8; mastered 3.5 s and enemy shots erased); the caster braced while it rings: rooted on their own game (`BellRooted`, read from the effect) and 25% less damage (`BellCut` in Combat.cs); card Great Bell (`Stats.BellRadiusMult`/`BellStunBonus`); the look draws everything from `FxKind.SiegeBell`: the reach ring, toll rings and shockwave (`DrawSiegeBellFloor`), the ghost bell and each stunned enemy's draining ring (`DrawSiegeBellOver`), the halo (`DrawSiegeBellGlow`); `Sfx.SiegeBell` is the three tolls in one take; preview `BELL=<s> [BELLCAP=1] ZOOM=1.3 shot.sh x hud 1 6 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotSiegeBell.cs`); test suite `siegebell` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/SiegeBell.cs`)
- `Exsanguinate.cs`, `Game/ExsanguinateLook.cs`: the tank Hemomancer's Exsanguinate, the Barbed Lash: whips hook every enemy in reach, stunned, then a yank reels them in to touching you and drains them on arrival (`Enemy.ReelBy`/`ReelT`, `TickReels`); the look draws the whips, hooks, torn veins, braid, knot and impact from `FxKind.Exsanguinate`'s age (`Docs/Mockups/exsanguinate-rework.html`, take B)

- `Bloodbath.cs`: the tank Hemomancer's second ultimate (key 1 of the lobby's Ultimate row; `AbilityId.Bloodbath`), a channel: no upfront cost, a `ZoneKind.Bloodbath` pool 170 px round the cast that grows out over 0.9 s (`BathReach`) and drains you 4%/s for up to 10 s; a second press (`BathPress`), 15% health or going down ends it (`EndBath`: inert and drying, `Zone.Aim` 1). While on, its own tick slows (Heavy and bosses through `Enemy.WadeMult`), bleeds 15/s while in it and heals the caster 25% (no 3% on those ticks: `bathTick`), 20% less damage for allies in it (`Combat.cs`); `FxKind.BathHeal` for the heal numbers. - `Phoenix.cs`, `Game/PhoenixLook.cs`: the Summoner Healer's Phoenix (`PhoenixFlies` from `TickMinions`: glides after the owner's cursor, heals 8% max health within 220 px every 2 s; revive in `Minions.cs`); the firebird look (flame wings, tail plumes, embers, pulse rings and a range ring that burns down, timed from `Duration - T`); preview `PHOENIX=<s>` (`Tools/Preview/ShotPhoenix.cs`)
