- `Burrower.cs`, `Game/BurrowerLook.cs`: the Tank Summoner's Earthquake (a 25-damage slam round you, then a `MinionKind.Burrower` that breaks out at the cursor and tunnels 3 s, biting each enemy it passes under once for 30; `FxKind.BurrowSnap` for the head popping up under a bite) and its drawing (mound, scar, dust, rock)
- `Hydra.cs`, `Game/HydraLook.cs`: the Summoner DPS's Hydra ability (a stationary minion whose heads each spit at a different enemy) and its drawing
- `SoulLantern.cs`, `Medkit.cs`: Healer Summoner's Soul Lantern (linked lantern minion), Healer Soldier's Deployable Medkit (thrown case, healing orbs)

## Enemies and bosses (Core)
- `EnemyAI.cs`: enemy roster per wave, movement, firing, contact damage, bullet budget
- `EnemyIndex.cs`: enemies by type, gathered once per tick (`EnemiesOf`, `StaleEnemyIndex`) for per-enemy checks (Worker pack, Guard rally, `TheWarden`, Guards' nearest Rioter); `StillIn` (not removed earlier this tick)
- `NonShooters.cs`: the enemies that don't shoot: the hook (the Hook Thrower: aim, throw, yank via `KnockPlayer`, open window), the sword cut's brain (Acolyte, `Swordsman.cs`) and the Turnkey's kin (`IsPlanted`, `NonShooterBlast`, `ClearPods`); `SyncNonShooter` sends PhaseT and T2
- `Turnkey.cs`, `Game/TurnkeyLook.cs`: the Warden faction's Turnkey (runs from players, `TurnkeyRun`/`Slide` off walls and out of corners; plants Floor Mines, Shackle Traps and Mop Slicks (`PatchKind.Slick`); builds Cell Doors into the nearest free outer wall, facing in (`DoorSpot`: floor in front, none behind; phase 3 while built, gone if it dies at the work), that let Rioters out, jammed when it dies); `IsPlanted`/`IsTrap` (don't hold a wave open, `ClearPods`); its debut wave is `FactionMember.Debut` (`PickDebuts`, `ApproachWaveOf` in `Approaches.cs`); preview `TURNKEY=1 NINJA=0 ZOOM=2.4 shot.sh x hud 3`; test suite `turnkey`
- `Game/CellDoorLook.cs`, `Tools/Preview/ShotCellDoor.cs`: the Turnkey's Cell Door in the wall (a barred cell front, a prisoner pacing behind, a red beacon spinning faster as its Rioter is due with beams and chevrons on the floor; jammed: chained; being built: a gold outline and a dotted line to the Turnkey); the floor wash in the body pass (`DrawCellDoorFloor`), the door after the walls (`DrawCellDoorsOver`, WorldRenderer.cs); preview `TKDOORS=1 [TKCYCLE=1 TKT=<s>] [TKCLOSE=1] EXPANDMAP=0 MAPVIEW=2,2,1 NINJA=0 ZOOM=2 shot.sh x hud 3`
- `WaveRecipe.cs`: the late wave recipe from wave 13: `EnemyJob` (Pressure, Hunter, Zoner, Disruptor, Anchor), job shares, headliners, types and squads retiring (`Docs/EnemyRoles.md`; report `wavemix`)
- `Variety.cs`, `Readability.cs`: squads, elite traits (and their bubbles), player motion; shooters taking turns
- `Game/JobMarkerLook.cs`: the job marker under each regular enemy (bar + shape: dot Pressure, chevron Hunter, ring Zoner, diamond Disruptor, square Anchor; `Docs/EnemyReadability.md`)
- `Game/LungeLaneLook.cs`: the shared lunge wind-up lane for the Lunger and its kin and the Riot Shield (`Docs/Mockups/lunge-telegraph.html`)
- `Game/ShooterAimLook.cs`: the shared shooting line (DrawAimLane) of the aimed shooters while they wind up (Sniper, Shard, Shade, Grunt, Taser and Security Guards)
- `Game/EnemyTagLook.cs`, `Profile.TagSeen`: (also the Settings > Gameplay "Enemy names on hover" tag under the cursor, and "Name labels" (N): a name over you and every enemy, `DrawNameLabels`) the name and job tag an enemy type carries for its first three sightings (profile "tag" lines, format 6)
- `Core/BruteEnrage.cs`: Brute enrage (`BruteRage`/`BruteSpeedMult`/`BruteDamageMult`, from health lost; used in `MoveEnemy` and the contact damage in `EnemyAI.cs`; redder in `Game/WorldEnemies.cs`)
- `Core/NewEnemies.cs`, `Game/NewEnemyChips.cs`: the new-enemy chips under the between-waves box (`Game.NewTypesAt`, `Game.EnemyNote`; unmet types only, via `Profile.TagSeen`)
- `Zombies.cs`: Zombie Infection modifier (seven zombie kinds), and the Lich faction's Grave Caller (`CallGraves`, `CallerDied`, `PatchKind.Grave`, `Zombies.PhCalled`; drawn in `ZombieLook.cs`, `DrawGravePatch`; mockup `Docs/Mockups/lich-faction.html`; preview `GRAVES=<s> ZOOM=1.6 Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud 1`; test suite `zombies`)
- `Approaches.cs`, `WardenFaction.cs`: boss factions/approach mixes (`WaveLesson.Trickle`: a lesson's star spread over the wave's second half; `FadeAt`/`FactionMember.Fade`: the Long Campaign's 3-wave fade of the last faction; `Approaches.Campaign(n)`: the campaign buildup's per-faction slices; `Game.PickFaction`; a Campaign cycle's per-wave `WaveLesson`s, `FactionDef.Lessons`, `Game.LessonNow`/`PickLesson`), Warden's faction enemies (Riot Shield, Gas Grenadier, Taser Guard: it charges `TaserWindUp` 0.7 s, then one Fork Bolt, `Defs.TaserBolt`, that stuns for `StunTime`; the Warden's searchlight turn and last stand send them, `SendTaserGuards`, instead of firing stun shots himself)
- `ShieldBrains.cs`: brains step 1, the shield family (Riot Shield, Comb Shield, Slab Bearer, Carcass Bearer, Parrier): `ShieldMove`, `ShieldSpecial`, `ShieldGuard` and its `CombGuard` (the leak), `SlabGuard`/`CarcassGuard` (`Shattered` drops), `ParrierGuard` (the bounce); the Riot Shield's code itself stays in WardenFaction.cs (`RiotShieldMove`, `RiotShieldFace`, `RiotShieldSpecial`, `RiotShieldBlocks`, `WearRiotShield`); its officer body under the shield is `DrawRiotShieldBody` (WardenLook.cs, preview `SECSHIRT=3`)
- `LungeBrains.cs`: brains step 2, the lungers (Lunger, Shiv, Hornet, Driller, Mummer, Fencer, Scythe Wraith): `LungeNumbers` (start, circle, wind-up, track, dash, speed, recover, damage), `LungeMove` (move, leap lock in `Turn`, leap `Contact`), `LungeSpecial` (the wind-up/leap/recover clock, `LungerWaits`)
- `ChaseBrains.cs`: brains step 3, the brutes and chasers: `ChaseMove` (straight at you) for Brute, Thug, Bumble, Gravedigger, Swarmer, Wisp, Stomper, Gulper, Harlequin (`BrainRole.Brute` for all but the Swarmer and Wisp); specials `StompSpecial`, `GulpSpecial`, `CrossingSpecial`
- `ThugPunch.cs`: the Thug's charged punch (`ThugPunch`, `ThugPunchSpecial` on its brain): planted and face-locked, a `PunchWind` wind-up then a narrow jab wedge (`PunchReach`, `PunchHalf`, `PunchDamage`), on the Butcher's `LarderStage`/`LarderT` (sent by `SyncLarder`); the look (`DrawThugFloor`, the fist in `DrawThugBody`) is in `Game/PrisonLook.cs`; test `thugpunch` (`Tests/ThugPunch.cs`); preview `THUG=1 KNIGHT=1 ZOOM=2.6 shot.sh x hud`
- `GuardSwing.cs`: the riot Guard's baton (`GuardBaton`, `GuardSwingSpecial` on its brain): no plant, fast forehand/backhand swings in a 120° arc while you're close, on the Butcher's `LarderStage`/`LarderT` (phases `BatonWinding`..`BatonActingBack`, sent by `SyncLarder`); the look (corrections officer, nightstick, amber arc and white crescent: `DrawGuardBody`, `DrawGuardFloor`; the ready tell as you close in: `GuardReady`, `DrawGuardReady`) is in `Game/WardenLook.cs`; preview `GUARD=1 KNIGHT=1 ZOOM=2.6 shot.sh x hud` (`GUARD=2 ZOOM=2.2`: walking in)
- `LobBrains.cs`: brains step 5, the lobbers: `PlantMove` at `GrenadierRange` (ShootBrains.cs), `LobFire` (ring, colour, lead, patch: Gas Grenadier, Honey Lobber, Rock Hurler, Bile Spitter, Dusk Lobber; drives `GrenadierWind`/`GrenadierFire`), `CircleFire` (the Marshal's `MarshalCircle`)
- `ShootBrains.cs`: brains step 6, the shooters: `PlantMove(range)` (also the lobbers'; the Maw Priest's turns slowly), `SecurityMovePart` (the Needler's strafe as numbers), fire parts `BoltFire`, `CrackFire`, `MagmaFire`, `MawFire`, `PistolFire`, `DaggerFire`, `HoldFire`; `SaluteSpecial` (Gallant)
- `BasicBrains.cs`: brains step 8a, the basic faction: `KeepMove` (Grunt, Sniper, Mortar, Shielder), `ShardMove`, `SpinnerMove`, `ShielderGuard`; the generic `CodeMove`/`CodeFire`/`CodeSpecial`; each one's fire/special code (`GruntFire` ... `MedicHeal`)
- `NonShooters.cs` brains (step 8c): `HookMove`, `StillMove`, `SwordsmanMovePart`, wired in `NonShooterBrains`
- `BossAddBrains.cs`: brains step 8f: Hive Bomber (`BomberMove`, `BomberSpecial`), Gutworm, Riot Captain (`CaptainMovePart`, `CaptainGuard`), the challenge rooms' Gatekeeper and Shadow (`RoomFirePart`), the Mummer's `MaskSpecial`, and the adds their boss moves (a still move)
- Brains sections (step 8c-8f) at the end of `NonShooters.cs`, `ReaperFaction.cs`, `Infall.cs`, `Penumbral.cs`, `Squall.cs`, `Zombies.cs`, `LateGame.cs`: each family's per-enemy methods (split out of its old `...Move`/`...Special`/`...Fire`) and its `...Brains(t)`
- `PrisonKin.cs`: `Game.IsBrute` and `Game.IsLunger`, read from the brains' roles (`BrainRole.Brute`, `BrainRole.Lunger`); every Brute/Lunger check goes through them
- `Brain.cs`: **enemy behaviour classes** (Docs/EnemyBehaviours.md): `Brain` (`Move`, `Fire`, `Special`, `Guard` parts and `BrainRole` flags), the part base classes (`MovePart` with `Turn`, `FirePart`, `SpecialPart`, `GuardPart` with `Setup`/`Wear`/`Shot`), the registry (`Game.BrainOf`, `BuildBrains`), `BrainHas` for the `IsBrute`/`IsLunger`/`IsWallKin`/`IsSwordsman`/`IsHook` checks, `IsSecurity`, `BrainWindUp` (`WindUpOf`), `SharesWith` (the sound, look and thrall a type borrows by role; replaced the kin table); hooked into `MoveEnemy`, `Face`, `Special`, `EnemyFire`, `BlocksShot`, `WearShield`, `SpawnEnemyAt` (EnemyAI.cs) and Collide's blocked bullet (Combat.cs); test suite `aitrace` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/AiTrace.cs`: every non-boss enemy alone for 12 s, hashed per frame vs `Golden/aitrace.txt`)
- `SecurityGuard.cs` (Core), `Game/SecurityLook.cs`: the Warden's Security Guard: a single-shot pistol aimed straight at you (no lead), walks while it locks on, keeps a strafe side 2-4 s (`Enemy.PhaseT`), eases to a stop to reload; test `Tools/SimTest/Tests/Security.cs` (`run.sh 1 1 security`); (tracer rounds, `Defs.TracerRound`), seven to a magazine then a 2.2 s reload; it holds fire until within `SecurityEngage` of its target, one shot per 1.5 s; its ammo is `Enemy.Phase`; the look draws its lane, reload ring, body (a navy prison officer, `OfficerTorso`/`OfficerCap` shared with the Guard in WardenLook.cs; preview `SECSHIRT=1`), casing, pips and the tracer (`Docs/Mockups/security-guard.html`)
- `ReaperFaction.cs` (Core), `Game/ReaperCultLook.cs`: the Twin Reapers' faction, the Dusk and Dawn cult (`Approaches.cs` table, squads, the Dusk and Dawn finale, lessons; `Docs/Mockups/reaper-faction.html`): Bound Wraiths (`PairBound` in `SpawnEnemyAt`, `Enemy.LinkId`, the orbit, tether and the channelled revive, `SyncBound`), the Scythe Wraith's reap (its mark and dash are the Lunger's with `Scythe*` numbers), Lamplighter (lines, then a cross) and Mourner (twin spiral, then a rest); reskins Wisp (Swarmer), Acolyte (`IsSwordsman`, `Sickle*` numbers), Gravedigger (`IsBrute`), Shade (the old Phantom blink, `LateGame.cs`); every member drawn in its sworn side's colour (`CultTint`); test `Tools/SimTest/Tests/ReaperCult.cs` (`run.sh 1 1 reapercult`)
- `QuarryFaction.cs` (Core), `Game/QuarryLook.cs`: the Colossus's faction, The Quarry (Docs/Approaches.md "The Quarry", `Docs/Mockups/colossus-faction.html`): Rubble (Rioter), Quarryman (Guard), Stomper (Thug) on brains (Docs/EnemyBehaviours.md; `QuarryKin.Is`); the Stomper's small stomp ring (`Enemy.Stomp`, a Colossus `StompRing`: `StomperSpecial`, `UpdateStompRings`, `StomperPush`, `SyncStomper`; `HurtByRing`/`PushOutOfRing` shared with Colossus.cs); step 2: Slab Bearer (Riot Shield kin, `DropSlab` → `PatchKind.Slab` cover) and Rock Hurler (Gas Grenadier kin, `HurlerLead`, `PatchKind.Rubble` pile, landing in `TickQuarryHost`), cover stops shots in `QuarryCoverStops` (Combat.cs); `QUARRY2=1` preview; step 3: Driller (Shiv kin, its leap's crack), Geomancer (Taser Guard kin, `GeomancerCrack` sends a crack), Cinder Priest (Taser Guard kin, `Defs.MagmaBolt`, lava where it stops; loose lava ticks in `TickQuarryHost`), cracks are `Enemy.Crack` (`TickCrack`, `SyncCrack`, drawn with ColossusLook's `DrawCrack`); `QUARRY3=1` preview; step 4: the Avalanche finale and the Colossus's `Lessons` (Approaches.cs), the Riot line's wall is any Riot Shield kin (`IsWallKin`, WardenFaction.cs); looks draw the rock, hard hat and lifting foot, rings via `DrawStompRing`; preview `QUARRY=<s> Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud`; test suite `quarry`
- `LarderFaction.cs` (Core), `Game/LarderLook.cs`: the Devourer's faction, The Larder (Docs/Approaches.md "The Larder", `Docs/Mockups/devourer-faction.html`), step 1: Gnawling (Rioter), Butcher (Guard), Gulper (Thug) on brains (Docs/EnemyBehaviours.md; `LarderKin.Is`); `Enemy.LarderStage`/`LarderT` (`SyncLarder`), `LarderSpecial` (host): the Butcher's wind-up and `Chop` (a half-circle), the Gulper's swell and breath (`GulpBit` at its mouth; the pull is `GulpPullAt`, from `DevourPull`); `LarderPlanted`/`LarderFaceLocked` in EnemyAI; step 2: Carcass Bearer (Riot Shield kin, `DropCarcass` drops a `Morsel`), Bile Spitter (Gas Grenadier kin, leads like the Rock Hurler, a `PatchKind.Bile` pool burning in `TickLarderHost`), Gnawlings go for morsels (`GnawlingTarget`) and eat them (`GnawlingEat`, `GnawFed`); with no Devourer, `TickLarderHost` also runs `StepOnMorsels`; step 3: Gutworm (`GutwormSpecial`: dive, chase, mark, erupt; `GutwormUnder` blocks hits, contact and drawing), Hook Thrower (the hook's code, `IsHook` in NonShooters.cs, with `HookThrow*` numbers and damage), Maw Priest (Taser Guard kin; `PriestFace`, `MawFan` fan, `LarderBlocks`/`PriestHit` heal from the front, ×1.25 behind); looks: `DrawBrood`, `DrawButcher`, `DrawCarcassBearer`, `DrawBileSpitter`, `DrawBileLobFx` (from GasLobLook.cs), `DrawGutworm`, `DrawHookThrower`, `DrawMawPriest`, the tells in `DrawLarderFloor` (ripple and mark, the maw); step 4: the Feast finale (a Riot line of Carcass Bearers) and the Long Campaign's ten `Lessons` (Approaches.cs); preview `LARDER=<s> [LARDER2=1|LARDER3=1] Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud`; test suite `larder`
- `CourtFaction.cs` (Core), `Game/CourtLook.cs`: the Duelist's faction, the Masked Court (`Docs/Mockups/duelist-faction.html`): Fencer (Shiv kin, a short hop: `Fencer*` numbers in the Lunger's case, EnemyAI.cs), Blade Dancer (Guard kin; its lunge and blades are the Duelist's: a `DuelState` with `DuelDash.Dancer`, `BladeDancerSpecial`, `TickDuelDash`/`TickDuelBlades`/`SyncDuel` in Duelist.cs, drawn by `DrawDuelFloor`), Parrier (Riot Shield kin; `ParrierBounce` from Collide sends front hits back, fired in `UpdateCourt`); step 2: Second (Security Guard kin, a fan of `Defs.ThrownDagger`, `SecondFanFire`), Marshal (Gas Grenadier kin, a `PatchKind.DuelCircle` round you: `KeepInMarshalCircles` from your move in Game.cs, the edge hurts in `UpdateCourt`, drawn by `DrawDuelCirclePatch`), Gallant (Taser Guard kin that never fires: `GallantSpecial` salutes and lunges with the Duelist's `DuelDash.Salute`; a perfect parry staggers it, `DuelOpen`); step 3: Harlequin (Thug kin, `HarlequinSpecial`: two `DuelDash.Cross` dashes with a vault between, `Enemy.CrossAt`), Mummer (Shiv kin, `MummerSpecial`: blinks into three with two `EnemyType.MummerCopy`, circles you, solid vs hollow shots; `EndMasquerade`, `MummerDied` from Combat.cs); step 4: the Ring of Honour (`SquadForm.Honour`: `HonourSpawn`, `HonourMove` sends opposite Fencer pairs in by turn, `HonourHolds` in Variety.cs `LungerWaits`) and the Duelist's `Lessons` (Approaches.cs); `CourtKin.Is`; previews `COURT=0.4 ZOOM=1.6 Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud 1` (step 1), `COURT2=1` with it (step 2), `COURT3=1` (step 3), `COURT4=1` (the Ring of Honour); test suite `court`
- Bosses by Campaign wave (shown; track wave in brackets): `Warden.cs` 10 (5), `Queen.cs` 20 (10), `Reapers.cs` 30 (15),
  `Singularity.cs` 40 (20), `Colossus.cs` 50 (25), `Duelist.cs` 60 (30), `Lich.cs` 70 (35), `Herald.cs` 80 (40),
  `Devourer.cs` 90 (45), `Eclipse.cs` 100 (50); `LateGame.cs` is the shared
  late-boss logic
- `BossFight.cs`: the base of every boss's state (`WardenState`, `QueenState`, ..., `ReaperFight`): `BossFight<TStage, TTurn>` (Stage, TransTo, Turn, TurnT, TransT, Step, Fired, TurnLen, ActT; `NextTurn` steps the phase's turn list), `Enemy.Boss` (the per-boss properties `e.Warden`, `e.Queen`, ... read it), `Game.BossOf<T>` (made when first asked for); a new boss derives its state from it
- `BossStages.cs`: **every boss's stages in one table** (Docs/DifficultyTiers.md step 2): `BossStage` rows (stage, health share it starts at, `StageEntry` Timed/Instant/Stagger, the mechanics tiers it's in), `Game.StagesFor(boss, tier)`/`StagesOf`, `StageIndex`, `StageAt`, `NextStage` (each boss's tick asks it, then checks its own conditions); Easy has no last stands; the Warden's Cell Blocks bar and the Eclipse's "STAGE n / N" read it (`Game/HudBossBars.cs`); preview `MECH=Easy WARDENTRANS=2,-1 shot.sh x hud 3 1 1`; test suite `stages` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/BossStages.cs`)
- `BossRules.cs`: boss numbers by mechanics tier (Docs/DifficultyTiers.md step 3): `BossRules` (`RestPace`, `ExtraAdds`, `EscortMult`), `Game.TierBossRules`, `BossRulesNow`, `BossTurnLen` (every boss's Rest turns), `BossAdds` (each summon), `EscortMult` (`BossWaves.Queue`); test suite `bossrules` (in `Tools/SimTest/Tests/BossStages.cs`)
- `Targets.cs`: shared targeting helpers: `FirstAlive`, `AlivePlayersList`, `NearestPlayer`, `RandomLivingPlayer`,
  `BossTarget(e)` (its target while up, else the first player up), `NearestEnemy` (optionally filtered: `NearestEnemy(at, ok)`) and `NearestInLeash`, `Targetable(e)` (host: spawned in and alive) and `Hittable(p)` (up and out of hit grace)
- `FoeQuery.cs`: **what an attack hits** (Docs/Targeting.md): `FoesInCircle`, `FoesInBox`, `FoesInCone`, `AllFoes` (allocation-free struct queries over the Targetable enemies, optional `cover` = BlocksShot); every player attack loop uses them; test suite `foequery`
- `Pvp.cs`: **PvP in the Hangout** (Docs/Targeting.md step 3): `PvpOn` (always in Practice, the Hangout), `PvpDamage`, `StandOf` (a player's stand-in `Enemy`, `Enemy.Stand`), `PvpHurt` (HurtEnemy and DotDamage send a stand-in's hit here), `TryFoe`/`FoeExists` (an id that may be a stand-in's), `StandBase`, `TickPvp` (status left on a stand-in becomes the player's: stun, `Player.PvpSlowT`, knockback, damage over time), `PvpWallPush` (Blood Wall and Ossuary Wall keep other players off), `PvpHitMult` (marks and damage cards on PvP hits), `PvpStunImmune`; queries, `NearestEnemy`, `NearestInLeash` and `SnapFoes` take the attacker as `by`; test suite `pvp`
  `BossTarget(e)` (its target while up, else the first player up) and `Targetable(e)` (host: spawned in and alive)
- `BossLab.cs`, `BotBrain.cs`: Boss Lab fights (command line only: `run.sh 1 1 lab`, the balance grid, boss tests), the "smart" bot (the Warden's gates: `ReadWardenGates` picks the next gate's door and walks to it in time, `GateDanger` with `Warden.GateFrontIn`; test suite `botgate`)
- `BotParry.cs`: the smart bot's parry (`BotParry`, `WantParry`, `FirstHit`: the soonest shot or sword cut it can't dodge, timed for a perfect parry; `ParrySkill` per bot skill; `BotParries` counter); test suite `botweapon` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/BotWeapon.cs`)
- `BotDodge.cs`: the smart bot's Dodge (`BotDodge`, from `SmartBot`): evasive dodges (`ScoreDodges` in `ChooseMove` scores the 8 ways, hits counted after `DodgeInvuln`; a ready perfect parry goes first) and travel sprints (dodge held into `SprintT` when over `BotSprintFar` from its anchor); `DodgeSkillOf` per skill (nerve, gap, sprint gap); `BotDodges`/`BotSprints` counters; test suite `botdodge` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/BotDodge.cs`)
- `BotPicks.cs`: the bots' level-up picks (`BotPickUpgrade` from Game.cs `LocalControl`, `BotCardScore`: core stats by role (`CoreValue`), niche cards made for the kit, rarity, new abilities, diminishing copies); also used by the bot's shop (Shop.cs `BotShop`: heal, best card per gold, revive token, stock, done); suite `botpicks`
- `BotWeaponAlt.cs`: the smart bot's weapon Alt (`BotWeaponAlt` sets `PlayerInput.WeaponAction` from Game.cs `LocalControl`; `AltRule` runs the weapon's `WeaponDef.BotAlt` rule (a static `<Weapon>BotAlt` beside each weapon's code; the ones of weapons without a file of their own are here), taps via `Tap` with the bot's own cooldowns, held Alts marked `WeaponDef.BotAltHold`; `BotAltPresses` counter); suite `botweapon`
- `BotKatana.cs`: the smart bot's Katana (from the Katana's Alt rule): stores a stance one step from its finisher by switching away, waits a rolled moment (a crowd, a boss, a volley; never past the reset) to play both finishers back to back, plays one alone sometimes, and sometimes leaves a combo halfway for Hayate's dash-cuts (far target, volley) or Senbon's sweeps (a crowd hugging it); counters `BotKatanaPairs`/`Stores`/`Cancels`/`Finishers`; suite `botweapon` (`BotKatanaTest`)
- `PartyBots.cs`: bots that fill the lobby's open slots (extra sessions joined over localhost; stepped by `ArenaApp`; buttons in `LobbyUi.cs`; `Game.BotParty`: their run is unrecorded but not a `LabRun`, so the Campaign keeps the Expanding arena and lives; test `PartyBotCampaignTest`)
- Boss loot: `QueenLoot.cs`, `ReaperLoot.cs` (legendary weapons/abilities)

## Networking (Core)
- `Pings.cs`: pings (`PingKind`, `Session.SendPing`, host pause and cap, Focus snapping to an enemy; `Msg.PingMark`); a partial of `Session`
- `HangoutGame.cs`, `Game/HangoutUi.cs`: **Practice, the Hangout mode** (Docs/Hangout.md): `GameMode.Hangout` (hidden; `Game.HangoutOn` is a flag on `Msg.Start`), the real match pinned between waves behind the lobby (`Game.Practicing`; the screen stays the lobby, lobby requests check `Game.InRun`), `TickHangout` (`Intermission`, timer held, controls never held, no XP, no cooldowns, no dummies, `HangoutRespawn` beam respawns), `SyncPractice`/`Msg.PracticePlayer` (a loadout change swaps one player in place; a newcomer restarts the room), `Session.StartHangout` (`Msg.HangoutOpen`); the lobby window (`DrawPractice`, its input `PracticeInput`, the glitch swap `TickPracticeSwap`/`DrawPracticeSwap`; opened by `LobbyUi.DrawLobbyTabs`' Practice key); preview `PRACTICE=1` / `PRACTICEFX=<s>` `shot.sh x lobby`; test suite `hangoutmode`
- `HangoutSpawners.cs`, `Game/HangoutSpawnerLook.cs`: the Hangout's twelve enemy spawners round the edges (`Spawners`, `SpawnerChoices`: the regular enemies; `UseSpawner`: F on/off, B or the wheel steps the enemy, `CameraZoom.cs`; `TickSpawners`: one every 3 s, 5 alive; `Msg.HangoutSpawner`/`HangoutSpawners`); the pad, ghost and tag (`DrawHangoutSpawners`, `DrawHangoutSpawnerTags`); preview `HANGOUTPADS=1 shot.sh x hud 1 1 1`
- `LobbyAvatars.cs`: the Hangout room's characters, their positions and `Msg.LobbyMove`/`LobbyPos`; a partial of `Session`
- `LobbyCombat.cs`: Hangout PvP: shots, health, knock-out, respawn, the Dash/Barrier/Burst heal abilities (`Msg.LobbyFire`/`LobbyShot`/`LobbyAbility`); a partial of `Session`
- `Defs.cs` (Role.Wildcard, `Defs.Weapon`/`Upgrades.FirstAbility` map a Wildcard's kit*3+choice picks to DPS gear, `MakeWild`/`WildSecond`), `Profile.WildcardUnlocked`, `Game/WildcardGearUi.cs` (the lobby's Gear cards and pick window), `Upgrades.WildUlt`/`UltKitOf` (a Wildcard's Ult pick is class * 4 + key: any class's DPS ultimate): the Wildcard role (`Docs/Wildcard.md`; test: `run.sh 1 20 wildcard`)
- `Game/WildcardGearUi.cs`: the Wildcard's lobby gear: four slot cards (`DrawWildSlots`: weapon, 1st, 2nd, ultimate) and the pick window over the right column (`DrawWildPickWindow`: one column of class labels, one open at a time, `FoldArrow`; hover plays the option in the preview via `wildHoverSlot`/`wildHoverVal` read by `DrawShowcase`, and fills the info box); preview `WILDCARD=1 [WILDSLOT=0-3] [WILDOPEN=<class>] shot.sh x lobby`
- `Dodge.cs`: the Dodge, everyone's tiny dash with invulnerability (Ctrl or a double tap of a movement key; `LocalDodge`, `Msg.Dodge`; key tracking in `Game/ArenaApp.cs`) and its hold-to-sprint (`PlayerInput.DodgeHeld`, `SprintT`/`SprintMult`, moved by your own game; replicated `Player.Sprint` for teammates' wakes; its wall kick (`WallKick`, from the push out of props and the walled arena edge in `Game.cs`: reflects the dash, refunds the cooldown once; `Msg.Dodge`'s free flag; `WallKicks`); wake `DrawSprintWake` in `Game/NinjaLooks.cs`; preview `SPRINT=<s>`)
- `Graze.cs`, `Game/GrazeLook.cs`, `Game/GrazeSoundBank.cs`: Graze, an enemy shot skimming past a player (within `GrazeReach` of the hit circle, moving away, not invulnerable) charges their ultimate by `GrazeUltCut` (`TryGraze` from the enemy-bullet loop in `Combat.cs`, once per shot: `Bullet.Grazed`; one per `GrazeGapTime`: `Player.GrazeGap`); `Msg.Graze` to all (`LandGraze`: `FxKind.Graze` streak, the owner's own ult cut, `Player.Grazes`, `Sfx.Graze`); the HUD's ult key flashes (`tileGraze`, `MiniGrazeFlash` in `Game/HudTiles.cs`); preview `GRAZE=<s> [TILEFX=graze,0.04] shot.sh x hud` (`Tools/Preview/ShotGraze.cs`); test `graze`
- `Session.cs`: host/join, lobby, **`Msg` enum**, **`ProtocolVersion`** (bump only when a message or snapshot layout changes; history in `Docs/ProtocolHistory.md`) and **`IdsHash`** (a fingerprint of every enum and card, sent with Hello, so new ids need no bump)
- `Net.cs`: `NetPeer`, length-prefixed TCP (`BytesIn`/`BytesOut` for the lag chart)
- `LagStats.cs`, `LagNet.cs`, `Game/LagChartUi.cs`: the lag chart (F5): `LagStats` (30 s strip of quarter-second columns: ping, snapshot gap, frame time, fps, KB/s; stalls, p95, 1% low, quality, marks, the copied summary, the CSV log), `Game.TickLag` (pings 4/s, `Msg.NetPings` with every player's ping, wave/boss/storm marks), the overlay and its Settings row; `Docs/LagChart.md`; test `run.sh 1 1 lag`; preview `LAG=pill|chart|guest Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud 6 12.2 3`
- `GameNet.cs`, `NetSync.cs`: all snapshot and input serialisation (one ref pass for read and write)
- `RunTimeline.cs`: Records > Recent runs data: `RunTimeline` (a sample of every player's running totals and HP% every 10 s of play: `Rates`, `Series`, `AccuracyPerWave`, text save/load), `RunTimelineStore` (`Profile.Folder/runs/<id>.run`, newest `Profile.RecentKept` kept), and the `Game` sampler (`TickRunTimeline` from `FixedStep`, `FinishTimeline` from the run's End record in `RunSaves.cs`). `RankLog` (in `Profile.cs`) carries the run's class, mode, time, damage and the file name

## Saving, stats and text data (Core)
- `OldNames.cs`: old code names (renamed to match the game's names, Docs/Glossary.md) mapped to the current ones, so older profiles, run saves and wave files load
- `Diag.cs`: `Diag.Warn`, where a file, save or sound failure the game recovers from is reported (once per message; ArenaApp sends it to Player.log)
- `SaveData.cs`, `RunSaves.cs`, `Profile.cs`: saved runs (every arena; the Expanding map via `ExpansionGame.cs` `SaveExpansion`/`ResumeExpansion`), profile, records, unlocks
- `Core/Pacman.cs`: the Pac-Man maze game's simulation (maze, chompers, role ghosts, waves, lives, levels; no Unity; test suite `pacman`; `Docs/Pacman.md`)
- `PacmanUi.cs`: the Pac-Man game screen for a Pac profile (Play opens it): maze, dots, chomper, role ghosts, HUD, keys (`Docs/Pacman.md`)
- `PacmanLook.cs`: the Pac-Man skin for Pac profiles (chomper, role ghosts, dots) and the Pac-Man disk (`Docs/Pacman.md`)
- `ProfileStore.cs`, `ProfilesUi.cs`, `DiskCarousel.cs`: up to 50 profiles (a folder each under `profiles/`; `ProfilesUi.cs`: the screen's state, choosing, naming, renaming and each profile's floppy disk art with its easter-egg looks, names in Permanent Marker `Ui.HandFont`) and the "Who's playing?" screen before the menu (`DiskCarousel.cs`, `Docs/Mockups/whos-playing-carousel.html`): the monitor with its drive, an endless side-scrolling carousel of big disks (`whoCarPos`, `CarRect`, arrows, keys, wheel, drag), PROFILE n OF m, Manage profiles (RENAME / DELETE chips) and New profile (a blank disk named as you type); `DiskLookFor` gives a name its easter-egg disk
- `ProfileTransition.cs`, `DriveSlot.cs`: the disk boot sequence from Who's playing? to the menu (`Docs/Mockups/disk-boot-sequence.html`, 4.8 s: pick, carry, insert through the slit, read, retype; a skip fast-forwards it in 0.5 s; the menu's case then drops in, MenuMachine.cs), its cues; `DriveSlot.cs` is the floppy drive in the monitor's chin (states `DriveAt`, `DriveLoaded`, the lip drawn over a disk)
- `WhoBoot.cs`: Who's playing? booting up as the game opens (`Docs/Mockups/whos-playing-load-in.html` C, 3.45 s: fade from black, glass power-on, boot lines while the drive reads, flash and title, disks rise, keys rise; any key skips; `WhoBootDiskRise`, `WhoBootRise`)
- `DiskEject.cs`: the eject key beside the monitor's drive (`DrawDriveEject`) and the 2.7 s way back to Who's playing? (the case powers down and lifts away, the disk springs out of the slit and drops back onto the carousel: `StartProfEject`, `TickProfEj`, `DrawProfEj`, `DriveEjectAt`); rides on `profTr` with `profEj`
- `EnemyVariants.cs`: enemy variants that spawn in place of the regular one (same type and brain; `Enemy.Variant`, replicated): `VariantTable` (base, chance, speed and health), `RollVariant` from `NewEnemy` (an id hash, no rng); the Rioter's Ball and Chain (30%, 0.75x speed, drawn in `WardenLook.cs`); test suite `variants`
- `CoreDrops.cs`: the glowing Core Carrier enemy (`EliteTrait.CoreCarrier`, rolled in `SpawnEnemyAt`, drawn in `VarietyLook.cs`) and `DropCores`; the run's `CoresFound` rides the End record (`RunSaves.cs` `PayCores`)
- `Variants.cs`: weapon variants (`Docs/Variants.md`): `Tiers` (2) and `Cost(owned)` in Cores, `RunCores`/`HuntCores` payouts; Cores and bought tiers live on `Profile`, the lobby's Variants window is `LobbyUi.DrawAscensionWindow`
- `Expertise.cs`: class expertise (0-10, the run's XP also goes to the class played: `Profile.AwardXp(..., kit)`, `Profile.ClassXp`/`ExpertiseLevel`) and weapon skins (`WeaponSkinDef`, `Expertise.Skins`: the Rubber Chicken so far; one per weapon: `Profile.WeaponSkins`/`SkinOf`/`EquipSkin`); on the wire as (weapon, skin) pairs on `Msg.Cosmetics` and each lobby entry (`Expertise.Pack`/`Clean`/`SkinIn`, `LobbyEntry.Skins`), read in the run with `Game.SkinOf`; the host swaps the Twin Blades' cues for the chicken's (`Game.TwinSfx`); tests in suite `rank`
- `Rank.cs`: the cosmetic account rank: XP curve, tiers, XP for a run (`Rank.Award`), the cosmetics table (frames, trails, a background); XP, equipped cosmetics and recent runs live on `Profile`, the lobby carries each player's `Rank`/`Frame`/`Trail` (`Msg.Cosmetics`, `Session.ShareCosmetics`)
- `RunStats.cs`: per-player run stats for the end screen
- `Scratch.cs`: `ListPool<T>` and `Game.SnapEnemies`/`SnapKeys`/`Free`: reusable lists for "copy the enemies, then change them while walking" so ticks don't allocate (rent, walk, return); tests `Tools/SimTest/Tests/Scratch.cs` (suite `scratch`). `V2.CloserThan`/`WithinReach`/`FartherThan`/`FartherOrAt` (`MathTypes.cs`): distance tests without the square root, use them instead of `DistanceTo(x) < r`
- `WeaponDps.Generated.cs`: generated weapon DPS table, don't edit
- `Docs/Reference.md`: generated weapon, ability, enemy, role and class tables (health, speed, damage, cooldowns, DPS, text) from the definitions; don't edit. `Tools/SimTest/Tests/RefDoc.cs` builds it (`run.sh 1 1 refdocwrite` rewrites it; suite `refdoc` fails when stale)
- `Game/DodgeSoundBank.cs`: the Dodge's Cloth Swish (`Sfx.DodgeSwish`, raised in `Dodge.cs`, own screen only; `Docs/Mockups/dodge-sounds.html`)
- `Game/FootstepSoundBank.cs`, `Game/Footsteps.cs`: players' footsteps, the Servo Tap (`SoundBank.PlayFootstep`, not an Sfx: local only, never sent); `TickFootsteps` reads each player's speed from their drawn position, steps every 40 + 0.08 × speed px, louder and brighter with speed, allies quieter with distance (`Docs/Mockups/footstep-sounds.html`)
- `BossSounds.cs`: audio cues for boss attacks
- `Game/NinjaSoundBank.cs`: the Ninja DPS's sounds (Dragon Fang Kunai whoosh `ShotKunai` via the weapon def's `Sound`, `NinjaBlink`, `NinjaStepCast`/`NinjaStepStrike`, `NinjaVanish`, `NinjaFanOpen`/`NinjaFanBurst`, `NinjaLotusCast`, the Great Star's `GreatStarThrow`/`GreatStarCatch` raised in `GreatStar.cs`), raised in `Ninja.cs`; the Shinobi set of `Docs/Mockups/ninja-dps-sounds.html`
- `Game/TreantSoundBank.cs`, `Game/SeedLook.cs`: the Treant Rod's sounds (`SwingRoot` leaf rustle on the lash, `ShotSeed` Pod Pop, `ShotSeedHit` Tick; `Docs/Mockups/treant-rod-sounds-seeds.html`) and the Treant's seed as a Thorn Dart (`Defs.TreantSeed`, fired by `TickTreantSeeds` in `Core/KitWeapons.cs`)
- `Game/FireballSoundBank.cs`: the Fireball's sounds, the Deep Roar of `Docs/Mockups/fireball-sounds.html` (`Sfx.ShotFireball` the launch, the weapon def's `Sound`; `Sfx.ShotFireballBurn` raised in `Combat.cs` when a fireball ignites an enemy); render with `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh ShotFireball`
- `Game/KatanaSoundBank.cs`: the Katana's sounds, the Steel set of `Docs/Mockups/katana-sounds.html` (`KatanaSwipe1/2` sweeps and spins, `KatanaLunge` dashes, `KatanaHit` on the Weapons volume; `KatanaStrikeCut` Senbon's slashes, `KatanaStanceSwap` the switch, `KatanaBurstSfx`, `KatanaCaltrops`, `KatanaFling`), raised in `Katana.cs` and `Knight.cs` (`Swing.Sound`, `KatanaHitCue`)
- `Game/LightSoundBank.cs`: the Lightbringer's sounds, built like the Twin Blades' bank (a major-key `Chime`, `Sparkle`, `Pad`): `LightCut1/2` (Dawn and Mercy), `LightNoon`/`LightRadiant` (sent with the step's effect at the click: the swell up to the blow at 0.35 s, then the struck chord), `LightHit`, `LightHeal`/`LightWard` (a crescent reaching an ally, `Combat.cs` `HealAllyHit`), `LanceGather`/`LanceRelease` (the Sunlance), `LanceFizzle` (your own key with too little light); raised in `Core/Lightbringer.cs`; render them with `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh Light` and `Lance`
- `MeleeSounds.cs`, `Game/MeleeSoundBank.cs`: the melee rushers' cues (made from replicated state on every machine, never sent), `QuietAt` (sounds fade with distance, enemy shots too), deaths that merge when a kind dies together
- `Game/RioterSoundBank.cs`: the Rioter's sounds, the Mob voice (every yell two or three rough, detuned voices: `Shout`, `Mob`, kept dark by `Dark`): `Sfx.RiotYell` (within earshot), `RiotAttack`, `RiotHup` (the bottle's wind-up), `RiotWhoosh` (the throw, sent by `RiotBrains.cs`), `RiotShatter` (`UpdateBottleSounds`), `RiotDie`, `RiotStep`; played from `Core/MeleeSounds.cs`; render `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh Riot`

## World rendering (Game)
- `OrbLook.cs`: health orbs as green discs with a white cross like the Medkit's (`DrawPlainOrb`), the Hemomancer's crimson blood orb (`DrawBloodOrb`, preview `BLOODORB=1`) and the pickup burst (`DataBurst`: sparks, a ring, a rising plus)
- `WorldRenderer.cs`: GL drawing of the arena (floor, layers, `DrawEnemy`/`DrawPlayer` entry, GL primitives; `DrawArenaOver`: the layer over the contents (Attack Helicopter, Deployable Turret, Warhorn Volley, Dragon's Dogma), also drawn by the Practice window and the loadout preview)
- `FloorGridLook.cs`: the arena floor grid, the "Pressure grid" (50 px lines anchored to the world, `RoomTraps.Tile`: spike plates and slow fields sit on whole squares of it (`RoomTraps.SnapPlate`/`SnapCentre`, Dev Kit traps too), preview `GRIDTRAPS=snap|loose`; bends round players, takes their colour near them, ripples from kills, a small burst in your colour when you Dodge (`Game.Dodges`) (`FloorDashBurst`, preview `DASHBURST=<s>`); Settings > Visual effects > Arena floor: Bending / Static, pref `floor_bend`; preview `FLOORBEND=0`)
- `FloorStylesLook.cs`: the arena floor's other patterns (Settings > Visual effects > Arena floor, pref `floor_style`, keys `FloorStyleChoices`): Flux (needles), Seismo (scan lines), Plates (flipping plates), Circuit (seeded traces per area, `CircuitFor`, pulses from kills, dodges and footsteps); drawn from `DrawFloorGrid` by `DrawFloorPattern`; preview `FLOORSTYLE=<1-4>` (with `FLOORBEND=0`, `DASHBURST=<s>`)
- `WorldPlayers.cs`, `WorldEnemies.cs`, `WorldShots.cs`, `WorldEffects.cs`: players, enemies/zones/minions, shots, effects
- `RedShotLook.cs`: a boss's red aimed shot (`Defs.PierceShot`, 30 damage, unblockable): a barbed black-cored spearhead with a red streak (`DrawRedShot`); its ring glow is in `ShotLooks.cs`; preview `REDSHOT=1 ZOOM=2.2 shot.sh x hud`
- `CharacterModels.cs`: the 18 player models (role build + class costume) and the held-weapon models (`DrawHeld<Weapon>`, registered in `WeaponLooks.cs`); `LookDrawsArms`: the weapons whose own look draws the arms (the model leaves its arms out); `LookDrawsPauldrons`: those whose arms have their own pauldrons (the model leaves its shoulder plates out)
- `MartyrLook.cs`: the Healer Hemomancer's Martyr (look A, Offering, of `Docs/Mockups/martyr.html`): chest cross, bead string, rise burst and the ward halo (12 beads from `Player.WardT`); `FxKind.MartyrCast`
- `ClassLooks.cs`, `AbilityLooks.cs`, `HemomancerLook.cs`: per-class ability/buff overlays on the model, ability feedback, Hemomancer visuals
- `WeaponLooks.cs`: one `WeaponLook` per `WeaponId` (`LookOf`): the held model (`Held`), the grip (`Hold`, `HoldOffX`), whether its look draws the arms and pauldrons (`Arms`, `Pauldrons`), a melee weapon's energy colour, resting pose and model (`MeleeColor`, `Rest`, `Model` for `DrawMeleeModel`), the Soldier guns' effects (`DpsShot`/`DpsTick`/`DpsPose`, `TankShot`/`TankPose`); a new weapon adds its `L` line
- `RepulseLook.cs`: the Repulsion Cannon's blast (muzzle star, smoke, ring, sparks, a small kick; Visual effects on), the Undertow's current (`DrawRepelPull`) and meter arc (`DrawRepelMeter`); mockup `Docs/Mockups/repulsion-pull.html` (option B); its sounds (`Sfx.SpellPullStart/Hum/Stop`) are recipes in `SoundBank.cs`, listen page `Docs/Mockups/undertow-sounds.html`
- `RootLook.cs`: the Treant Rod's root lash and the root tether to its Treant (`DrawRoot`, `DrawRootLashFx`, `DrawRootCollar`: the lash swells out of a knot and rootlets at your feet; `Docs/Mockups/treant-root-lash-follow.html`, C)
- `LumenWardLook.cs`: Lumen Ward's cone of light (look A, Cathedral, of `Docs/Mockups/sanctify-cone.html`: bands, god rays, dust; your own drawn from your live aim)
- `DeathLotusLook.cs`: the Ninja's Death Lotus (Crimson Reaping: dark arena, three glowing cuts and a lotus stain per enemy struck, camera kick; `FxKind.DeathLotus`)
- `LifelineLook.cs`: the Healer Soldier's Lifeline (heartbeat pulse, halo and column of light on each player it reached; `FxKind.Lifeline`)
- `ElementalistLooks.cs`: Meteor (falling rock, impact), Blizzard (a soft-edged lane of whiteout) and Tsunami (crest, wet drips) looks
- `NimbusLook.cs`, `ChalkLook.cs`: the Nimbus's Anvil cloud, rain curtains, forked bolt and scorch, and the Eye of the Storm hurricane (blade arms, `StormBlade`); Spirit Chalk's ink, sealed loops and the ring on penned enemies
- `ShotLooks.cs`, `Juice.cs`, `Ambience.cs`: weapon shot looks, screen shake/particles/glow, weather and trails
- `JuiceMoments.cs` (Game): kill hit-pause, multikill calls (`DrawMultiKill`, `multiStyle`), the wave's last kill, boss-death slow motion (`BossSlowSpeed` in `EndSpeed`), level shock, low-HP heartbeat, lasting floor marks; preview `MOMENT=multi|clear|level|lowhp|marks` (`Tools/Preview/ShotJuice.cs`)
- `CameraZoom.cs`: scroll-wheel zoom (1x to 2x on you, focus held inside the arena; Settings → Gameplay), `ZoomPoint`/`UnzoomPoint` used by `Px`, `ToUi` and `MouseArena`, `UiWorldScale` for HUD sizes around world things
- `CameraRotate.cs`: camera rotation (Rotate camera left/right keys, `[` `]`, swing to the next quarter turn; `RotateView`/`UnrotateView` inside `ZoomPoint`/`UnzoomPoint`, `LeanTurn` turns the lean, `HoldInside` for the far axis); preview `CAMROT=0-3 ZOOM=1.4 shot.sh x hud`
- `FirstPerson.cs`: the first-person view (F8, PlayerPrefs `first_person`): `FpPx` behind `Px` (the floor through your eyes; `FpStand`/`FpStandShot`/`FpStandPlayer`/`FpFloor` round bodies in `DrawArenaContents` stand them up as cards), `FpVtx` behind `Vtx` (triangles collected, `FpFlush` draws them far to near, cut at the near plane), `FpWalls` (the layout's solids raised), `FpProp` (props as boxes and drums, hooked in `PropsLook.cs DrawProps`), `FpMove`/`FpAimPoint` (input), `FpSensSetting`/`SetFpSens`/`FpSensSlider` (Settings → Gameplay → First-person mouse, in `MenuDrawer.cs` and `BaySettings.cs`), `FpToUi` (labels); preview `FPV=<yaw>[,<pitch>]`
- `FirstPersonWeapon.cs`: the first-person view's weapon (`FpDrawWeapon`, from `FpEndWorld`): a gun, sword or staff (`FpVmShapeOf`) built from shaded boxes in camera space (`FpVmBox`/`FpVmPoint`), with walk bob, idle sway and a kick per fire interval while Fire is held (recoil and muzzle flash, alternating swings, a thrust); preview `FPVWEAPON=<WeaponId> FPVKICK=<1-0>[,<swings>]` with `FPV=`
- `OffscreenMarkers.cs`: while zoomed, edge arrows to off-screen teammates (colour + name, red when downed) and enemies (nearest 40; in Survival, enemies in the same direction merge into one arrow with a count: `MarkBins`, preview `SURVMARKS=45`; bosses always) and unopened treasure chests (gold) and unclaimed Stash caches (teal, Docs/BreachRooms.md), drawn in `DrawHud`
- `MoveFeel.cs`: movement feel, local only: each player's body stretched and leaned along their movement and squashed on stops and dash landings (a spring; `BeginBodyFeel`/`BodyFeelPoint`, applied in `Px`), footstep, dash and skid dust, a dash's fading copies (`DrawMoveGhosts`), your dash's camera kick (into `lean`); `TickMoveFeel` from `UpdateJuice`; walking's own weight is `WalkStartTime`/`WalkStopTime` (Core `Balance.cs`, `Game.cs`); preview `MOVEFEEL=walk|stop|dash [MFOFF=1] ZOOM=2`
- `CameraLean.cs`: the camera lean (view eases toward your aim and movement, 50% of 5% of the arena; Settings → Gameplay), `ToUi` for labels placed from a world position
- `MeleeLooks.cs`: melee attacks with Visual effects on: the weapon model swinging through a glowing arc (style E); the held poses (`MeleeRest`; the Kusarigama and Blood Lash held with arms, `DrawMeleeHeldIdle`), each swing's return into them (`DrawSwingModel`) and the hand-over (`MeleeSwingShowing`)
- `BearLooks.cs`: the Bear Totem: a claw on each hand, claw trails, the Bear companion, claw scars on bleeding enemies
- `Swordsman.cs`, `Game/SwordsmanLook.cs`: the sword cut, today the Twin Reapers' Acolyte's sickle (a non-shooter): close in, wind up (cone fills red), cut the cone (a band from the hand to its reach), recover; drawn from the age of the phase (`Enemy.SeenT`)
- `NonShooterLooks.cs`: the hook's aim line, cable and hook (look B of `Docs/Mockups/non-shooter-enemies.html`), and the dispatch to the Acolyte's and the Turnkey's looks
- `EnemyPresenceLook.cs`: regular enemies' soft shadow and quiet colour glow with Visual effects on (look D of `Docs/Mockups/enemy-prominence.html`) (the Warden's faction skips it: OfficerGlowLook.cs)
- `OfficerGlowLook.cs`: the Warden's faction glowing with Visual effects on (`DrawOfficerGlow`, the glow pass): a breathing halo in their colour (`HasWardenHalo`: officers, Rioter, Shiv, Turnkey; the Thug's red, growing with `BruteRage`); the officers' the kit lit (baton tip and badge, shield face, mask lenses and canister, taser prongs, pistol light) and a radio LED; preview `WARDENCREW=1|2 NINJA=0 ZOOM=3.2`
- `WallSpillLook.cs`: the walls casting light with Visual effects on (`DrawWallSpill`, the glow pass): the walls' colour washed across the floor on each wall box's open side (`SpillSides`, cached per wall shape) and round each pillar, rising with the walls; preview `shot.sh x hud 12`
- `LightingLook.cs`, `Core/LightCast.cs`: **lighting** (Settings > Visual effects > Lighting: `lightsOn`, Hard/Soft `lightsSoft`, Floor dim `floorDim`; Visual effects on): shots (merged per 56 px cell), muzzle flashes (`NoteMuzzleLight`, from Juice.cs), blasts, lightning, Railgun beams, bursts (`EffectLight`) and fire light the floor (`DrawLighting`, from DrawWorld under the contents, after `DimFloor`) with a faint haze over the bodies (`DrawLightHaze`, glow pass); limits: `MaxLights`, `LightCasters` per light (nearest, walls first), `LightRayBudget`, the crowd step-back (`LightCrowdFrom`/`Full`); Soft = 3 casts from a small source for the brightest `SoftLights`; shadows from walls, pillars, crates, chests, barrels, turrets, players and enemies (`ShadowScene`, only what's in reach; `LightCast.Cast` = a light's `LightFan`, `Lit`/`Reach`); preview `LIGHTING=1|3 [LIGHTS=0] [LIGHTSOFT=0|1] [FLOORDIM=0-2] shot.sh x hud 3 1 1`, cost `LIGHTPERF=<enemies>,<shots>[,<blasts>]` (`Tools/Preview/ShotLighting.cs`); suite `lighting` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/Lighting.cs`)
- `RadiantOrbLooks.cs`: Radiant Orb's stardust trail with Visual effects on (style B): motes and sparkles in the glow pass
- `LeapSlamLook.cs` (Game): the Knight's Leap Slam with Visual effects on (look C, Bulwark: the leap swells and lifts the knight, a closing hex landing marker, hex pressure rings, plates and sparks, a hex rune, a hex cage with a draining ring on each stunned enemy; `FxKind.LeapSlam`)
- `ShadowStepLook.cs` (Game): the Ninja's Shadow Step with Visual effects on (smoke bursts, splits round the enemy's sides behind two eyes, re-forms behind it; `FxKind.ShadowStep`, hides the body while it travels)
- `AirTrailLooks.cs`: the air trail with Visual effects on (look A, Ribbons): two fading streamlines behind any player, enemy or minion, scaled by drawn speed (none under 110 px/s, full at 330)
- `GunBowLooks.cs`: the Healer's Revolver (kick, flash, smoke, brass) and Mender's Bow (snapping string, ring of light; the held bow is in `BowFocusLook.cs`) with Visual effects on
- `TankGunLooks.cs`: the Tank Soldier's Scattergun (recoil, pump, shell), Flak Cannon (recoil, heat, lifted shell, airburst) and Riot Repeater (bolt kick, brass, glowing rounds) with Visual effects on
- `LaunchBlur.cs`: a short directional blur of the world (`ScreenBlur`): the frame's contents (from `BlurFromHere`, recorded by Juice.cs's hit-pause recorder) drawn again as two faint copies along a direction, fading; off with Screen shake off or Reduced effects; used by your own Twin Rifle grenade launch (`NadeLaunchFeel`); preview `NADET=0.02`
- `TwinNadeLook.cs`: the Twin Rifle grenade tubes' Rocket assist with Visual effects on: launch (your own: a slight shake and the blur, `NadeLaunchFeel`; backblast, mouth flash and smoke ring, that gun's own kick, breech opening and the 40 mm casing), the rocket's flame, lead-in from the drawn tube and smoke trail, and a tube reloading (breech, shell sliding in, snap); preview `NADET=<s>`, `NADELOAD=<s>` (`Docs/Mockups/twin-rifle-grenade-launch.html`)
- `ChargeCameraFx.cs`: the charge camera, your own hold-and-release weapon (bow, Railgun, Warhammer, Golem Staff, Arc Lightning, Imp Caller, Gore Mortar; a `CamProfile` each, `CamProfileOf`): the charge eases the view out and back against your aim, the release snaps it forward past rest and settles, every release kicking by its power (`Game.ChargeReleases`/`ChargeLastWeapon`/`ChargeLastCharge`, the bow's Focus, Scatter via `ScatterLooses`); a Focus arrow's first hit holds a frame or two (`FocusHitHold`, from `BowFocusLook.cs`), as does a full Railgun beam's or Warhammer slam's (`ChargeHitCheck`, from `Juice.cs`'s struck enemies); through `Px`'s push and punch, undone for the cursor in `MouseArena` (`ChargeCamUnpunch`); preview `BOWFOCUS=draw|bar|focus|swing|scatter,<s> shot.sh x hud 1 1 1`, `RAILGUN=<s> shot.sh x hud`
- `TwinRifleFx.cs`: the Twin Rifle's firing feedback past the guns: the light pool at each muzzle, the first-frame streak from gun to round, brass that bounces and stays on the floor (`PBrass`, aged by `BrassStep`), the elbows folding (`TwinArmFold`) and the body jolting back (`TwinJolt`); your own volleys' screen kick, push + punch (`TwinScreenKick`, applied in `Juice.cs` `BeginJuiceFrame` and `Px`; `Docs/Mockups/twin-rifle-screen-kick.html`)
- `DpsGunLooks.cs`: the Soldier DPS's Twin Rifle (held out wide so each muzzle is where its stream starts, `TwinSpreadPose`; per-gun kick and climb, handle flick, side-port flash, brass, barrels heating from the tip, shimmer, smoke after a burst), Railgun (coils lighting with the charge, rails heating with the heat meter, discharge arcs, rail kick, steam) and Burst SMG (spinning barrel tubes that heat up, growing flash, brass, smoke) with Visual effects on; the spray cone is in `WorldRenderer.cs`
- `SoldierLooks.cs`: Soldier DPS ability looks with Visual effects on: the comet streak behind your own dashes (made for the old Dash), the grenades' flight ring and Frag Burst blast (the Twin Rifle's tubes, Airstrike's bombs), Mark for Death's reticle and skull (no ability marks enemies since Claymore took its place)
- `ChainLightLooks.cs`: Chain Light with Visual effects on: a soft fan of light, beams and halos (style A, Radiance)
- `MendPulseLook.cs`: the Healer Soldier's Mend Pulse with Visual effects on: a swelling fill, a double-beat ring and a heart on each ally reached (style B, Heartbeat)
- `TankLooks.cs`: the Tank Soldier's Roar (wave, enemy "!" pops, orbiting plates, draining rim, tethers) and Bulldoze (lane, afterimages, bow wave, hit sparks, landing stomp) with Visual effects on, timed from the cast's `FxKind.Callout`
- `SanctumLook.cs`: Sanctum's Bubble look (shimmering shell, pulse discs and sparkles, rim flare, flicker at the end); the old plain dome with Visual effects off
- `GolemWallLook.cs` (Game): the Summoner Tank's Golem Wall as the Basalt look (golems with no timer, ember veins and cracks that grow with wear, ground seam, rise dust, rubble when broken; `Docs/Mockups/golem-wall-rework.html`)
- `CloneWallLook.cs` (Game): the Ninja's Shadow Clone wall as the Afterimage Phalanx (dash-out afterimages, landing ring, energy fence, glitch hits, shatter; `Docs/Mockups/shadow-clone-wall.html`)
- `ShieldWallLook.cs`: the Knight's Shield Wall as seven steel plates that slam down, kick, crack and break one by one (`Docs/Mockups/shield-wall.html`).
- `SmokeBombLook.cs`: the Ninja's Smoke Bomb cloud, crack and seal ring, with Visual effects on (`Docs/Mockups/ninja-smoke-bomb.html`).
- `GlacialTombLook.cs`: the Glacial Tomb in look C (Crystal Reliquary) of `Docs/Mockups/glacial-tomb.html`: frost hex lattice, spire cages on frozen enemies, boss spears, sprouts where shots were erased
- `AeroaegisLook.cs`, `FrostNovaLook.cs`, `FlameWallLook.cs`: Aeroaegis's gust shell, Frost Nova's shatter and ice blocks, Flame Wall's ring of fire, with Visual effects on (`Docs/Mockups/aeroaegis.html`, `frost-nova-rework.html`, `flame-wall-ring.html`)
- `JuggernautLook.cs`: the Juggernaut Suit: six plates bolting on with sparks, the 1.3x body (`JuggSwell`, in `DrawPlayer`), plates coming off a sixth of the armour at a time, the segmented armour bar over the health bar (`DrawJuggBar`), the flak tracers (`DrawJuggShardFx`); a plain ring with Visual effects off
- `DomeLook.cs`: the Fortress dome, a ring of gold plates that flash as enemy shots reach it (look B of `Docs/Mockups/fortress-dome.html`)
- `LegionLook.cs`: Legion (Summoner DPS ultimate): the blood moon and red tint, drawn from `Player.LegionT`
- `BulletRainLook.cs`: Bullet Rain (Elementalist DPS ultimate, `ZoneKind.BulletRain`): Rain Barrage, look B of `Docs/Mockups/bullet-rain.html` in rain blues: the locked-on zone and tether (`DrawBulletRain`), rounds falling and landing over the enemies (`DrawBulletRainAir`) && a square zone (`Zone.Radius` = half-side), sounds in `BulletRainSoundBank.cs` (cast, patter, end; `UpdateBulletRainSound`)
- `BulletRainSoundBank.cs`: Bullet Rain's three cues (`Sfx.RainCast`, `RainPatter`, `RainEnd`), played from the zone's clock by `UpdateBulletRainSound`, nothing sent
- `NinjaLooks.cs`: Ninja DPS looks: Vanish's smoke (puffs, wisps, bank and ring on the vanish spot) and Blade Fan's circling kunai
- `BarrierLook.cs`: the Tank Soldier's Barrier with Visual effects on: eight armour plates that slam down, recoil on blocks and are knocked off as it soaks (style C, Bulwark Plates)
- `Shrines.cs`, `Game/ShrineLook.cs`: health shrines (owner Nov 20, Docs/Nodes.md: the only node placed, `PlaceFloatingRooms` in ExpansionGame.cs): `Shrines.Ring`/`Reach`/`OverFrac`/`Bit`, `TickShrines` (host, from `TickExpansion`: walk up once per player, `Player.ShrinesUsed`), `UseShrine` (full health + `Player.OverHp`, soaked after shields in `Combat.cs Damage`, lost in `DownIfDead`); look `DrawShrine` (plinth, posts, floating cross; grey once you've used it), `ShrineCross` (the ghost marker), `DrawOverHealth` (the gold ring), the HUD's gold +n (`HudModes.cs`, `Hud.cs`); preview `EXPANDMAP=0 ZOOM=2 EXPANDROOMS=2000,200,600,600 EXPANDNODE=shrine:2300,500 EXPANDAT=2400,600 CAMAT=2350,520 [SHRINEUSED=1] [OVERHP=85] shot.sh x hud 3`; suite `shrines` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/Shrines.cs`)
- `RoomChests.cs`: room chests (owner Nov 20): `Expansion.RoomChestChance` (30%), `RoomChestWeights` (rarity by `ChestKind`), `RollRoomChest`, `CellMiddles`; `Game.RollRoomChest` (host: a placed shape piece, from `HostPlayPieceCard`, or an enemies/empty mystery room, from `OpenMysteryDoor`; a chest node with its kind in `MapNode.Rule`, read by `Expansion.NodeChestKind(seed, node, cards)`; vendor rooms never); suite `shrines`
- `Relics.cs`: Thorns (`ThornsDmg`, reflected from the contact hit in EnemyAI.cs), faction relics (20 over the 10 bosses, `PerFaction`, `OfFaction`/`FactionOf`, `Apply` stat changes, `PlaceRelicNodes` each boss cycle, Docs/RelicNodes.md) and relics (`RelicId`, `Relics`; a slot per player, `Player.Relic`, replicated and saved): relic nodes taken in `TickRelicNodes` (from `TickNodes`), the relic a node rolls (`ForNode`) or holds (`Held`, `MapNode.Drop`), Backup (`UseBackup` from `DownIfDead`), Hallway Runner (`RelicSpeedAt`, `InHall`), Wall Bounce (`WallBounce` from `Collide`, the bounced course sent at once by `SendBounces` over `Msg.Steer`); the look (gem, HUD chip `DrawRelicChip`) is in `Game/NodeLook.cs`; preview `EXPANDNODE=relic:x,y RELIC=1`; tests in suite `expand`
- `NodeScouting.cs`: `TickNodeFog` only clears an old save's fogged node (`MapNode.Hidden`) at once; node fog, scout towers and gold veins are gone (Docs/Nodes.md); the look (`FogMark`, fixtures) is in `Game/NodeLook.cs`; preview `EXPANDNODE=chest:x,y:hidden`; tests in suite `expand`
- `Game/ChestLooks.cs`: the chest kinds' look with Visual effects on (`ChestKind` in Props.cs: Upgrade, Gold, Vector, Relic, Piece chest, Buildables, Gadget; ink, trim, emblem `ChestEmblem`, the open spill; `DrawChestLook` for chest props and the relic node; every chest's opening (props, relic and vector chest nodes) `DrawNodeOpening` in NodeLook.cs (`CHESTOPENT=<s>` for the chests preview), preview `RELICOPEN=<s>` or `VECOPEN=<s>` with `EXPANDMAP=0 ZOOM=2`); the node roll `Expansion.NodeChestKind`/`Game.NodeChestKind`, rewards in `OpenChest` (Props.cs: `DropGoldBurst`, `GiveChestBuild`); preview `CHESTLOOKS=1 ZOOM=2 CAMAT=640,470 [FXOFF=1] shot.sh x hud 3 1 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotChests.cs`); suite `chests` (`Tools/SimTest/Tests/ChestKinds.cs`)
- `Game/NodeLook.cs`: map nodes on the arena floor (Docs/Nodes.md): the ghost marker until a room is built round one, then the shop's pedestals (`ShopPedestalLook.cs`), Black Market pedestal or chest (a claimed chest node holds a real chest prop, `TickNodes`; lid up and dim once opened); the stall and pedestal also stand in an opened tiny shop or Black Market room (`DrawNodeFixture`); preview `EXPANDNODE=kind:x,y[:spent|hidden];... EXPANDROOMS=... EXPANDAT=x,y`
- `RelicGauntlet.cs`, `Game/RelicGauntletLook.cs`: the Relic Gauntlet (Docs/MysteryDoors.md, owner Nov 21; a mystery hallway kind, `Mystery.GauntletChance`): `RelicGauntlet` (`Is`/`Mark`, the room's pits, turret posts and laser `Beams` as a function of a clock, `RelicFor`), `Game.RelicGauntletClock` (the host's, sent in `Msg.ExpandState`), `TickRelicGauntlets` (host: a beam hurts); look = red beams with a white core, rail slots, the spinner hub; preview `GAUNTLET=<s> GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDMAP=0`; test in suite `mysterydoor`
- `Game/RelicLook.cs`: relic pedestal and artefacts (Docs/RelicNodes.md; the relic node's fixture): `DrawRelicPedestal` (plinth, beam, floating artefact), `RelicGlyph` (16 artefact shapes), `RelicFactions` (10 boss factions, 1-3 artefacts each), the preview sheet `RELICSHEET=0|1` (`EXPANDMAP=0 EXPANDAT=640,330 ZOOM=1.3`, mode hud)
- `Game/BuildMotion.cs`: the build screen's motion (owner Nov 19): the power-on open (`NoteBuildDrawn`, `BuildSlide`, `DrawBuildPrint`, `DrawBuildPowerOn`), the gliding and spinning ghost (`GhostOffset`, `GhostSpin`, `DashFrame`, `DrawGridDeny`), the card fly and hand reflow (`StartCardFly`, `NoteHand`, `DrawCardFly`), the door ripple, shockwave and +1 ROOM (`DrawDoorSpot`, `DrawPlaceRing`, `DrawPlaceLabel`), the rail slide and typed title (`RailSlide`, `TypedTitle`), the wallet roll (`WalletShown`, `DrawWalletAfter`), the breathing corners; preview `BUILDAGE=<s>` (seconds since open), `WALLETSPEND=n,s`
- `Game/BuildMapLook.cs`: the build window's map as the "Holo floor": each room's tile grid (`DrawHoloGrid`), the walls and fins (`World.WallsFor`) and cover plus the hub's floor plan as lit blocks (`DrawHoloSolids`), breaches (`DrawHoloBreaches`) and gates (`DrawHoloGates`), from `DrawExpansionDraw` (its map key lists them, `DrawBuildRail`); preview `EXPANDDRAW=0,0,0,0 EXPANDZOOM=z,x,y`, `EXPANDMIDWAVE=1` (mid-wave, breaches)
- `PropsLook.cs`, `ZombieLook.cs`, `VarietyLook.cs`, `ChallengeLook.cs`: props, zombies, wave 15+ enemies, challenge rooms
- `BossBeamLook.cs` (Game): the boss wave's beam-in drawing (pillar up from where a player stood in a room, a second one down onto a landing ring in the hub; `FxKind.BossBeam`, hides the body in between); logic in `Core/BossBeam.cs`; mockup `Docs/Mockups/boss-teleport.html`
- `GasLobLook.cs` (Game): (its officer body, `DrawGrenadierBody`, is in OfficerLook.cs; preview `SECSHIRT=4`) the Gas Grenadier's overhand lob (`FxKind.GasLob`): target ring that fills, dotted arc, the canister in flight with a shadow, and the grenadier's arm, canister lift, lens glow and lean (`DrawGrenadierLob`, called from `DrawGrenadier`); host side is `GrenadierWind`/`GrenadierFire` in `WardenFaction.cs`; preview `GASLOB=<s>`; `Docs/Mockups/gas-grenadier-throw.html` option A
- `OfficerLook.cs` (Game): the Warden's officers in the smooth Rioter style with arms holding each weapon: shared `OfficerTorso`, `OfficerCap`, `OfficerArm`, `GuardStick`; the Guard, Riot Shield (hands on the shield's grips), Gas Grenadier (canister in hand), Taser Guard (two-handed taser); the Security Guard's body is SecurityLook.cs; preview `WARDENCREW=1|2 NINJA=0 ZOOM=3.2`
- `TaserLook.cs` (Game): the Taser Guard's bolt and hit (its body and taser, `DrawTaserGuardBody`/`DrawTaserGuardDetails`, are in OfficerLook.cs: the officer in slate, hi-vis back band; preview `SECSHIRT=2`), its Fork Bolt (white zigzag glyph, 16 Hz flicker, fork, halo), its hit (`FxKind.TaserHit`) and the guard's taser and charge; numbers match `Docs/Mockups/taser-guard-stun.html` option B; preview `TASER=0.3 Tools/Preview/shot.sh taser hud 3`
- `PrisonLook.cs` (Game): the Thug (moves like a Brute; its own body: a hunched bruiser, tattooed arms, taped fists) and the Shiv (moves like a Lunger; Hooded or Twin Shanks by id); preview `RESKIN=1 Tools/Preview/shot.sh <name> hud 1 0.5 1`
- `BubbleLook.cs`: a Shielded elite's twelve-cell bubble (`DrawBubble`, cells flash and break from the `Enemy.Cells` mask; `Docs/Mockups/shield-tells.html`) and its shield bar (`DrawShieldBar`)
- `WardenBodyLook.cs` (Game): the Warden's body, the Chief Warden (`Docs/Mockups/warden-rework.html` option A): greatcoat, peaked cap, collar, epaulettes, keys; a pose per move from the replicated `WardenState` (`WardenPose`: shotgun rack and recoil, heel spin, radio, pointing the light, arm over his eyes, baton slam with a crack ring, overhand gas throw, both hands on the shield, charge wind-up with speed lines and skid dust, kneeling for the armor, braced last stand, dazed) and the 5 s phase changes' set pieces (`WardenShiftPose`: stagger, then radio/keys/baton slam into Lockdown, roar/point/turn the key/rack into the Riot, kneel/rise into the last stand; the `Keys` pose); preview `WARDENTRANS=<1-3>,<s> WARDENHP=<hp>[,<armor>]`, `WARDENSHEET=all|<1-15> NINJA=0 ZOOM=2 shot.sh x hud 3 1 1`
- `WardenIntro.cs` (Game): the Warden's entrance, Lockdown (`Docs/Mockups/warden-intro.html` option B): a push-bullet cell closes round it, padlock, silhouette lit, the cell blown open at `Game.WardenIntroTime` (the host holds it and its escort that long: `EnemyAI.SpawnEnemy`), then a scan band types its name; replaces the boss card (Juice.cs) and holds the HUD; preview `WARDENINTRO=<s> shot.sh x hud 5 3 2`
- `QueenBodyLook.cs` (Game): the Hive Queen's body, the Brood Mother (`Docs/Mockups/hive-queen-rework.html` option B): head, tiara and stubby wings dragging a see-through egg sac of eggs; faces her nearest player or her nectar spray (`QueenFacing`); her phase on the sac (wax lattice in the Honeycomb, red and boiling with torn wings and embers in the Frenzy, cracked, half-empty and leaking honey in the Last Stand); the egg swell before her Eggs turn and the laying squeeze (`Game.QueenSwell`, from her replicated turn step); the royal dive pose (wings spread, legs and sac tucked, facing her landing), the landing squash (`QueenSquash`) and its cracks, honey and dust on the floor (`DrawDiveLanding`); her eggs arcing from the sac's tip (`DrawEggFlights`, `queenTip`); previews `QUEENSTAGE=<0-3>`, `QUEENHOLD=swell,<0-1>|lay,<s>|dive,<s>` with `FLANKERS=1 ZOOM=2.6 CAMAT=800,215 shot.sh x hud 3 1 1`
- `HeraldBodyLook.cs` (Game): the Storm Herald's body, the Tempest Herald (option B of a Preview-rendered mockup): a hooded caller facing its target, a torn cloak streaming downwind in the Gale, two rod spires on its shoulders arcing to each other, a crackling staff, eyes in the cowl; blue, then dark violet from the Supercell with charge off the hem, blazing in the last stand (`DrawHeraldBody`); a pose per turn from the replicated turn and its clock (`HeraldPose`, `HeraldPoseOf`: Idle volley jab, Call with the sky's bolt, Plant and Conduct for the rods, Slam, Sweep upwind, Gather an orb, Downburst swing, Storm front, Mark the Conductor's player, Hunch while changing phase); preview `HERALDSHEET=poses[:<1-12>]` (ZOOM=2.2 CAMAT=herald), `HERALDSHEET=1 ZOOM=1.38 CAMAT=640,360 shot.sh x hud 3 1 1` (the four phases), `CAMAT=herald` centres a zoom on it
- `ReaperShiftLook.cs` (Game): the Twin Reapers' fusion and split, from `R.TransT` (`Game.FuseMerge`, `Game.SplitBurst`): the moon-and-sun sigil closing, afterimages, the column of gold light and the Dual Reaper unfurling (`DualBirth`); the shake, burning seam, floor cracks, the chain snapping into flying links; preview `REAPERLOOK=fuse,<s>|split,<s> CAMAT=800,450 ZOOM=1.25 shot.sh x hud 6 1 1`
- `ReaperBodyLook.cs` (Game), `Tools/Preview/ShotReaperLook.cs`: the Twin Reapers' bodies, the Hooded Reapers (concept A of the look rework): torn cloak, pointed hood and eyes (red enraged, pale in Soul Bond), a pointed scythe posed by the reap (`DrawReaperScythe`), moon/sun sigil; the Dual Reaper's cloak, two hoods and pinwheel scythes; preview `REAPERLOOK=twilight|bond|dual|chase|sundered CAMAT=800,400 ZOOM=2 shot.sh x hud 6 1 1`
- `DuelistBodyLook.cs` (Game): the Duelist's body, the Maestro (its decoys the same): dark fencer, steel rim, white mask with gold filigree, gold epaulettes, crimson cape with a gold hem, swept-hilt rapier posed per move (`DuelPose`: drawn back on the lunge line, straight on the dash, across on guard, up in salute); battle-worn from To the Death (crimson rim, cracked mask, torn cape); its 2 s phase changes (`DuelShiftPose`: a bow and a lifted mask with violet silhouettes into the Masquerade, a spin carving the circle and a cut down the mask into To the Death); its last stand (`DuelLastSince`, timed from when this client first sees it, no snapshot field: cape thrown off, half the mask breaks away to a crimson eye, a shock ring and a last salute, then capeless with a crimson-edged blade); decoys flicker in as violet silhouettes on a thread from it, then snap solid (`DrawDecoyEntrance`, timed client-side) and shatter into violet shards where last seen when they go (`DrawDecoyExits`, from DrawDuelFloor); previews `DUELTRANS=<1|2>,<s>`, `DUELLAST=<s>`, `DUELDECOY=<s>` (`DUELGONE=<s>`: shattering), `DUELSHEET=all|<1-8> ZOOM=3 KNIGHT=1 CAMAT=800,450 shot.sh x hud 3 1 1` (`Tools/Preview/ShotDuelist.cs`)
- Boss visuals: `WardenLook.cs`, `QueenLook.cs`, `ReaperLook.cs`, `SingularityLook.cs`, `ColossusLook.cs`,

- `Game/ColossusBodyLook.cs`: the Colossus's body, the Quarry Golem (look A of the mockup artifact): sandstone with four slab plates (Awakening), bare and cracked (Crumbling), charred with lava seams and a core (Molten Core), kneeling (Last Stand, and while staggered: dizzy, plates rattling); chips coming off it through Crumbling (`DrawColCrumbling`); a boulder held over its head (`HoldT`, `ColBoulder`); poses from synced state (`ColFreshRing`: stomp wind-up and slam, `ChargeWarn`/`ChargeLeft` brace and ram, `ColFreshThrow`, `DazeT`, `TransT`); reuses `TitanStone` (TitanLook.cs) repainted by `ColPaint`.
