## HUD and in-run UI (Game)
- `RoundWindows.cs`: the between-round windows' shared frame: the dark layer over the arena (`RoundBackdrop`, each window's strength), a part fading and sliding in (`RoundPart`, fades `Ui.Alpha` too), the CRT tube a window sits in (`RoundTube`); used by the results, picker, shop, path choice and Wave cleared box
- `WaveClearedUi.cs`: the "Wave n cleared" box between waves as one CRT tube with Results / Build · n / Next wave tabs in any order and the Start wave n key on every tab (Docs/RoundFlow.md step 1; build panel with your pieces, "n pieces saved for later", Auto-ready waits for the build window while you hold pieces); each row's status chip (`BreakStatus`, step 2: picking, building, shopping, looting) from `Player.Break`; preview `WCSTATUS=pbslr-` (`Docs/Mockups/between-waves-flow.html` look B; squad tally, award tags, build summary, next wave, Ready key, Auto-ready switch, countdown; Minimize folds it into a strip at the top, `wcMin`); `ExpansionUi.cs` opens the build window from its Build step
- `ChatBox.cs`: the run text chat (Enter opens, bottom right, pills that fade; state in `Session.Chat`)
- `Hud.cs`: wave plate, player cards, ability bar, banners
- `HudLayout.cs`: **the HUD's layout**: the console (wave plate, the five keys and XP strip, your card), what stacks above it (chips, Riot Gear chip, teammate rows, chat, emote bar, mastery toasts, chest hint), the top row (boss bars, objective line, hint pill), the banner; the hide/show slides pivot on these rects
- `CardArt.cs`: the picker cards' holographic bodies (`Holo`, one baked texture per rarity colour, plus the scan `Band`; `HudPicker.DrawHolo` (also the Bazaar shelf cards in `ShopUi.DrawShopCard`) adds scanlines, the drifting band and the colour fringe; look: `Docs/Mockups/holo-level-up-cards.html`; `Body` is the older shaded gradient; `Ui.Glow`/`Ui.Dot` draw the glow and stack circles)
- `HudBossBars.cs` (the Warden's Cell Blocks bar: `DrawWardenCells`), `HudPicker.cs`, `HudSummary.cs`, `HudTab.cs`, `HudTiles.cs`: boss bars, upgrade picker (CRT look: lobby keys, boxed cards, `Docs/Mockups/crt-level-up-blue.html`), run summary (the "Rack" of `Docs/Mockups/crt-run-failed.html`: blue CRT tubes for title, totals, each player and the run's damage and health charts, the main menu's keycaps along the bottom), Tab menu, ability tile animations
- `EffectBar.cs`: the effect bar, a thin bar over an enemy's health bar that appears only while a timed status runs and drains with it (Wet so far; `Enemy.WetLeft`/`WetPeak`)
- `OverheadStack.cs`, `Tools/Preview/ShotOverlays.cs`: the overhead stack over each player, bottom up: health bar (wound, Blood Debt), vest, Juggernaut, Literal Tank and Armadillo bars, then the name (`Hud.cs`), all clear of the body's outer rings (`OverheadClear`); bars take rows with `OverheadRow`; Settings > Gameplay > Health bars (`hpBars`: you, teammates, enemies; `HpBarOn`, `EnemyHpBarsOn`); preview `OVERLAYS=<role 0-2>|all [OVERBARS=1] ZOOM=3.2 CAMAT=665,450 shot.sh x hud 0 12 1`
- `HealthBars.cs`, `Tooltips.cs`, `ApproachUi.cs`: damage-ghost bars and the wound's dark end (`DrawWound`), hover tooltips, objective line (the coming boss and how many waves off, the layout tag)
- `TipHolo.cs`: the lobby hover tips as a hologram (the lobby tips as a glitchy hologram; preview with `MOUSE=x,y shot.sh x lobby`, `HOLO=0` for the old look)
- `DownedLooks.cs`: going down and getting back up (mockup `Docs/Mockups/crt-downed-revived.html`, B, D, E, F): the tracker (`UpdateDownedLooks`: who went down or stood up, who is reviving), the glitching shed and the dim red husk (`DrawDownedBody`), the rebuild (`DrawRebuild`), the 36-segment revive ring and link (`DrawReviveRing`) and the DOWNED / REVIVED scan band (`DrawDownedBand`, in `Hud.cs`); the model tint is `mTintA` in `CharacterModels.cs`; client-side only, no protocol change
- `WaveBand.cs`: the CRT scan band for GET READY / WAVE N CLEARED (`Hud.DrawBanner`) and WAVE N STARTED (`LaunchIntro.DrawLaunchWave`); mockup `Docs/Mockups/crt-wave-banners.html` option B
- `SkipStatic.cs`: skipping an intro (main menu, New run boot, profile disk out) plays a 0.22 s static channel flip over a grab of the finished screen (`Docs/Mockups/intro-skip-static.html`, B)
- `PickIntro.cs`, `PickOutro.cs`, `RunEnding.cs`, `Recall.cs`, `LaunchIntro.cs`: picker open/close, run end, beam-out, run launch
- `SurvivalUi.cs`: Survival's HUD: `DrawSurvivalPlate` (SURVIVE, the m:ss clock, enemies left, score, a bar to the next faction; replaces the wave plate), `DrawSurvivalPick` (a plate while the clock is held for someone's level-up), `DrawSurvivalPhase` (a 5 s notice when a faction joins), `DrawSurvivalTab` (the lobby's Survival tab, tab id 9: its `SurvivalPage` (ModePageUi.cs): the factions as a list, a PRESSURE scope graphing the threat over the clock (`DrawSurvivalGraph`), your records and the rules; the mode is the carousel's fifth entry in `LobbyUi.cs`, preview `SURVLOBBY=1` with mode lobby); the run summary (`HudSummary.cs`) says YOU FELL with the time survived and the phase; the boss strip is hidden; preview flag `SURVIVAL=<clock s>[,pick]` (modes hud, gameover)
- `HuntMapUi.cs`: the hunt map screen (`DrawHuntMap`: the map left, hunt / party votes / next-sector keys / detail / lock-in right; shown while the party votes (`Hud.cs`); preview `huntmap huntmap 6 12.2 3`, flags `HMSEED`, `HMSTEP`, `HMVOTE`, `HMLOCK`)
- `PathsUi.cs`, `ShopUi.cs`, `RunStatsUi.cs`, `HuntUi.cs`: path cards, shop screen (two category panels, Upgrades and Services (Black Market: Buildables and Services), each opening a sideways carousel of that category, no stats sidebar; preview `SHOPCAT=-1|0|1`; look: `Docs/Mockups/shop-carousel.html`), run stats screen (the Tab screen, as hologram panels), hunt page (`HuntPage`, ModePageUi.cs: never-beaten bosses shown as ???, the host clicks an open hunt to pick it; preview flag `HUNT=<n>`: the nth boss picked)
- `InventoryUi.cs`: the Tab screen's Inventory page (`statsTab` 3, RunStatsUi.cs): wallet (gold, vectors, revive token, gadget, rerolls), relic, the room card hand, the Black Market stash, the team's room kits; lists scroll (`InvScroll`); click a card or buildable in a build round to use it (`UseInvCard`/`UseInvBuild`: opens the build screen on it, `tabSuppress` in ArenaApp.cs holds the Tab screen shut until Tab is let go); teammates' inventories (`invView`, header keys `DrawInvWho`); preview `STATSTAB=3 [INVEMPTY=1] [INVFULL=1] [INVOPEN=1 MOUSE=x,y] [INVVIEW=<id>] Tools/Preview/shot.sh x tab 23 12.2 3`
- `TabHolo.cs`: the Tab screen's hologram panels (glass, scanlines, band, fringe, flicker, switch-on), the projector beam, and its layout and text helpers
- `Game/HudModes.cs`: the HUD modes on H (corner, the default; on the player; hidden; the full console of `Hud.cs` is retired, `hudShown` stays false); corner adds level, lives, gold, vectors, relic, path and mastery (`MiniRow`); the CRT look of `Docs/Mockups/crt-hud-modes.html`; preview `HUDMODE=0|1|2 [GOLD=n] [LIVESUSED=n]`
- `Game/HudMastery.cs`: the corner HUD's mastery under each key (`MiniMastery`: four level cells and the level digit) and its moments (`TickMasteryTiles` catches XP in your slots: "+24" off the keycap; a level-up's brackets, "LV 3" pop and the line above the keys with the gain or the capstone); preview `MASTERY=1 MXFX=gain|level,<s>,<xp>[,slot] shot.sh x hud`

## Menus and lobby (Game)
- `ArenaApp.cs`: boot, input, frame loop, layout/scaling, `OnGUI` dispatch
- `DrawScratch.cs`: reused lists for the draw code's cache pruning (`FreshIds`, `FreshFx`), so a frame allocates nothing for it
- `Ui.cs`: IMGUI drawing primitives (fills, labels, bars, glows, pills, the CRT slider); the widgets (buttons, panels, switches, fields, cards, titles) are `DevKit.cs`'s `Dk...` and the CRT keys
- `ColorX.cs`: colour helpers: `c.WithAlpha(a)`, `c.ScaleAlpha(k)`, `rgba.ToColor()`, `c.ToRgba(a)` (use them instead of `new Color(c.r, c.g, c.b, a)`)
- `MenuBackgrounds.cs`: the menu background (`DrawMenuBackground`: the Holo Floor on the main menu unless `menuBgOverride`, else the player's pick, Deep Field by default), the reward-background plumbing for future unlockable ones (`RewardBackgrounds`, `SetBackground`), `BackgroundThumb` and the shared drawing helpers (`Hash`, `GradQuad`, `SoftGlow`, `Stars`, `Chevron`)
- `BackgroundReact.cs`: groundwork for reactive menu backgrounds (behind a run they ignore the pointer and clicks: `BgInRun`; pointer, pointer speed, click ripples; thumbnails map the pointer into their own view)
- `MenuBeat.cs`: the Holo Floor dancing to the music: the playing track's spectrum (`SoundBank.MusicSpectrum`) into 24 bands (`beatBands`, `BeatBand`), a beat when the bass jumps (`BeatPulse`, `beatWaves`), drawn by `HoloSkyline` / `HoloBeatLines` in `MenuHolo.cs`; ticked from `ReactTick`; preview `BEAT=<0-1> BG=0 shot.sh x bg|menu`
- `BackgroundArena.cs`: the three quiet reactive rank-reward backgrounds, no lines or grids so they never fight the arena floor (Deep Field the free default, Nebula Drift 29, Aurora Veil 44)
- `MenuSoundBank.cs`: the menu's holo sounds (`BuildMenuHolo`, the picks from `Docs/Mockups/menu-sound-lab.html`): phosphor hover tick, Play/New run power chord, Join/Settings chirp, Quit's CRT-off, the eject beeps and whoosh, split-flap title letters, the rooms' bleeps, neon sparks, the Play move's Data vault and Back, Continue's chord, DEL's blip and dissolve (`Sfx.MenuPlay` ... `RunDelete`, local only); played from `MenuSound`, `TickMenuSounds` (also the low menu hum loop), `RunRowHover` in `MenuHolo.cs`; `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh Menu`
- `MenuHolo.cs`: the holo look's helpers (`Docs/Mockups/menu-holo-floor.html`): neon (`NeonPath`, `NeonFlicker`, `HoloCorners`), title flap sounds (`TitleFlapCues`), Quit's tube-off (`QuitWithSound`), the run rows' hover tick, Settings' holo panel (`DrawPanelNeon`, `DrawSettingsLink`) and the Holo Floor background (`BgHoloFloor`, the default)
- `Menus.cs`, `MenuIntro.cs`, `MenuDrawer.cs`, `WindowAnim.cs`: main menu (`DrawMenu`: the machine, `MenuTitleAt`; MenuIntro.cs: the monitor's glass and typed title, the menu's entrance on the machine clock), pause/lobby/settings/how-to menus (CRT cabinet), slide clocks; the pause and lobby Esc menus dock in from the sides (`docking`, `DockPanelX`, `DockTrails`; `Docs/Mockups/pause-dock.html`);  both menus are a grey screen in the mode's colour (`CrtStyle.Accent = ModeColor()`, like the Game Mode screen); the pause, lobby, Settings and Profile cabinets and their windows (How to play too) are the Tab screen's hologram glass (`cabGlass`, `DrawHoloGlass`)
- `CrtWindows.cs`: the menu box (the lobby loadout's look, `CrtKit.LoadoutBox`: the lobby's gradient wash, `LobbyKit.Wash`, in `CrtStyle.BoxWash`) and its side window (opening, typed text, BeginDrawer/DrawerRow, WinKey/SwitchRow/WinSlider)
- `PcMonitor.cs`, `MachineLook.cs`, `UiRound.cs`: the machine's parts: the monitor (`DrawMonitor`: graphite case, the title's CRT glass in neon, the chin with ARENA CRT-14, grilles, power light, the drive and eject key; `DrawMonitorAbove` hides a disk going into the slit), the shared look (`Metal`, `NeonBox`, `MachKey` keycaps with a lamp, `RoundKey`, `Vent`, `HVent`, `Led`) and `Ui.RoundBox` / `Ui.RoundGrad` (rounded boxes of any radius, flat or with a gradient)
- `MenuMachine.cs`: the main menu's case (design B of `Docs/Mockups/main-menu-machines.html`): Play / Join game / Wiki keycaps, the control strip under the profile (Settings cog, POWER / DISK lights, Quit power key: `DrawStrip`, `DrawRoundKeys`, `FlatRoundKey`), the bay behind a roller shutter (`menuPlayK`; the runs via `DrawPlayPicker`, Join via `DrawBayJoin`), the profile screen (`DrawProfScreen`), POWER / DISK lights; its entrance on the machine clock `MachT` (`CaseDrop`, `MonitorShakeAt`, `DrawLandingDust`, the power-on, `PlayMachineCues`)
- `BayWiki.cs`: the main menu's Wiki on the case's bay screen (`menuWikiOpen`): a search box over `Wiki.Search`, the result rows, an entry's page with its text and references as links, ‹ BACK through the pages opened; preview `WIKI=<query> [WIKIPAGE=<name>] shot.sh x menu`
- `WikiPick.cs`: the wiki everywhere: the Wiki key (`KeyAction.Wiki`, tap: `OpenWiki` on the bay or as the modal `screenWiki` over a run, lobby or menu, pausing a solo run; hold: the eyedropper, `wikiPicking` with `Ui.PickText`, `WikiUnderPointer` (text, then enemies, minions, players, shots, props and chests; an enemy's status effects in the tag, right click opens the first), the tag `DrawWikiPickTag`; the wiki's PICK key: `StartWikiPick`, sticky until a pick or Esc, keeping a solo run paused); the pause and lobby menus' WIKI rows call `OpenWiki`; preview `WIKISCREEN=<query>[,<page>]`, `WIKIPICK=x,y`
- `BaySettings.cs`: the main menu's Settings on the case's bay screen (`Docs/Mockups/menu-settings-bay.html`): `menuSetOpen`, the four group tiles (`DrawSetTiles`), the tube-off switch between pages (`SetGo`), each group's page (`SetAudio`, `SetFx`, `SetGameplay`, `SetKeys`) with bar sliders, toggles, choices and rebind keys over Settings' own values; preview `MENUSET=<-1..3> [SETSCROLL=px] shot.sh x menu`
- `MenuVhs.cs`: the main menu buttons' VHS hover (with the `Sfx.MenuHover` tape-head tick on enter, via `MenuVhs.OnHover`; `Ui.SilentHover` mutes the generic hover tick for these buttons) and snap-to-focus click (`MenuVhs.Draw` wraps a button's draw call in `Menus.cs` and `SavesUi.cs`)
- `CrtStyle.cs`: **the CRT look's settings**: the palette (Ink, Dim, Bg, Danger, Body, Desc, Head, Gold, the box/corner/glass colours) and how every CRT key is drawn (fill and outline strengths, widths, text mix, locked/waiting/static keys, the HUD console's keys, the inverse-video keys); change a value to restyle every CRT screen
- `CrtUi.cs`: the blue-phosphor CRT look of the Play picker and the path choice (`Begin`, glowing `Text`, `SoftKey` (lit, danger, quiet) for every key on these screens, `End` for scanlines and glass); Reduced effects drops glow, roll bar and flicker
- `KeyUi.cs`: the menu's mechanical keycap buttons (`KeyUi.Key`: a keycap in a dark well with a corner lamp and a few pixels of travel; `KeyUi.Well` for rings). Used by the main menu's Play / Join game / Records / Settings (`Menus.cs`) and the Play screen's New run and Back (`SavesUi.cs`)
- `StormHammer.cs`, `Game/StormHammerLook.cs`: the Elementalist Tank's Storm Hammer: the throw is queued by `FireVolley` (`QueueHammerThrow`) and leaves the hand `HammerThrowRelease` later (`TickHammerThrows`); the Alt slam (`WeaponAlt.HammerSlam` over `Msg.WeaponDash`, `HostHammerSlam`, `TickHammerSlams`: wind-up, then a shockwave that hurts, shoves and electrifies each enemy once); `FxKind.HammerThrow` is the held hammer's pose clock (Pos2.X 0 throw, 1 slam), `FxKind.HammerSlam` the wave. Looks: held pose (`DrawStormHammerHeld`), Thunderhead projectile (`DrawStormHammerThunder`), Pulse wave (`DrawHammerSlamFx`, `DrawHammerGlow`); mockups `Docs/Mockups/storm-hammer-throw.html`, `storm-hammer-slam.html`; preview `HAMMERFX=throw:0.1|slam:0.45|wave:0.25`; tests `run.sh 1 30 storm` and `stormslam`
- `Keybinds.cs`, `KeybindsUi.cs`: every rebindable key (`KeyAction`, defaults, page groups `Groups`, optional second key per action, Mute starts unbound (`Unbindable`), the × on a row unbinds any action (`Unbind`, saved as 0), saved in PlayerPrefs, rebind capture, `Fill` for "{Action}" tokens in text) and Settings → Keybinds. Weapon secondaries read `PlayerInput.WeaponAction` (Left Alt) in `Core/Ninja.cs` `LocalWeaponDash`
- `MapView.cs`: the Map key (M) cycling the Expanding/Survival map: minimap → full map → off, remembered (`map_view`); the grow-and-zoom and CRT fold animations (`MapGeoNow`, `Folded`), the full map's title, legend and corner brackets, the "Map off" note; drawn by `DrawRoomsMinimap` (`RoomsLook.cs`) in the window it gives; preview `MAPVIEW=<from>,<to>,<t>` or `clip.sh x hud 6 3 3 MAPCYCLE=0:3.6:0.05` with `EXPANDMAP=6 ZOOM=1.5`
- `Game/MapStyleLook.cs`: the map's dress (`ArenaApp.MapStyle`: 1 Holo, the look; 0 the old plain one, 2 Blueprint, for comparison): backing, lattice grid, scanlines and sweep (`SweepReveal`: the sweep reveals enemies, then they fade; preview `MAPFOES=<n> UTIME=<s>`), bracket corners, the minimap's tab, aim arrows, view-box corners, the full map's sector letters; hooks in `DrawRoomsMinimap` (`RoomsLook.cs`); preview `MAPSTYLE=<n> GRIDCORE=1 EXPANDMAP=6 FOG=1 FOGROOM=3 ZOOM=1 [MAPVIEW=1,1,1] shot.sh x hud 8 3 3`
- `LoadoutCarousel.cs`: the lobby loadout's carousel rows (Class, Weapon, Ability, Ultimate, four keys each: `BeginCarousel`/`CarItem`/`EndCarousel`, floating arrows (`FloatArrow`, no key box) that fade at the row's ends, mouse wheel, the pick scrolled into view; only Class scrolls for now, `CarScrolls`), the loadout's grid (`LoadoutTop`, `HeadingInk`, `PreviewTop`, `PreviewEnd`, `SeparatorY`, `BottomTop`: the Role heading's letters on the preview's top, its bottom on the Ultimate keys', the rows evenly spaced), the detail box's role and class pages (`DetailRole`/`DetailClass`) and the retired role/class hover tips (`LoadoutHoverTipsRetired`); preview `CAROUSEL=row,first DETAIL=role|class shot.sh x lobby`
- `PerksUi.cs`: the lobby's Perks view, opened by the loadout's Perks key (`DrawPerksKey`, right of the class name; look A: your slots left, perk cards right; click to equip, hover for the rules) and the perk icons on party cards (`DrawSeatPerks`); see `Docs/RiotGear.md`
- `Core/Favorites.cs`, `FavoritesUi.cs`: saved favourite loadouts (`FavLoadout`, `Favorites.Save/Delete/Load`, 6 slots, `Profile.Favorites` as "fav" lines, profile format 8); the lobby's **Saved loadouts** drawer over the loadout's right column (`DrawFavoritesKey`: the star tab left of the Loadout tab, `LobbySavedKey`, `DrawFavorites`, typed names, the pencil to rename (`Favorites.Rename`), X then SURE? to delete); preview `FAVS=1|name|sure|rename|key shot.sh x lobby`; test suite `favorites`
- `PlayerInspect.cs`: click a lobby party card: yours opens your profile (`OpenOwnProfile`), a teammate's opens their large holo card (`OpenInspect`/`DrawInspect`: frame, card background, name effect, trail, rank badge, XP, kills / best wave / bosses, equipped names; `LobbyEntry.XpPct/BestWave/Kills/BossKills`); `Docs/Mockups/lobby-profile-inspect.html`
- `ProfileUi.cs`: your profile as a CRT cabinet (header, profile screen with badge/name/XP, four tab keys, option keys, Back; opened by the main menu's player card, also the pause and lobby menus' Profile row without Look) and the "YOUR PROFILE" nudge beside the card (`DrawProfileHint`)
- `ProfileWindows.cs`: the profile's CRT windows (cab window kind 3): Rank > Progress / Tiers / XP sources, the Unlocks tab
- `CosmeticsUi.cs`: the Look windows (`DrawLookWindow`: try it on, how others see you, equip list for Frame, Trail, Background, Card background, Name effect, Aim line, Cursor) and the Reward ladder
- `ChickenLook.cs`, `ChickenSoundBank.cs`: the Twin Blades' Rubber Chicken skin: `DrawChicken` (held by the feet, the neck bending behind the swing and stretching with its speed: `ChickAt`, screaming while it cuts, squashing on the hit), `DrawChickenTrail` (feathers instead of the blade trail), `chickenOn` set by each Twin Blades draw from the swinger's skin (`TwinBladesLook.cs`); the sound pack `BuildChicken` (`Squeak`: a reed voice through two nasal resonances, `Resonate`, `Thwap`; `Sfx.ChickCut1` ... `ChickCatch`); previews `SKIN=twin-chicken SWING=<s> SWINGW=TwinBlades TWINSTEP=1..5 shot.sh x hud`, `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh Chick`
- `WeaponSkinUi.cs`: the Look tab's Weapon skin section (`DrawSkinWindow`, section 7): the lobby class's weapon keys, each weapon's skins to equip, a live preview (the Twin Blades: the Crescent Waltz on a loop, `DrawTwinSkinPreview`), the class's expertise card; preview `SECTION=7 [SKINKIT=Knight,Dps,0 SKINXP=<n> SKINEQ=<skin id>] shot.sh x cosmetics`
- `RankLook.cs`, `RankUi.cs`, `RankTrails.cs`, `FrameLooks.cs` + `FrameLooksMore.cs` (the fifteen frames; the last eight are ranks 56-70), `CardBackgrounds.cs` + `CardBackgroundsMore.cs` (the eighteen party-card fills, ranks 7-71), `NameEffects.cs` (the twenty name looks, ranks 5-79, `DrawName`), `AimLineLooks.cs` (the nine rank-unlocked aim lines and Classic, `DrawAimLook`; local only), `CursorLooks.cs` + `CursorLooksMore.cs` (the eighteen rank-unlocked crosshairs and Classic, `DrawCursorLook`; local only), `EmoteLooks.cs` / `EmoteBar.cs` (the thirteen emotes and the hold-to-open bar; state in `Session.Emotes`), `LightCycleTrail.cs` (the rank-45 corner-snapped wall trail): the rank badge (a pixel shield per tier), the results strip, badge hover card and Records > Rank, and the trails drawn behind players
- `LobbyRoom.cs` (with `Core/LobbyAvatars.cs`): the lobby's Hangout tab, a big scrolling room where everyone's character walks with WASD wearing their cosmetics, with a minimap, a PvP switch and the preview-window grow transition (`Docs/Hangout.md`)
- `LobbyLayout.cs`: **the lobby's layout**: every box round the lobby's edges (header keys, the mode card, party column, Ready/Start, the tab row: the Saved star, Loadout, Difficulty, Modifiers and Practice at the right end (folder tabs: `LobbyKit.FolderTab`), the big screen, the Game Mode screen's page) and the bands the entrance, leave, New run boot, return and launch cut it into; move a box here and those animations follow
- `ModeScreenUi.cs`: the lobby's **Game Mode screen**: the mode carousel and the mode's own page (Quick Play, Campaign, Boss Hunt, Survival) full screen, a view inside the lobby (open while `lobbyTab` is a mode tab: `IsModeTab`/`OnModeScreen`/`ModeTabNow`); a new hosted lobby opens on it, TO THE LOBBY / Back / Esc leave it, the lobby's mode card (`DrawModeCard`, CHANGE or VIEW) opens it; preview `LOBBYTAB=8 shot.sh x lobby` (4 Boss Hunt with HUNT=1)
- `LobbyFx.cs`: the lobby's live effects (Lobby FX Lab mockup): the neon dot running round the loadout preview (`DrawPreviewRunner`), the loadout's separator line (`DrawLoadoutSeparator`), `HoloBoot` (the scan-wipe power-on: lobby pieces in `LobbyEnter.cs`, the CRT cabinet and its window in `CrtWindows.cs`), the proximity glow (`LobbyGlowBox`, `DrawLobbyProxGlow`), the Ready shockwave (`TickReadyShock`, `DrawReadyShock`; preview `READYFX=<s>`); the launch glitch is `LaunchIntro.DrawLobbyLaunchGlitch` (preview `LAUNCHFX=<s>`), the crawling scanlines `LobbyCrt.CrtScanlines`
- `LobbyKit.cs`: lobby-only digital CRT keys, headings, panel, tiles and stat bars (`LobbyUi.cs` draws the lobby with them)
- `LobbyCrt.cs`: the lobby's blue CRT screen and glass (every lobby draw), and New run's CRT boot into the lobby (`StartNewRunCrt`, `DrawLobbyEnteringCrt`; the lobby powers on after the log, `DrawCalmLoadIn` style 4 = `DrawPowerOnOpen`, also used by ModeBlink.cs, `CRTSTYLE=` previews the others, `CRT=<s>` the time; its sounds `PlayCrtEntryCues` with the `Sfx.Crt*` cues: power-on thump, typing keys, line beeps, scan sweep, ready chime; `LobbyEnter.cs` has the other entrances)
- `LobbyCrtLeave.cs`: the lobby's Back with effects on (`Docs/Mockups/lobby-return.html` option A, 1.05 s): the CRT power-cycle, the screen squashing to a white line and the main menu snapping open from it (`DrawMenu`, no clicks); one squash for GUI (`GUI.matrix`), GL (`crtSquashOn` in `CanvasPx`) and the background (`DrawCrtLeaveBackdrop`); `crtLeave`, `LeaveLenNow`; Reduced effects or effects off keep the reversed entrance (`LobbyEnter.cs`); preview `LOBBYCRTOUT=<s> shot.sh x lobby`
- `ModeBlink.cs`: the Game Mode screen's way back to the lobby with effects on, the quick relink (`Docs/Mockups/gamemode-lobby-transition.html` B, about 2.6 s, hitch-proof clock `TickModeBlink`, any key skips): `LeaveModeScreen` (TO THE LOBBY, Back, Esc; `ModeScreenBack`: Back/Esc on the screen New run opened leave to the main menu), the old page squashing to a line that cools to blue, the boot's blue CRT (`ModeRelinkBlue`) typing `> QUICK PLAY ... SET.`, then New run's power-on (`DrawPowerOnOpen`) opening the lobby (`DrawModeBlink`, drawn by `DrawLobby`; sounds `PlayModeRelinkCues`); preview `LOBBYTAB=8 MODEBLINK=<s> shot.sh x lobby`
- `RunGraphs.cs`: Records > Recent runs (`DrawRecentRuns`: run strip, stat tiles, Overview and Trends tabs; GL line/area charts with hover readouts)
- `LivesUi.cs`: the lives chip over the wave panel (hearts; Core/Checkpoints.cs) and `DrawLifePrompt` (Respawn [Y] / Game over [N] before the results; RunEnding.cs holds the ending; preview `RETRY=1 ENDING=1 shot.sh x gameover 10 5 1`); the Deathless line is in HudSummary.cs, "CHECKPOINT SAVED" in WaveClearedUi.cs
- `SavesUi.cs`: the saved runs on the case's bay screen behind the shutter (`DrawPlayPicker`: YOUR RUNS, one scrolling list, newest first and taller, Continue and DEL on each row, New run at the foot), Records, unlock toasts, the lobby's resume seats
- `Game/CrtKit.cs`: shared CRT cabinet pieces (retyping text: a label swapping to another rewrites in place, `Morph`, preview `PAUSEMORPH=<s>` in mode pause; power-on, boot-log line, scan bar, soft digital key, the floating chassis and soft edges)
- `Game/DifficultyUi.cs`: the lobby's Difficulty tab (five cards, then every number as a slider with a text box; host edits, others read)
- `Game/ModifiersUi.cs`: the lobby's Modifiers tab (a carousel row per tier, `LoadoutCarousel.cs` rows 4-6, cards `DrawModCard`; the Enabled box `DrawModTray`); preview `LOBBYTAB=2 CAROUSEL=<4-6>,<first>`

## Dev tools and editors (Game)
- `Core/RunSnapshot.cs`, `SnapshotUi.cs`: run snapshots (F6 save, F7 load the newest, the dock's SNAP sheet): `RunSnapshot.Capture`/`Restore` write and read every `Game` field by reflection (`GraphWriter`/`GraphReader`: types once, shared objects by reference, objects reached from statics as paths, the Game as the live one; fields matched by name), `Skip` (session, recorders, hooks, Bot), `CantLoad` (host, no guests: `NetPeer.PeerCount`), files in `Profile.Folder/snapshots` (`SaveFile`, `List`, `Rename`, `Kept` 40), `Game.AfterSnapshotLoad`; preview `DEVSHEET=6 [SNAPDEL=1] [SNAPREN=1]`, `SNAPTOAST=<text>`; test suite `snapshot` (loads back and replays 8 s tick for tick, random waves and Campaign)
- `UiScreens.cs`: the tool-screen stack (`UiScreen`, `OpenScreen`/`CloseScreen`, `HandleScreenEsc`, `DrawScreens`; `devMenuOpen`, `waveEdOpen` are properties over it): add a window by registering one screen (HANDOFF.md "Adding a screen or a menu row")
- `UiLayout.cs`: `DkStack` (column / row / cell / grid layout) and `DkWindow` (the tool window shell)
- `DevKit.cs`: the CRT look of the dev tools and the chat box: `DkPanel`, `DkSub`, `DkCard`, `DkTitle`, `DkKey` (the one CRT key: states on/locked/view-only/static/quiet, icon, sub-line, lamp; `LobbyKit.Key`, `WinKey` and `CrtUi.SoftKey` were merged into it), `DkChoice`, `DkSwitch`, `DkField`, the one widget kit for the dev tools, chat and most menus (the old `Ui.Panel/Button/Toggle/Choice/Field/Card/Fancy/HudPanel` are gone); also `DkBox`, `DkHud`, `DkTag`, `DkBar` (used by `DevMenu.cs`, `ChatBox.cs`)
- `DevSpawnerLook.cs`: dev spawners' front end: the Spawn-an-enemy sheet's Spawner row (`DrawSpawnerRow`: every, max alive, Undo, Clear), the pad with a ghost of its enemy and the ring to the next spawn (`DrawDevSpawners`, world pass), its tag (`DrawDevSpawnerTags`, HUD); preview `DEVSPAWNER=1 shot.sh x hud`, `DEVSHEET=5 SPAWNERMODE=1 shot.sh x dev`
- `DevSpawnItems.cs`: the F1 dock's Spawn-a-gadget-chest-or-relic sheet (PARTY > ITEMS & CHESTS…, `devSheet` 7, `DrawDockGadgetsRelics`): gold and vectors scatter in front of them as their own pickups (`Game.DevSpawnCoins`), a gadget drops at the player's feet as their own copy, a chest of any kind stands in front of them (not holding the wave), a relic goes straight into their slot (`Game.DevSpawnGadget`, `Game.DevSpawnChest`, `Game.DevSetRelic` in `Core/DevTools.cs`); preview `DEVSHEET=7 shot.sh x dev`; test suite `gadgets`
- `DevDock.cs`: the F1 dock (`DrawDevMenu`: pinned keys, WAVE / PARTY / VIEW / LABS drawers, stat-editor side drawer, Spawn-an-upgrade / Spawn-an-enemy / Snapshots sheet (`DrawDockUpgrades`, `DrawDockEnemies`; `Game.DevSpawnLoot` and `Game.DevSpawnEnemy` in `Core/DevTools.cs`; the Gadget / relic sheet in `DevSpawnItems.cs`); non-modal in a run, `MouseOnDock` stops shots under it and `DockOwnsEvent` (from `OnGUI`, ArenaApp.cs) keeps its clicks, drags and wheel from the screen under it)
- `DevMenu.cs`: the stat editor rows (`DrawDevPlayers`, `DevCard`)
- `Core/CellLayoutEdits.cs`, `Game/CellEditorUi.cs`: the Cell Editor (F1 > LABS > CELL EDITOR): pick any `CellLayouts.All` layout, drag out walls (snap 10, min 20), click sleeper / trap / wall-trap / loot spots (drag one to move it; with WALL, dragging a wall moves it, Shift draws over it), right click erases, undo, SAVE (`CellLayoutEdits.Set` replaces the entry for new rooms, writes CellLayoutEdits.txt, loaded at start by `LoadCellLayoutEdits`); `Problems` flags the chest, door-lane and slot rules; `Tools/CellEdits/fold.py` writes the file into `CellLayouts.cs`; preview `CELLED=<index> shot.sh x celled`; test in suite `celllayouts`
- `Core/CellTemplateRequests.cs`: what the Cell Editor asks of the code, in `CellTemplateRequests.txt`: your own templates (`NEW BLANK`, `COPY TO MINE`, `SAVE MINE`) to add and built-in layouts marked for deletion (`MARK DELETE`); changes no layout index
- `Core/DevTraps.cs`, `Game/DevTrapUi.cs`: Dev Kit traps (F1 > VIEW): click the arena to place spike plates, slow fields and turrets (`DevPlaceTrap`, `Msg.DevTraps` sends the list; `RoomTrapList` adds them to the map's own), right click removes
- `Game/RoomViewUi.cs`: the Cell Editor's ROOM VIEW tab: a piece shape (turn, lattice spot) with each cell's rolled layout (`CellLayouts.PlansOfCells`), a click opens that cell's layout, TEST THIS SHAPE
- `Core/HubLayoutEdits.cs`, `Core/HubLayouts.Generated.cs`, `Game/HubEditorUi.cs`: the Cell Editor's HUB tab: the six hub floor plans' walls, pillars and crates (`HubLayoutData`, `Layouts.Replace`/`PartsOf`), HubLayoutEdits.txt, folded by `Tools/CellEdits/fold.py` into the generated `Data` that `Layouts.Make` reads (`HubLayoutEdits.Committed`); preview `CEDTAB=2 HUBID=1..6 shot.sh x celled`
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/FloorGrid.cs`: test suite `floorgrid`: the six arena floor plans, the cell layouts and pit variants, rolled room cover, the Survival room and the Wall Kick panels all sit on the 50 px floor grid (`RoomTraps.Tile`)
- `Core/LayoutTest.cs`: TEST LAYOUT (`Game.DevTestCells` builds a piece free in a Grid run with its cells' plans fixed by `CellLayouts.ForcePlan`, `DevTestHub` puts a hub plan down), host / solo dev only; test suite `layouttest`
- `Core/CoreLayoutEdits.cs`, `Game/CoreEditorUi.cs`: the Cell Editor's CORE tab: the ten core floor plans (`CoreLayouts.All[i].Pieces`: the quarter or half, its mirrors drawn dim), tools wall / pillar / crate / heavy crate, `Problems` (door lanes, gate floor, inside the core), CoreLayoutEdits.txt folded into `CoreLayouts.cs` by `Tools/CellEdits/fold.py`; TEST = `Game.DevTestCore` (`LayoutTest.cs`); preview `CEDTAB=3 COREID=0..9 shot.sh x celled`
- `EnemyPreview.cs`: `DrawEnemyPreview`, one enemy drawn live in a UI box (dev tools' enemy list, pause menu's next boss, path cards, Game Mode page)
- `../Editor/ArenaEditor.cs`: scene setup and Windows build menu item
- `Game/ClickLog.cs`: a temporary lost-click debugging aid: F9 shows the last mouse presses and releases and logs them

## Audio (Game)
- `SoundBank.cs`: the sound table (one `Entry` per `Sfx`: takes, gap, pitch wander; `Categories()` puts each on a volume slider), the 32-voice pool (`PlayClip`: a sound per source, oldest stolen; music priority 0), the recipes built on a background thread at startup (`BuildAll`, `Ready`; `PumpClips` makes the clips a few a frame), your own sounds (`OwnSoundFiles`, `LoadProjectSounds`, `DecodeOwnSounds`: `<Sfx name>[_n].wav` in persistentDataPath/sounds or AudioClips in `Assets/Resources/Sfx` replace a sound), the music's two crossfading voices (renders copied into clips 2 s a frame, `UploadMusic`), and the core recipes; each `*SoundBank.cs` adds its system's recipes
- `GameMusic.cs`, `MenuMusic.cs`: in-run and menu music
- `MusicPack.cs`: the Inspector asset for your own clips (replace menu / battle / boss music, a battle track per faction (`Factions`, `SoundBank.FactionTrackId`), extra tracks); `CustomMusic.cs`: the music folder scan and WAV decoder for your own tracks; `MusicLibrary.cs`: every track the music player lists (extra mood recipes) and the `Jukebox` selection logic; `MusicPlayerUi.cs`: Settings > Audio > Music player
- `PauseBackdrop.cs`: behind the pause and lobby Esc menus: the arena darkening over 1.5 s under faint moving static (`DrawPauseBackdrop`; Reduced effects: the darkening only)
- `PickSounds.cs`, `PauseGlitch.cs`: upgrade picker and pause-menu sounds

## Tools and docs (repo root)
- `Tools/CompileCheck`, `Tools/SimTest` (tests in `Tools/SimTest/Tests`), `Tools/RenderCheck`, `Tools/Preview`, `Tools/SoundCheck`, `Tools/SoundForge` (`index.html` a browser synth page (layers, EQ, seamless loops, output volume, saved presets) that exports WAVs named after `Sfx` sounds for the sounds folder; `names.py` refreshes its name list from the enum), `Tools/EnemyForge` (Enemy Forge, Docs/EnemyForge.md: `Server.cs` a localhost server with the real roster from `Defs.Enemies` and the designs folder, `web/index.html` the editor + JS preview sim + brief for Claude, `Window.cs` the Windows app window (WinForms + WebView2, built with `-define:WINDOW`), `package.sh` builds the single-file `EnemyForge.exe` that `.github/workflows/enemyforge-release.yml` publishes, `run.sh [port]` runs from source): see `HANDOFF.md` §3
- `Tools/SimTest/extras.sh`, `Tools/SimTest/ci_coverage.py`, `.github/workflows/checks.yml`, `.github/actions/install-mono`: CI runs `core`, `features` and the other pass/fail suites (the extras, two shards) side by side; one shared mono install; `ci_coverage.py --extras` works the extras out from `Program.cs` (every suite `FeatureTests` does not cover), so a new suite needs no list; extras run in parallel over the in-memory network (`NetPeer.Loopback`, Core/Net.cs).
- `Tools/SimTest/Content.cs`: content queries for tests, read from the real definitions (`Content.RoleWithoutUlt`, `RoleWithWeapons`, `RoleWithAbilityChoices`, `UltFallsBack`, counts like `WeaponCount`, `TotalUlts`, `DpsFourthWeapons`): a deterministic pick, a clear failed check when none matches; `Content.Fuzz` (suite `contentfuzz`, or `CONTENTFUZZ=1` before any suite) adds a fake 4th weapon, 4th 1st ability and an ultimate on a free key to every role and class to prove tests don't name a class as their example (HANDOFF §3 "Writing tests that survive new content")
- `Tools/Preview/ShotPerf.cs`, `Tools/SimTest/Tests/Perf.cs`, `Tools/SimTest/Tests/SurvivalPerf.cs`, `Tools/SimTest/Tests/BreachCrowd.cs`: lag measuring: `PERFRUN=` (a Quick Play breach wave drawn every frame: vertices, draw ms, top vertex sources; `PERFMAP=<n>`: on a grown map; with `SURVIVAL=840 SURVMARKS=55`: Survival's room); report `breachcrowd` (`run.sh 1 1 breachcrowd [secs] [wave]`: a Warden breach wave as played on a six-breach map, alive enemies by type and host ms) and the `perf` and `survperf` reports (sim ms a frame; `run.sh 1 1 survperf [secs] [start min]`: Survival at the cap); the vertex budget it guards is `OffScreen`/`SegsFor`/`CrowdTol` (`WorldRenderer.cs`: a crowd's curves drawn to a looser tolerance, up to 0.8 px at 90 enemies on screen) and `PartCrowd` (`Juice.cs`); preview `CROWD=<n> NINJA=0 shot.sh x hud 3 1 2` (a Warden mob round you), `VTXTRACE=1` prints any shot's top vertex sources (run `mono shot.exe` in `Tools/Preview/out`)
- `Tools/Preview/variants.sh`, `Tools/Preview/clip.sh`, `Tools/Preview/sweep.py`, `Tools/Preview/ShotClip.cs`: the variant sheet (one scene with different env flags, a labelled grid) and GIF clips (the live match running on from the shot, `CLIP=<frames>` in `ShotClip.cs`, or a sweep of a scene's time flag)
- `Tools/Preview/ShotFlameWall.cs`: `FLAMEWALL=<s since the cast>`, Flame Wall cast at your cursor with shots flying in (the ring of fire in `Game/FlameWallLook.cs`)
- `Tools/Preview/ShotLighting.cs`: `LIGHTING=1|2|3`, lighting on the Pillar Ring (1-2: a Spinner's ring of shots past the pillars, Grunts, a crate, barrel and chest, a blast and a Flame Wall; 3: a Railgun beam, a lightning chain, a frost burst and a muzzle flash); `LIGHTS=0` off, `LIGHTSOFT=0|1`, `FLOORDIM=0-2`; `LIGHTPERF=<enemies>,<shots>[,<blasts>]`: the lighting's draw cost in a crowd (off, hard, soft, soft + dim)
- `Tools/Preview/ShotChalkFire.cs`: `CHALKFIRE=<s since the ignite>`, the Spirit Chalk Alt played through the game: a sealed loop round three grunts, then set alight (-1: before); `ZOOM=2.4`, mode hud
- `Tools/Preview/ShotRedShot.cs`: `REDSHOT=1`, two fans of a boss's red aimed shots flying at you, a violet orb and small shots for scale (`Game/RedShotLook.cs`)
- `WaveNumbers.cs`: the wave-size numbers in one place (`Picks`, `SpawnInterval`, `EscortCount`, opening delay) and `WaveRules` (`Game.Rules`: Standard for Quick Play and the Campaign, Hunt with its own `CountMult`); `Docs/WaveRefactor.md`
- `WavePlan.cs`: the wave being built (`Game.plan`): size, pool, melee list, the distinct-type cap and the boss faction's share; `BuildWave` (`EnemyAI.cs`) is a list of phases (`StageBoss`, `StageSize`, `StageZombies`, `StageApproach`, `StagePath`, `StageRecipe`, `StageLegacy`, then `MixApproach`)
- `Spawner.cs`: how a built wave comes in: `TickSpawning` picks the pacing (`SpawnPacing`: Hold, Timed, Interval), `SpawnNextEntry`/`SpawnNextSingle` are the one place that hands out the next squad or single, `PendingEnemies` is `Game.Remaining`; test suite `spawndump` (every arrival of a few modes played headless vs `Golden/spawndump.txt`)
- `BossWaves.cs`: what a boss wave holds (`Queue`: boss, partner, escort), the boss-and-partner pair (`Own`) and where bosses stand (`SpawnPoint`); `SpawnEnemyAt` (`EnemyAI.cs`) is `NewEnemy` (stats, `ScaleBoss`) + `PlaceEnemy` (home, position, first timers)
- `Approaches.cs` Warden lessons follow the chart in `Docs/Approaches.md` (`WaveLesson.Star/Trickle/Also`, `FactionDef.LessonSquads`; the new enemy is last in the queue, `stragglers` in `Variety.cs`); test `wardencycle`, `trickle`
- `BasicFaction.cs`: the **basic faction**: the eight plain enemies left in the waves (`BasicFaction.Types`, `Def` with squads and the Last stand set piece), the only plain enemies left (the 24 no faction used were removed Nov 18), `SquadLive`, `Game.FactionFor` (Quick Play and Boss Hunt: a boss with no faction gets the basic one); test suite `basicfaction`
- `HiveBees.cs`: what sets the Hive's bees apart from their kin: Worker pack speed, the Soldier's charge armour, the Comb Shield's leak, the Needler's strafe (numbers at the top)
- `HiveKin.cs`, `Game/HiveLook.cs`: the Hive Queen's faction (Docs/HiveFaction.md): eight bees in the Warden's roles (`HiveKin.Is`; their behaviour is their brains: Brain.cs, Docs/EnemyBehaviours.md), the Hive `FactionDef` in `Approaches.cs`, the Lobber's honey and the Stinger's `StingBolt` in `WardenFaction.cs`, the look ported from `Docs/Mockups/hive-faction.html` (`DrawHiveBody`, `DrawCombPlate`, `DrawStingBolt`, the Lobber's ring and glob in `GasLobLook.cs`); test suite `hive`; preview `HIVE=0.4 HIVELOB=1.8 shot.sh x hud 1`
- `Penumbral.cs`, `Game/PenumbralLook.cs`: the Eclipse's faction, the Penumbral Court (Docs/EclipseFaction.md), step 1: Ember (weaves in, `EmberSparks` drops `Defs.EmberSpark` shots that hang), Corona Priest (`CoronaRing`: one shot at you, the slot beside it open; `PriestWindUp` in `WindUpOf`), Heliograph (plants, flickers, burns a lane: `HelioStep`, `HelioLaneDistance`, `HelioLocked`; at most `MaxHeliographs` a wave, `CapBeams` in Variety.cs); bot danger `PenumbralBotDanger`; the look draws the lane under the enemies (`DrawPenumbralFloor`, from `DrawVarietyFloor`) and the sparks (`DrawEmberSpark`); step 2: Moonward (its Facing is the moon's angle; `MoonwardStops` in Collide soaks players' shots), Gnomon (`GnomonStep`: plants, swells, flashes everyone outside `InGnomonShadow`), Dusk Lobber (a Gas Grenadier by `HiveKin`, lobbing `PatchKind.Dusk`: `TickPenumbral` drains, `DrawDuskPools` darkens over everything, `DrawDuskLobFx`); step 3: Sickle (`SickleThrow`: a `CrescentShot` on the boss's loop, smaller), Nova (`NovaStep`: lights a fuse, `NovaLit` takes more damage in HurtEnemy, then bursts); step 4: the Last Light set piece (`SquadForm.LastLight`: `LastLightSpawn`, `LastLightMove`, `LastLightPoint`, preview `LASTLIGHT=<s>`); test suite `penumbral`; previews `PENUMBRAL=0.4 Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud 1`, `PENUMBRAL2=0.4 [GNOMON=1.6]`, `PENUMBRAL3=0.4 [NOVAT=2]`
- `Infall.cs`, `Game/InfallLook.cs`: the Singularity's faction, the Infall (Docs/SingularityFaction.md): the Accretion ring set piece (`SquadForm.Accretion`: `AccretionSpawn`, `AccretionMove`, `AccretionSlot`; its band: `AccretionBands`, `SyncAccretion` in the snapshot, `DrawAccretionBands`; preview `ACCRETION=<s>`), bot dangers (`InfallBotDanger`, `AccretionBotDanger`), Lens (`LensFaces` blocks hits on its face, `BendRoundLenses` in MoveBullets turns players' shots away, `LensFace` turns slowly), White Dwarf (darts, flashes, `WhitePulse`: `KnockPlayer` and players' shots turned into its own), Graviton (lobs a Small Well, `EnemyType.SmallWell`: `GravitonLob`; it pulls and bends shots like the boss's corner wells: `Pulls`/`DriftOf` in `WellDriftAt`, `PullIntoWells`), Orbiter (slings its orbs; `Phase` counts throws), Collapsar (dies into an `Implosion`: `CollapsarDied`, a pull then a ring), Debris (melee, an inward spiral: `InfallMove`), Void Acolyte (three orbs, the outer two curving to meet on you: `AcolyteVolley`), Pulsar (plants, flickers, sweeps two opposite beams a quarter turn, rests: `InfallSpecial`, `PulsarBeamDistance`, `PulsarLocked`; at most `MaxPulsars` a wave, `CapBeams` in Variety.cs); the look draws the beams under the enemies (`DrawInfallFloor`, from `DrawVarietyFloor`); test suite `infall`; previews `INFALL=0.4 ZOOM=1.7 Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud 1`, `INFALL2=0.4` (step 2), `INFALL3=0.4` (step 3)
- `Squall.cs`, `Game/SquallLook.cs`: the Storm Herald's faction, the Squall (Docs/StormFaction.md, step 1): Dust Devil (weaves in, `DevilTouched` shoves you with `KnockPlayer` and veers off, `Enemy.Phase` 1), Squall Gunner (`GunnerVolley`: three shots upwind that curve back to where you stood; its side is `Enemy.Strafe`, never flipped), Stormcaller (a `Hazard` of kind `HazardCaller`, landing in `CallerStrike` from `TickHazards`, drawn amber in `DrawHazard`); step 2: Grounder (`GrounderFace`, `GrounderFaces` in `BlocksShot`, `GroundShot` from the bullet loop charges `Enemy.Phase`, `Thunderclap`), Pylon pair (`PairPylon`, `PylonPartner`, `TickPylon`, `PylonLineDistance`; at most `MaxPylonPairs`), Lineman (`Enemy.LinkId` = the marked player, `Conduct`); the floor layer `DrawSquallFloor` (the pair's line, the mark ring); step 3: Thunderhead (`TickThunderhead`, a row of `HazardDownburst` strikes), the Herald's Ball Lightning made a wave enemy (in `TickHerald`: slows/freezes, `HeraldBall` decides boss shots; `BallWaveXp` in `KillXp`; `MaxBalls`); step 4: the Storm front set piece (`SquadForm.StormFront`, `StartStormFront` from `SpawnSquadAt`, `TickStormFronts`: columns of `HazardCaller` strikes with a drifting gap, `InStormFront`) and the ten lessons in `Approaches.cs`; test suite `squall`; previews `SQUALL=0.4` / `SQUALL2=0.4` / `SQUALL3=0.4 ZOOM=1.7` / `SQUALL4=0.4 Tools/Preview/shot.sh x hud 1`
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/ProtocolGuard.cs`, `Tools/SimTest/Golden/protocol.txt`: suite `protocol`, the protocol guard: every Core enum's values and a hash of every wire read/write line, kept for the current `Session.ProtocolVersion`; fails when they change without a bump, when an old enum value gets a new number, or when the version has no `Docs/ProtocolHistory.md` line (`PROTOCOL=update run.sh 1 1 protocol` rewrites it)
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/EveryEnemy.cs`: suite `everyenemy`: every enemy type in `Defs.Enemies` (bosses too) spawns, shows on the friend's screen, takes a hit both see, dies and is gone, so a new enemy is checked the day it's added
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/ListEntries.cs`: suite `lists`: every run modifier, path, Boss Hunt and challenge room is complete (in its list, named, described) and plays over the network
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/ChartCheck.cs`: report `chartcheck` (`run.sh 1 1 chartcheck`): the Campaign's shown waves 1-20 from the real wave builder next to the chart in `Docs/Approaches.md` (new enemy, squads, Warden leftover %)
- `Tools/SimTest/Tests/WaveDump.cs`, `Tools/SimTest/Golden/wavedump.txt`: suite `wavedump` (the wave refactor's safety net, `Docs/WaveRefactor.md` step 0): builds every wave of every mode on fixed seeds and compares with the golden file; `WAVEDUMP=update Tools/SimTest/run.sh 1 1 wavedump` rewrites it when a wave is meant to change
- `Tools/BalanceGrid/grid.sh`, `Tools/BalanceGrid/heatmap.py`, `Tools/SimTest/Tests/BalanceGrid.cs`: the balance grid (report `grid`: every role, class and weapon vs each listed boss in the Boss Lab, `LabFight` in `BossLab.cs`; run in parallel, drawn as a heatmap with outliers)
- `Docs/History.md` (finished to-do entries, audit notes, changelog), `Docs/CodeNotes.md` (older detailed per-file notes, not maintained), `Docs/Mockups/`

## Where to look for...
| Task | Files |
|---|---|
| Add or change a weapon | `Defs.cs` (weapon def) or the class's file (`Knight.cs`, `Ninja.cs`, `KitWeapons.cs`...), `Weapons.cs`, `Combat.cs`, look in `ShotLooks.cs`/`WorldShots.cs` |
| Make an attack hit things (area, lane, cone) | `FoeQuery.cs` (`FoesInCircle`, `FoesInBox`, `FoesInCone`, `AllFoes`), `Combat.cs` (`HurtEnemy`), Docs/Targeting.md |
| Add or change an ability | the class's file; registry in `Upgrades.cs` (`AbilityDef`); `Abilities.cs` for Soldier; feedback in `AbilityLooks.cs` |
| Add a new class | `HANDOFF.md` "Adding a class", `Defs.cs` (`Kit` enum, at the end), `Classes.cs`, `ClassLooks.cs`, `Docs/ClassTemplate` |
| Tune damage, health, wave numbers | `Balance.cs` (index of where every system's numbers live), `Difficulty.cs`, `Docs/UpgradeBalance.md` |
| Add or change an enemy | `Defs.cs` (`EnemyType`, def), `EnemyAI.cs` or `Variety.cs`, look in `WorldEnemies.cs`/`VarietyLook.cs` |
| Add an enemy variant (spawns in place of a regular one) | `EnemyVariants.cs` (`EnemyVariant`, `VariantTable`), the look reads `e.Variant` |
| Edit a boss fight | the boss's Core file plus its `*Look.cs`; bars in `HudBossBars.cs`; snapshot sync in the boss file |
| Change what a weapon mod does for a kind of weapon or one weapon | `WeaponMods.cs` (`ByKind` rows; a weapon's `Mods` entry) |
| Upgrade cards and picker | `Upgrades.cs` (cards; class cards in each class file, `Game.<Class>Cards`), `Game.cs` (`HostPick`, `NextOffer`), `HudPicker.cs`, `CardArt.cs`, `PickIntro.cs`; class/weapon pools planned in Docs/UpgradePools.md |
| Milestone upgrades (every 10 levels, the Milestone Compass) | `Upgrades.cs` (`Milestones()`, `MilestoneOffer`), `Game.cs` (`GrantXpTo`, `NextOffer`, `HostPick`), `Tools/SimTest/Tests/Milestones.cs` |
| Damage numbers, hit effects, shake | `Juice.cs` (`HitFlashMix`, `ImpactCrowd`), `WorldEffects.cs`, `Combat.cs` (`HurtEnemy`, `Damage`, `SplashFxNearby`); `Enemy.FlashHeat`; test `hitfx` |
| Late-game pressure (fire rate, budget, melee share, enemy jobs) | `WaveRecipe.cs`, `NonShooters.cs`, `Docs/EnemyRoles.md`, `Docs/LateGamePressure.md`, report `wavemix`, `EnemyAI.cs` (`WaveFireRate`, `ShotBudget`, `MeleeShare`), `Readability.cs`, `Variety.cs` |
| Camera lean (view shift, mouse/label offsets) | `CameraLean.cs`, `Px` in `WorldRenderer.cs`, `MouseArena` in `ArenaApp.cs` |
| Scroll-wheel zoom | `CameraZoom.cs` (same hooks as the lean; screen overlays skip it in `DrawWorld`) |
| First-person view | `FirstPerson.cs` (hooks: `Px`, `OffScreen`, `DrawWorld`, the body loops of `DrawArenaContents` in `WorldRenderer.cs`; `Vtx`/`SwitchToGlow` in `Juice.cs`; `Move`/`MouseArena`/`Update` in `ArenaApp.cs`; `ToUi` in `CameraLean.cs`) |
| HUD layout | `HudLayout.cs` (where everything goes), `Hud.cs`, `HudTiles.cs`, `HealthBars.cs` |
| Menus, lobby, settings | `Menus.cs`, `MenuDrawer.cs` (pause / lobby rows: `PauseMenuRows`, `LobbyMenuRows`), `LobbyUi.cs`, `Ui.cs`; new tool window: `UiScreens.cs`, `UiLayout.cs`; move lobby boxes in `LobbyLayout.cs` |
| Multiplayer, new network message | `Session.cs` (`Msg`, `ProtocolVersion`), `GameNet.cs`, `NetSync.cs`; bump the protocol version |
| Save/load, records, unlocks | `RunSaves.cs`, `SaveData.cs`, `Profile.cs`, `SavesUi.cs` |
| Recent runs graphs | `RunTimeline.cs` (data, sampler, files), `RunGraphs.cs` (page), Tests `RecentRuns.cs` |
| Profiles, "Who's playing?", main menu machine | `ProfileStore.cs`, `ProfilesUi.cs`, `DiskCarousel.cs`, `ProfileTransition.cs`, `DriveSlot.cs`, `DiskEject.cs`, `PcMonitor.cs`, `MenuMachine.cs`, `MachineLook.cs` (`Docs/Profiles.md`) |
| The in-game wiki (entries, references, search screen, Wiki key, eyedropper) | `Wiki.cs`, `BayWiki.cs`, `WikiPick.cs`, `Tools/SimTest/Tests/Wiki.cs` (suite `wiki`), `Docs/Wiki.md` |
| Account rank, XP, cosmetics | `Rank.cs`, `Profile.cs`, `RankLook.cs`, `RankUi.cs`, `RankTrails.cs` (`Docs/Rank.md`); class expertise and weapon skins: `Expertise.cs`, `WeaponSkinUi.cs` |
| Shop and gold | `Shop.cs`, `ShopUi.cs` |
| Black Market (buildables, stash, trap placing) | `Market.cs`, `ShopUi.cs` (`DrawBuildCard`), `ExpansionUi.cs` (Teleporter and Trap tools) |
| Power stacking (diminishing returns, wounds) | `Upgrades.cs` (`Compute`, `CopyStrength`), `Combat.cs` (`Player.Wound` in `Damage`, `HealCap` in every heal), `RunStats.cs` (`Heal`), `HealthBars.cs` (`DrawWound`), `HudPicker.cs` (`UpgradeNote`), `Tools/SimTest/Tests/Stacking.cs` (suite `stacking`); Docs/PowerStacking.md |
| The boss between waves (BossSlot, BossNow/BossNext, negative start wave) | `BossSchedule.cs`, `Hunts.cs`, `Waves.cs` (`BossStartsNext`), `GameNet.cs` (snapshot), `SaveData.cs`/`RunSaves.cs` (`slot`), `DevTools.cs` (`DevJumpToWave(n, true)`), `Tools/SimTest/Tests/BossSlot.cs` (suite `bossslot`) |
| Expanding vs Classic balance (bots) | `Tools/SimTest/Tests/ArenaBalance.cs` (report `arenabal`) |
| Expanding arena economy (bots) | `Tools/SimTest/Tests/VectorBalance.cs` (report `vecbal`: vectors, proposals, rooms built per round) |
| Arena layouts (walls, edge gates) | `Layouts.cs` (floor plans), `LayoutRules.cs` (pick per cycle, `PushOutOfSolids`, `SolidStops`, spawn through gates, `LayoutDir` steering), `LayoutLook.cs`, `Tools/SimTest/Tests/Layouts.cs` |
| Paths, challenge rooms, props | `Paths.cs`/`PathsUi.cs`, `Challenges.cs`/`ChallengeLook.cs`, `Props.cs`/`PropsLook.cs` |
| Sounds and music | `SoundBank.cs`, `GameMusic.cs`, `MenuMusic.cs`, `MusicLibrary.cs` (add a track here), `MusicPlayerUi.cs`, `BossSounds.cs`; hear them with `Tools/SoundCheck/render.sh` |
| Pixel icons | `PixelArt.cs` |
| Bots, Boss Lab, lobby bots | `BotBrain.cs`, `BossLab.cs`, `PartyBots.cs` |
| Pick between looks (variants) or see motion (GIF) | `Tools/Preview/variants.sh`, `Tools/Preview/clip.sh` (`HANDOFF.md` §3) |
| Which class or weapon is too strong or weak vs a boss | `Tools/BalanceGrid/grid.sh [waves] [runs] [roles]` (heatmap in `Tools/BalanceGrid/out/grid.png`) |
| See every UI screen, or what a UI change changed | `Tools/Preview/uishots.sh [-r git-ref]` (list in `Tools/Preview/ui-screens.txt`; `HANDOFF.md` §3) |
| A change doesn't compile or a test fails | `Tools/CompileCheck/check.sh`, `Tools/SimTest/run.sh`; Unity stubs in `Tools/Preview/Stubs.cs` (one copy for every tool) |
- `Tools/MapSketch/`: standalone iPad + Apple Pencil map sketcher (`index.html`, PWA manifest, README; Slow/Turret/Spikes trap markers): neon wall/hatch look of `LayoutLook.cs`, shapes as vectors, grid piece presets (Piece tool, the 600 px lattice of `GridPieces.cs`), PNG/JSON export; not part of the game build
